*Zijian Gu,Weikai Lin,Shuang Zhou,Zihan Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniMod框架，通过要求每个模态独立预测诊断来缓解多模态学习中的捷径学习问题，在哈佛青光眼和CheXpert数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多模态训练存在捷径学习问题：模型倾向利用更容易学习的模态（如文本中的诊断线索）而忽略更难学习的特征（如图像中的细微视觉模式），导致图像模态未能充分提取诊断性特征。

Method: UniMod框架同时监督图像单模态、文本单模态和多模态分类任务，使每个模态都必须独立提取诊断特征。此外加入跨模态对齐用于知识迁移，以及同诊断患者间的模态内监督对比对齐。

Result: 在Harvard-Glaucoma上达到0.850 AUC，比OGM-GE和Gradient Blending高出1.6-1.8%；在CheXpert Plus上达到0.966 AUC，超过基线5%以上。扩展至5类多标签诊断时无需架构改动，平均AUC比CGGM提升0.097。

Conclusion: UniMod通过强制每个模态独立具备诊断能力来有效缓解捷径学习，显著提升多模态医学诊断性能，并具有扩展到多标签诊断任务的灵活性。

Abstract: Multi-modal learning combining medical images and clinical text is promising for disease diagnosis. However, standard multi-modal training leads to shortcut learning: models exploit the easier modality (e.g., diagnostic cues in text) while neglecting harder-to-learn features (e.g., subtle visual patterns). We propose UniMod, a framework that mitigates shortcut learning by requiring each modality to predict the diagnosis on its own. It supervises image-only, text-only, and multi-modal classification simultaneously, so each modality must extract diagnostic features. We add cross-modality alignment for knowledge transfer and within-modality supervised contrastive alignment over same-diagnosis patients. On Harvard-Glaucoma, UniMod reaches 0.850 AUC, outperforming OGM-GE and Gradient Blending by 1.6-1.8%; on CheXpert Plus, it reaches 0.966 AUC, surpassing them by over 5%. UniMod also extends to 5-class multi-label diagnosis without architectural change, improving mean AUC by 0.097 over CGGM.

</details>


### [17] [From Detection to Understanding: TAR and TAR-Bench for Multi-Task Traffic Anomaly Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10317)
*Han Zhang,Yilin Zhao,Zaid Pervaiz Bhat,Zheng Tang,Varun Praveen,Vidya N. Murali,David C. Anastasiu,Tomasz Kornuta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了TAR流量异常推理数据集和TAR-Bench评估基准，包含44,040条链式思考训练标注和960条人工精选测试标注，用于训练和评估视频-语言模型在流量异常推理方面的能力。通过MAVEN工具生成结构化事件描述和推理轨迹，实验表明多任务微调可带来一致增益，该数据集是AI City Challenge 2026 Track 3的官方数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-语言模型在异常检测方面研究较多，但缺乏对流量异常场景中高阶推理能力（如时序推理、场景推理）的系统性训练和评估资源。作者旨在超越简单的异常检测，推动模型具备复杂推理能力。

Method: 构建TAR数据集：MAVEN工具将多尺度视频证据整合为结构化事件描述，再生成问答对和推理轨迹；包含10个任务、44,040条训练标注、3,670个CCTV视频（约26小时）来自8个公开数据集。TAR-Bench评估组件：80个待评估片段（来自17个公开YouTube视频）配有960条人工精选测试标注。

Result: 对11个视觉-语言模型在TAR-Bench上评估发现，强问答准确率不能可靠预测时序或场景推理能力。多任务微调带来一致增益，完整10任务模型相比零样本基线聚合评分提升21.4分。

Conclusion: TAR和TAR-Bench为流量异常推理提供了官方训练数据和领域内评估基准，已被选为AI City Challenge 2026 Track 3的官方数据集，数据集已在Hugging Face公开。

Abstract: We present TAR (Traffic Anomaly Reasoning) and TAR-Bench datasets, resources for training and evaluating video-language models beyond anomaly detection. TAR contains 44,040 chain-of-thought training annotations across 10 tasks for 3,670 CCTV videos ($\sim$26 hours) from eight public datasets. Its evaluation component, TAR-Bench, contains 960 human-curated test annotations for 80 held-out clips trimmed from 17 public YouTube videos. TAR's training annotations are produced with MAVEN, which consolidates multi-scale video evidence into structured event descriptions before generating question-answer pairs and reasoning traces. On TAR-Bench, eleven vision-language models reveal that strong question-answering accuracy does not reliably predict temporal or scene reasoning ability. Multi-task fine-tuning on TAR yields consistent gains, with the full 10-task model improving aggregate score by 21.4 points over its zero-shot baseline. TAR and TAR-Bench provide the official training and in-domain evaluation data for AI City Challenge 2026 Track 3. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/nvidia/PhysicalAI-Traffic-Anomaly-Reasoning

</details>


### [18] [ENCORE: Efficient Noise Context-Aware Representation for Low-Dose CT Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10343)
*Minwoo Yu,N. Robert Bennett,Jongduk Baek,Adam S. Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ENCORE框架，利用CT噪声的非平稳性和空间相关性特征，通过噪声分布重建、噪声上下文提取和FlyingConv模块实现高效低剂量CT去噪，同时支持零样本条件去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习CT去噪方法使用为自然图像设计的通用架构，未能考虑CT噪声的非平稳性和空间相关性特征，导致去噪效果受限。

Method: 1) 基于更真实的噪声分布重制定噪声合成过程，超越传统高斯近似；2) 提取局部噪声功率和相关性上下文指导去噪；3) 提出FlyingConv模块，根据每个局部图像区域自适应调整卷积权重。

Result: 在去噪质量和计算效率上均取得显著提升，并可通过在推理时操纵噪声上下文图的强度实现零样本条件去噪，动态控制输出图像纹理。

Conclusion: 本文展示了显式利用CT噪声特征和上下文信息能够显著提升去噪性能与效率，且提供代码开源。

Abstract: While deep learning-based denoising has become widely adopted in low-dose CT, conventional models use generic architectures designed for natural images, failing to account for non-stationary and spatially correlated CT noise characteristics. To address this, we propose an Efficient Noise COntext-aware REpresentation (ENCORE) framework that explicitly leverages CT noise characteristics and anatomical features. First, we reformulate the noise synthesis procedure based on a realistic noise distribution beyond the conventional Gaussian approximation, establishing a rigorous foundation for training pair generation. Next, we extract local noise power and correlation contexts to guide the denoising process. To fully leverage the potential of noise context, we propose a FlyingConv module, which adaptively changes convolution weights for each local image region. Notably, our approach demonstrates substantial gains in both denoising quality and computational efficiency. Furthermore, manipulating the intensity of the noise context maps at inference time enables zero-shot conditional denoising, allowing for dynamic control over the output image texture. The entire pipeline is available at https://github.com/minwoo-yu/ENCORE.git

</details>


### [19] [CasDeblurGS: Cascaded 2D-to-3D Multi-View Consistency for 3D Gaussian Splatting from Two Blurry Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10345)
*Haeyun Choi,Minhyuk Jang,I-Gil Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CasDeblurGS，一个级联框架，仅从两张带运动模糊的图像（已知内参、无位姿、无辅助清晰图、无需逐场景优化）重建连贯3D场景。通过两阶段分别提取局部2D对应关系并聚合为无位姿3D高斯表示，提供全局3D引导以完成最终去模糊和新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊感知的神经渲染方法通常需要大量多视角冗余、精确相机位姿或昂贵的逐场景优化，无法适应仅有少量运动模糊图像的实用场景。本文旨在解决一个严苛但实际的问题：仅凭两张运动模糊图像（不依赖位姿、辅助清晰图像或测试时优化）重建连贯的3D场景。

Method: 提出CasDeblurGS级联框架，分两阶段进行：(1) 第一阶段通过遮挡感知的对应关系过滤构建局部可靠的2D指引；(2) 第二阶段将中间恢复结果聚合为无位姿的3D高斯表示，其输入视角重渲染提供稠密的全局3D指引，用于最终恢复。最终视角可产生更连贯的3D表示和更高质量的新视角合成。

Result: 在真实世界和合成的Deblur-NeRF场景上，相比强基线分别提升PSNR 1.19 dB和2.11 dB。通过渐进式消融、跨视角对应关系可视化和相机重投影分析，验证了渲染质量和多视角几何一致性的提升。

Conclusion: CasDeblurGS通过从局部2D对应到全局3D引导的渐进式恢复策略，在极稀疏（仅两帧）且运动模糊的条件下实现了高质量的3D场景重建与新视角合成，同时避免了对手动位姿和逐场景优化的依赖。

Abstract: Free-viewpoint 3D scene media is increasingly important for immersive applications, yet practical capture often suffers from severe view sparsity and motion blur. Although neural rendering has advanced sparse-view synthesis, existing blur-aware methods typically require substantial multi-view redundancy, accurate camera poses, or costly per-scene optimization. We address a stringent yet practical setting: reconstructing a coherent 3D scene from only two motion-blurred images with known intrinsics, without input-view poses, auxiliary sharp images, or per-scene test-time optimization. To this end, we propose CasDeblurGS, a cascaded framework that progressively recovers reliable cross-view information from local 2D correspondences to global 3D guidance. Stage 1 constructs locally reliable guidance through occlusion-aware correspondence filtering, while Stage 2 aggregates the intermediate restorations into a provisional pose-free 3D Gaussian representation whose input-view re-renders provide dense global guidance for final restoration. The resulting views enable a more coherent 3D representation and higher-quality novel-view synthesis. Experiments on real-world and synthetic Deblur-NeRF scenes show consistent gains over strong baselines, improving PSNR by 1.19 dB and 2.11 dB, respectively. Progressive ablations, cross-view correspondence visualization, and camera reprojection analysis further demonstrate improvements in both rendering quality and multi-view geometric consistency.

</details>


### [20] [Towards Unified Dynamic Face Landmark Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10346)
*Sebastian Regalado,Varshanth R. Rao,Ruowei Jiang,Parham Aarabi,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Unified Dynamic FLD方法，通过Face Part-Anchored Landmark Positions (FPALPs)概念将不同N点标注数据集统一为单一数据集，并利用基于FPALP的查询与跨模态解码器实现动态任意数量地标预测，一个模型即可在多个数据集上训练并可灵活输出任意指定地标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部地标检测方法存在两大功能局限：(1) 每个N点基准数据集需要独立训练不同的网络参数；(2) 在N点数据集上训练的模型只能可靠输出这N个特定地标，无法泛化到其他数量或位置的地标。

Method: 首先提出FPALPs概念，将每个地标表示为面部部件轮廓上从0（起点）到1（终点）的进度值，使任意来源数据集的地标都可以统一表达。其次，用基于FPALP的查询表示每个地标，通过跨模态解码器逐步细化该查询，最终根据表示预测地标坐标。整体框架称为Unified Dynamic FLD。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在获得统一的单模型多数据集训练能力和灵活任意数量地标预测能力的同时，与现有最先进方法相比保持竞争力，在多个场景下甚至表现更优。

Conclusion: Unified Dynamic FLD通过FPALP统一表达与查询驱动的动态解码，解决了传统FLD模型中跨数据集训练受限和输出地标数量固定的问题，实现了更灵活、更统一的landmark检测流程。

Abstract: Although advancements in face landmark detection (FLD) methods continue to push performance boundaries, they overlook two major functional limitations: (1) different network parameters need to be trained independently for each ``$N$-point'' benchmark dataset, and (2) a model trained on an ``$N$-point'' dataset reliably outputs only the $N$ landmarks. In our work, we first conceptualize Face Part-Anchored Landmark Positions (FPALPs), wherein each landmark is treated as a progression value between zero (start) and one (end) along a face part's contour. Every landmark can be expressed in the FPALP format, irrespective of its source dataset, hence unlocking the ability to unify all ``$N$-point'' datasets into a single dataset. Secondly, we represent each landmark with an FPALP-based query, refine it progressively with a cross-modality decoder, and predict its coordinates based on the final representation. Our approach, called Unified Dynamic FLD, embodies these two design choices and streamlines the landmark detection pipeline by enabling (1) a single model to learn on any number of ``$N$-point'' datasets, and (2) yield any number of specific landmark predictions by loading the designated landmark queries at runtime. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets show that our method delivers these benefits while remaining competitive with, and in several cases outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [21] [FormStruct-Bench:A Hierarchical and Diagnostic Benchmark for Table-Form Document Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10396)
*Lujie Ban,Jiangtao Zhu,Yuanheng Yu,Jiasheng Shi,Chenhao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FormStruct-Bench，一个分层、可诊断的表格型文档结构识别基准，通过70个可复用模板扩展到7,000个验证实例（其中1,100个测试实例经人工审核），在页面、模式、组件三个粒度评估14个系统及两个SFT变体，发现文档级最佳得分83.85%而细粒度结构得分低于18%，揭示内容读取与层级结构恢复之间存在显著鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么评估整体文档输出，要么评估传统表格网格，聚合分数无法揭示结构失败发生在何处。为将表格式文档转化为机器可处理记录，不仅需要恢复可见内容，还需恢复组织内容的多级结构，因此需要一种能追溯特定结构失败模式的诊断性基准。

Method: 构建一个分层的、诊断性的基准FormStruct-Bench：标注70个可复用模板，通过保留来源的Director–Artist–Verifier流水线扩展为7,000个已验证实例，其中模板不相交测试集中的1,100个实例额外经过人工审核。评估协议使用五个主要指标和三个结构特定诊断指标，涵盖页面、模式和组件层级，并对难度、结构约束和视觉退化进行切片分析。

Result: 在14个API托管和本地可部署系统以及两个SFT变体上，最佳文档级分数达83.85%，而最佳细粒度结构分数仍低于18%，表明内容读取与层级/区域组织恢复之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 现有系统在读取表格文档内容上表现良好，但在恢复可靠表格理解所需的层级结构和区域组织方面能力不足，FormStruct-Bench通过分层评估机制使聚合性能能够追溯到具体结构失败模式，为后续研究指明改进方向。

Abstract: Transforming table-form documents into machine-processable records requires recovering not only their visible content but also the multilevel structure that organizes it. However, existing benchmarks evaluate either holistic document outputs or conventional table grids, and their aggregate scores provide little insight into where structural failures occur. We introduce FormStruct-Bench, a hierarchical and diagnostic benchmark that evaluates table-form document structure recognition at both the document level and progressively finer component levels, allowing aggregate performance to be traced back to specific structural failure modes. To construct auditable ground truth at scale, we annotate 70 reusable templates and expand them into 7,000 verified instances through a provenance-preserving Director--Artist--Verifier pipeline; all 1,100 instances in the template-disjoint test set additionally receive human review. Our evaluation protocol uses five primary metrics and three structure-specific diagnostics across page, schema, and component levels, together with slices over difficulty, structural constraints, and visual degradation. Across 14 API-hosted and locally deployable systems plus two SFT variants, the best document-level score reaches 83.85%, whereas the best reported fine-grained structural score remains below 18%. These results reveal a pronounced gap between reading document content and recovering the hierarchy and regional organization required for reliable table-form understanding.

</details>


### [22] [A second-order theory of texture for depth from focus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10411)
*Sreekar Ranganathan,Ioannis Gkioulekas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于波动光学的粗糙表面纹理外观理论，揭示传统计算机视觉认为"无纹理"的表面实际上由于微观几何产生的散斑而具有纹理外观，并证明通过窄带光谱滤波可增强这种二阶纹理的对比度，从而显著改善被动对焦深度重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动深度对焦通常依赖于场景表面的纹理特征，但传统计算机视觉认为许多表面（如光滑、均匀颜色）是"无纹理"的，导致深度重建在这些场景下失效。作者希望通过波动光学的视角重新审视表面的纹理来源，挖掘微观几何带来的潜在纹理信息，以突破这一限制。

Method: 作者从波动光学出发建立粗糙表面纹理外观的理论模型，识别出由表面微观几何产生的"主观散斑"作为二阶纹理的来源。理论分析了该纹理的统计特性和对比度性质，并提出在自然环境光下使用窄带光谱滤波器来增强散斑对比度的策略，同时结合仿真和实际实验进行验证。

Result: 理论和实验证明，即使看似无纹理的表面也能产生可测量的纹理外观；窄带光谱滤波能显著增强这种散斑纹理的对比度，进而在被动对焦深度重建中获得大幅度的精度和鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 该研究重新定义了"纹理"的内涵，表明微观光学效应（散斑）可作为被动深度感知的可靠信号源，为看似无纹理场景的深度重建提供了新的理论依据和实用解决方案。

Abstract: We present a theory of textured appearance of optically rough surfaces based on wave optics, emphasizing the role of texture for passive depth from focus. Our theory shows that even surfaces that traditional computer vision would consider textureless can produce textured appearance, due to subjective speckle from surface microgeometry. We analyze the properties of this second-order texture, and show that we can enhance its contrast under natural ambient lighting by simply using a narrowband spectral filter. Doing so results in dramatic improvements in passive depth reconstruction of seemingly textureless scenes, as we demonstrate through extensive theory, simulations, and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [23] [DriveVLA-M0: Failure-Aware Memory Augmentation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10413)
*Zebin Xing,Yupeng Zheng,Qiang Chen,Linbo Wang,Yichen Zhang,Pengxuan Yang,Junli Wang,Deheng Qian,Xiaoqing Ye,Junyu Han,Yifeng Pan,Qichao Zhang,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DriveVLA-M0，一种具备失败感知潜在记忆的检索增强VLA模型，用于端到端自动驾驶，通过在推理时检索历史失败案例并注入LoRA进行测试时训练，实现针对性纠错，在NAVSIM基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA驾驶模型缺乏利用历史失败经验或适应分布偏移的机制，导致在曾失败过的相似场景中持续表现不佳。需要一种能记忆、检索并利用失败案例来指导当前决策的机制。

Method: DriveVLA-M0构建了一个存储失败案例及其结构场景表征和专家轨迹标签的潜在记忆池；设计专门的检索模型，解耦静态道路结构和动态智能体交互以实现结构接地检索；推理时通过轻量级的解耦LoRA测试时训练（TTT）机制将检索到的案例注入模型，无需修改骨干网络即可实现针对性纠错。

Result: 在NAVSIMv1和NAVSIMv2基准上超越此前方法，Navtest上达到94.1 PDMS，Navhard上达到47.0 EPDMS，仅引入26.44 ms的TTT反向传播延迟开销；模型可随记忆规模扩展，增加记忆即可获得无需训练的额外性能提升。

Conclusion: 失败感知的记忆检索与轻量级测试时训练相结合，是提升VLA模型在复杂驾驶场景下鲁棒性及适应性的有效途径，且具备良好的可扩展性和低延迟特性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving by enabling unified reasoning across perception, language, and planning. However, existing approaches lack mechanisms to exploit past failures or adapt to distribution shifts, causing the model to persistently underperform on similar scenarios where it has previously failed. In this paper, we propose DriveVLA-M0, a retrieval-augmented VLA with failure-aware latent memory. We construct a latent memory pool that stores failure cases along with their structure scene representations and expert trajectory labels, and design a dedicated Retrieve Model that decouples static road structure and dynamic agent interactions to enable structurally grounded retrieval. At inference time, retrieved cases are injected into the model via a lightweight decoupled LoRA-based test-time training (TTT) mechanism, allowing targeted and scenario-specific correction without modifying the backbone. Extensive experiments on NAVSIMv1 and NAVSIMv2 benchmark demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms prior methods, achieving 94.1 PDMS on Navtest and 47.0 EPDMS on Navhard with only 26.44 ms TTT backward latency overhead. Furthermore, we show that DriveVLA-M0 scales effectively with additional memory, enabling training-free performance gains through memory expansion. The code is available at https://github.com/ZebinX/DriveVLA-M0.

</details>


### [24] [GeoSeg-OV: Bridging Geospatial Gaps with Structural Guidance for Open-Vocabulary Remote Sensing Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10426)
*Ruizhong Liu,Tingzhang Luo,Zaiyan Zhang,Jundong Chen,Hongruixuan Chen,Shaoguang Huang,Hongyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoSeg-OV框架，将辅助视觉基础模型（VFM）的特征从视觉-文本匹配中解耦，重新用作结构引导以进行成本聚合和特征解码，解决了开放词汇遥感分割中地理域偏移导致的视觉-文本匹配弱化问题，在HRLC基准上超越SOTA +2.5/+2.7 mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇遥感分割面临地理域偏移（不同区域、空间分辨率、采集平台）导致的视觉-文本匹配弱化和跨数据集泛化受限问题。现有方法虽引入辅助视觉基础模型（VFM）特征与文本嵌入耦合作为额外匹配证据，但可能引入不一致的匹配信号，且未充分利用VFM结构敏感的表征。

Method: GeoSeg-OV将VFM特征从视觉-文本匹配中解耦，重新用作结构引导：(1) 从多旋转CLIP特征构建朝向鲁棒的成本体；(2) 冻结VFM并行提取多尺度结构敏感特征；(3) 提出结构引导聚合（SGA），整合成本token、CLIP语义引导与VFM成对结构偏置进行空间传播，随后进行文本条件类别推理；(4) 提出成本感知解码（CAD），基于当前解码器上下文自适应精炼和融合多尺度语义与结构引导。

Result: 在覆盖六大洲七个数据集的全球高分辨率土地覆盖（HRLC）基准上，GeoSeg-OV在两种训练设置下分别比SOTA高出+2.5和+2.7平均mIoU。大规模零样本案例研究进一步证明了其在无需目标域标注或重训练的情况下跨地理域和类别系统的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将辅助VFM特征从匹配解耦并重构为结构引导，是提升开放词汇遥感分割跨域泛化的有效范式，避免了不一致匹配信号的干扰，同时充分挖掘了VFM结构敏感表征的价值。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary remote sensing segmentation has recently emerged as a promising paradigm that enables pixel-level recognition of arbitrary categories specified by natural language, including classes unseen during training. However, geospatial domain shifts caused by heterogeneous regions, spatial resolutions, and acquisition platforms weaken visual-text matching and limit cross-dataset generalization. Recent attempts have begun to incorporate auxiliary vision foundation models (VFMs), typically coupling their features with text embeddings as additional matching evidence. However, this strategy may introduce inconsistent matching signals while leaving the structure-sensitive representations of VFMs insufficiently exploited. We therefore propose GeoSeg-OV, which decouples auxiliary VFM features from visual-text matching and repurposes them as structural guidance for cost aggregation and decoding. GeoSeg-OV constructs an orientation-robust cost volume from multi-rotation CLIP features, while a frozen VFM extracts multi-scale structure-sensitive features in parallel. We propose Structure-Guided Aggregation (SGA), which integrates cost tokens and CLIP semantic guidance with VFM-derived pairwise structural biases for coherent spatial propagation, followed by text-conditioned class-wise reasoning. We further introduce Cost-Aware Decoding (CAD) to adaptively refine and fuse multi-scale semantic and structural guidance based on the current decoder context. On the global High-Resolution Land Cover (HRLC) benchmark spanning seven datasets across six continents, GeoSeg-OV outperforms the state-of-the-art by +2.5 and +2.7 average mIoU under two training settings. A large-scale zero-shot case study further demonstrates its generalization across geographic domains and category systems without target-domain annotations or retraining.

</details>


### [25] [Lesion-Aware Adaptive Fourier Neural Operator for CT-to-PSMA PET Synthesis in Prostate Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10429)
*Rashmi Bhaskara,Waleed M. Almutairi,Matthew Gopaulchan,Maram Musaad Alqurashi,Francis Asamoah,Alex Ocana,Clinton D. Bahler,Oluwaseyi M. Oderinde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LAFNO（病变感知自适应傅里叶神经算子），用于CT到PSMA-PET的合成，通过两个CT衍生的代理通道（对比代理和紊乱代理）替代高维影像组学条件，结合全容积重建与病变级监督（总病变活性、肿瘤核心对比度、瘤周监督），在保持整体图像质量的同时显著降低TLA误差并提升肿瘤核心影像组学可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CT/MRI到PET合成模型通常使用全局损失（如L1或MSE），对所有体素同等对待。在全身体PSMA-PET中，肿瘤体素仅占很小体积但承载临床关键信号，导致模型虽能达到较高的SSIM/PSNR，却会低估病变活性或破坏肿瘤特定结构。虽然影像组学提供了有意义的肿瘤强度和纹理描述符，但直接以影像组学作为条件需要耗时的手动病灶分割和特征提取。

Method: LAFNO以傅里叶神经算子为基础架构，从CT图像提取两个高效的代理通道注入模型瓶颈层：（1）对比代理，表征局部密度变化；（2）紊乱代理，表征局部纹理异质性。这两个通道受PSMA-avid肿瘤核心和瘤周区域影像组学分析的启发。训练中结合全容积重建损失与病变级监督，包括总病变活性（TLA）、肿瘤核心对比度和瘤周监督。

Result: 在TCIA PSMA-PET-CT-Lesions数据集上对比四个基线架构：LAFNO在整体图像质量上保持竞争力（18F-PSMA的SSIM为0.960，68Ga-PSMA为0.938），同时将每位患者的TLA误差降至18F-PSMA的48.3%和68Ga-PSMA的64.0%，并在两种示踪剂的所有影像组学特征类别中取得最高的肿瘤核心可重复性。瘤周可重复性仍依赖示踪剂类型。

Conclusion: LAFNO通过轻量级CT代理通道实现病变感知的PET合成，在保持整体重建质量的同时显著改善病变区域活性和影像组学保真度。然而，瘤周区域的生物学保真度仍具挑战性且依赖示踪剂，表明合成PSMA-PET在肿瘤微环境相关特征的保留上仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Deep learning models that synthesize PET from CT or MRI can reduce patient dose and scanner demand, but are typically optimized with global losses such as L1 or mean squared error (MSE) that treat all voxels similarly. In whole-body PSMA-PET, tumor voxels occupy only a small fraction of the volume, yet carry the clinically relevant activity signal; as a result, models can achieve high structural similarity index measure (SSIM) and peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) while still underestimating lesion activity or failing to preserve tumor-specific structure. Radiomics provides biologically meaningful descriptors of tumor intensity and texture, but direct radiomics conditioning is time-consuming because it requires feature extraction from delineated lesion regions. We propose LAFNO, a Lesion-Aware Adaptive Fourier Neural Operator for CT-to-PSMA-PET synthesis that replaces high-dimensional radiomics conditioning with two efficient CT-derived proxy channels. Motivated by radiomics analysis of PSMA-avid tumor core and peritumoral regions, LAFNO uses a contrast proxy for local density variation and a disorder proxy for local texture heterogeneity, both injected into the model bottleneck. LAFNO combines whole-volume reconstruction with lesion-level total lesion activity (TLA), tumor-core contrast, and peritumoral supervision. We evaluated LAFNO against four baseline architectures on the TCIA PSMA-PET-CT-Lesions dataset. LAFNO remained competitive on whole-volume image quality, achieving SSIM of 0.960 and 0.938 for 18F- and 68Ga-PSMA, respectively, while reducing per-patient TLA error to 48.3% and 64.0% for 18F- and 68Ga-PSMA, respectively, and achieving the highest tumor-core radiomics reproducibility across all feature classes for both tracers. Peritumoral reproducibility remained tracer-dependent, indicating that biological fidelity in synthetic PSMA-PET remains challenging.

</details>


### [26] [DynaPPI: A Large-scale Dynamic Protein Dataset for AI-driven Advances in Protein Interactomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10435)
*Jiabao Wei,Zilong Geng,Yuze Wang,Jianjun Li,Ning Ding,Bowen Zhou,Bing Zhang,Zhiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了DynaPPI，一个包含蛋白质复合物形成过程的分子动力学轨迹数据集（从解离链到结合态），用于弥补现有数据集仅关注静态快照或单体轨迹的不足，使扩散模型能学习动态结合轨迹并准确预测未知多链蛋白质复合物的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态或动态蛋白质数据集仅包含静态快照或单实体轨迹，忽视了多个单体形成复合物的动态过程，导致AI驱动的生物学研究中，预测未知多链蛋白质复合物（蛋白质复合体）的结构仍是一个未解决的挑战。扩散模型虽在蛋白质骨架生成中表现强大，但因缺乏动态结合过程数据而受限于未知复合物的预测。

Method: 构建DynaPPI数据集，包含蛋白质复合物形成过程中从解离链到结合态的分子动力学（MD）模拟轨迹；在此基础上，扩散模型可以显式学习已知复合物的动态结合轨迹，并利用其生成能力预测未知复合物的结构。

Result: 扩散模型借助DynaPPI数据集能够显式学习已知复合物的动态结合轨迹，并准确预测未知复合物的结构，从而进一步推动AI驱动的结构生物学和蛋白质相互作用组学研究。

Conclusion: DynaPPI作为连接静态结构生物学与动态分子相互作用时间本质的关键资源，弥合了二者之间的鸿沟，为扩散模型预测未知蛋白质复合物结构提供了数据支撑，有望催化AI驱动的结构生物学和蛋白质互作组学的发展。

Abstract: Diffusion models have been widely explored in protein backbone generation due to their powerful generation capabilities.However, in today's AI-driven biological research, predicting the structure of unknown multi-chain protein aggregates (called "complexes" in biology) remains an unsolved challenge.This is because existing static or dynamic protein datasets focus solely on static snapshots or single-entity trajectories, neglecting the dynamic process of multiple monomers forming complexes.To alleviate this dilemma, we present DynaPPI, a dynamic protein dataset comprising molecular dynamics (MD) trajectories of protein complex formation from dissociated chains to the bound state, as a pivotal resource to bridge the gap between static structural biology and the inherently temporal nature of dynamic molecular interactions.Benefiting from this dataset, diffusion models can explicitly learn the dynamic binding trajectories of known complexes and accurately predict the structures of unknown complexes based on their diverse generative properties, thereby further catalyzing AI-driven structural biology and protein interactomics.

</details>


### [27] [MammoMix: Leveraging Mixture of Experts for Robust Mammogram Breast Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10437)
*Dinh Tan Nguyen,Hoang Quan Dang,Chen Zhang,Sai Ho Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MammoMix，一种基于混合专家（MoE）范式的乳腺病变检测框架，通过为每个特定领域训练专属专家模型，并利用门控机制动态加权融合专家输出，配合MoCAE校准模块调整置信度，显著提升了跨数据集（CSAW、DDSM、DMID）的检测精度与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺钼靶影像中的病变检测因图像质量、病灶外观和人群分布在不同数据集间存在差异而极具挑战。现有检测器（如YOLO、DETR）在单一数据集上表现优异，但在异构数据源上训练或应用时性能往往下降，亟需一种能够跨域泛化且稳健的检测框架。

Method: MammoMix基于混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts）范式：每个专家模型在特定领域数据上训练，专门学习该数据源的独特特征；一个门控机制根据输入图像自适应地为各专家分配权重，融合其输出实现领域自适应的推理；此外引入MoCAE校准模块，调整置信度分数以反映真实预测不确定性。

Result: 在CSAW、DDSM和DMID三个公开乳腺钼靶数据集上的实验结果表明，MammoMix在平均精度和可靠性方面均优于基线检测器，尤其在数据变异性更大的数据集上优势更为显著。

Conclusion: 专家专精化与校准后的集成融合能够显著增强模型的泛化能力和稳健性，MammoMix为实现跨真实临床领域的可靠AI辅助乳腺癌筛查提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Breast lesion detection in mammography remains a challenging task due to variations in image quality, lesion appearance, and population demographics across datasets. While current object detectors such as YOLO and DETR achieve strong results on individual datasets, their performance often degrades when trained on or applied across heterogeneous sources. To address this, we propose MammoMix, a novel framework based on Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) paradigm for robust and generalizable lesion detection. In MammoMix, each expert model is trained on a specific domain, allowing it to specialize in distinct characteristics of its source data. A gating mechanism adaptively weighs contributions from each expert based on input image, combining their outputs to enable domain-adaptive inference. To improve reliability, we further incorporate a calibration module, MoCAE, which adjusts confidence scores to reflect true predictive uncertainty. We evaluate MammoMix on 3 public mammography datasets: CSAW, DDSM, and DMID, covering diverse clinical settings. Results show that MammoMix outperforms baseline detectors in both average precision and reliability, particularly on datasets with greater variability. Our findings demonstrate that expert specialization and calibrated ensemble fusion significantly enhance model generalization and robustness. MammoMix offers a promising step toward dependable AI-assisted breast cancer screening across real-world clinical domains.

</details>


### [28] [Stream Forcing: Constructing Unified Training Trajectory for Robust Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10439)
*Yueting Zhu,Yuehao Song,Kaicheng Zhang,Bao Tang,Shaoyu Chen,Qian Zhang,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Stream Forcing，一个统一训练框架，通过将视频扩散采样重新表述为帧索引的噪声级随机过程，解决流式视频生成中训练-推理不一致与训练覆盖不足之间的权衡，并在UCF-101基准上实现36.6%（质量）和27.9%（零样本长时程外推）的FVD提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成的推理过程遵循专门的去噪顺序，而先进的训练策略需要多样的噪声级配置，二者之间存在根本性的训练-推理不匹配。现有方法难以同时兼顾训练-推理一致性和训练覆盖度，需要一种能够平衡两者的统一训练框架。

Method: 将视频扩散采样重新表述为帧索引的噪声级随机过程；在此随机过程空间中构建连续训练轨迹，使采样调度从独立采样逐步进化到推理一致的采样；引入联合校准算法和时序相关采样算法，分别保证轨迹平滑性和跨帧相关性。

Result: 在UCF-101基准上，Stream Forcing显著提升生成质量，FVD改进36.6%；同时支持零样本长时程视频生成外推，FVD改进27.9%。

Conclusion: Stream Forcing通过统一的训练框架有效平衡了训练充分性与推理效率，在流式视频生成质量和长时程外推能力上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Streaming video generation holds strong potential for world modeling, where future frames must be inferred online sequentially to form a continuous video stream. However, streaming video diffusion models introduce a fundamental train-inference mismatch: inference follows a specialized denoising order, whereas advanced training strategies typically require diverse noise-level configurations. To address this trade-off between train-inference consistency and training coverage, we reformulate the video diffusion sampling as a frame-indexed stochastic process over noise levels. Within this stochastic process space, we construct a continuous training trajectory along which the sampling schedule progressively evolves from independent sampling to inference-consistent sampling. We further introduce a joint calibration algorithm and a temporal correlative sampling algorithm to ensure trajectory smoothness and cross-frame correlation. Building on these designs, we propose Stream Forcing, a unified training framework for streaming video generation that balances training sufficiency and inference efficiency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Stream Forcing significantly improves generation quality with a 36.6% FVD improvement on the UCF-101 benchmark. Furthermore, our method facilitates robust zero-shot extrapolation to long-horizon video generation with a 27.9% FVD improvement on the UCF-101 benchmark.

</details>


### [29] [FUSE: Frame-Unified Stress Estimation from Facial Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10442)
*Stefanos Gkikas,Thomas Kassiotis,Yang Guo,Guangliang Li,Giorgos Giannakakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FUSE（Frame-Unified Stress Estimation）框架，将完整面部视频记录（最长120秒，3600帧）作为单一输入进行压力状态估计，无需时间窗口分割。通过将时间维度折叠到通道维度，并使用统一的不对称注意力架构处理高维输入。在58名受试者的压力数据集上，FUSE在t=15时达到最高准确率69.44%，全帧配置为69.03%，计算成本在12.48至348.78 GFLOPs之间波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的压力检测方法通常将完整录像分割成短时间窗口进行分类，这引入了窗口长度、重叠和聚合等额外设计选择，同时限制了跨整个录制的直接时间信息分析。作者旨在消除时间窗口化需求，实现完整录制的单次推理。

Method: FUSE将完整录制的所有帧融合为单一的二维表示，具体做法是将时间维度折叠到空间表示的通道维度，生成高维输入。随后使用统一的不对称注意力架构处理该输入。实验评估了从全帧输入到稀疏子采样的七种时间步长配置（t=1至t=15），采用分层受试者级别的协议。

Result: 在58名受试者的压力数据集上，FUSE在t=15时达到最高测试准确率69.44%，全帧配置（t=1）达到69.03%，两者具有竞争力。计算成本随时间步长变化从12.48 GFLOPs到348.78 GFLOPs，展示了时间密度与效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在该设置下，面部视频压力检测并不需要时间窗口分割，完整录制的推理可以在单一统一架构中实现，FUSE的时间折叠设计是有效的。

Abstract: Automatic stress detection from facial video offers a practical path to non-intrusive affect monitoring, yet existing video-based approaches commonly decompose full recordings into short temporal windows before classification. This design introduces additional choices regarding window length, overlap, and aggregation, while limiting direct analysis of temporal information across the entire recording. In this study, we present FUSE (Frame-Unified Stress Estimation), a facial-video stress detection framework that processes complete recordings as a single input without temporal windowing or external segmentation. The name reflects the defining operation of the method: rather than dividing a recording into short clips, all frames are fused into one unified two-dimensional representation from which the stress state is estimated. This unification is realized by folding the temporal dimension into the channel dimension of the spatial representation, and the resulting high-dimensional input is processed using a unified asymmetric-attention architecture. At a temporal stride of t = 1, FUSE retains the full 120-second recording as one input, corresponding to 3,600 frames at 30 fps. Experiments on a 58-subject stress dataset using a stratified subject-level protocol evaluate seven temporal-stride configurations, ranging from full-frame input to sparse subsampling. FUSE achieves the highest test accuracy of 69.44% at t = 15, while the full-frame configuration remains competitive at 69.03%. Across the stride range, computational cost varies from 12.48 to 348.78 GFLOPs, showing the trade-off between temporal density and efficiency. These results demonstrate that temporal windowing is not required for effective facial-video stress detection in this setting, and that complete-recording inference can be achieved within a single unified architecture.

</details>


### [30] [Bridging Event Streams and DiT: Event-Guided Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10479)
*Guixu Lin,Yuyang Yu,Xiang Ji,Linyao Chen,Zhengwei Yin,Mengshun Hu,Mingdeng Cao,Shengfeng He,Yinqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个基于适配器的框架（BridgeEventDiT-VFI），通过将事件相机的高时间分辨率运动线索注入预训练的图到视频扩散模型中，在不进行大规模重新训练的情况下显著提升视频帧插值质量，解决大时间间隔和复杂运动下的模糊、结构失真及时间不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频帧插值在应对大时间间隔和复杂运动时，现有的潜在扩散模型方法常常产生运动模糊、结构扭曲和时间不一致等问题。事件相机能够提供高时间分辨率的运动线索，有助于弥补这些不足。但现有的事件辅助模型需要从头训练，成本高昂。因此，希望利用预训练扩散模型的强大生成能力，同时以最小的架构改动引入事件信号。

Method: 提出基于适配器的框架，将事件相机信息整合进预训练的图像到视频扩散模型。具体而言，使用图像扭曲事件（IWEs）和双向稀疏光流来提供空间和时间上对齐的引导信息，在扩散生成过程中注入这些事件引导的结构与运动线索，从而缓解插值伪影并提高重建保真度与时间一致性。

Result: 在真实和合成基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在各项指标上持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过适配器方式将事件线索注入预训练扩散模型，能够以极小的架构改动有效提升视频帧插值在复杂场景下的性能，兼顾重建保真度和时间一致性，展示了事件信号与预训练生成模型结合的潜力。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models have recently advanced video frame interpolation by synthesizing intermediate frames between input images. However, handling large temporal gaps and complex motion remains challenging, often resulting in motion blur, structural distortions, and temporal inconsistencies. Event cameras provide high-temporal-resolution motion cues that are well suited for bridging these gaps and improving interpolation quality. To exploit this advantage without training an event-assisted model from scratch, we propose an adapter-based framework that incorporates event-derived cues into a pre-trained image-to-video diffusion model with minimal architectural changes. Specifically, our method leverages Image Warped Events (IWEs) and bidirectional sparse optical flow to provide spatially and temporally aligned guidance during generation. By injecting these event-guided structural and motion cues into the diffusion process, our approach reduces interpolation artifacts and improves both reconstruction fidelity and temporal coherence. Experimental results on real and synthetic benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches. The project page is at https://joseph-lin-tech.github.io/BridgeEventDiT-VFI/.

</details>


### [31] [When Vision Becomes Text: Visual Token Pruning via Cross-Modal Residual Guidance in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10489)
*Congyang Ou,Ruike Song,Yang Zhou,Libo Sun,Haokui Zhang,Zhenbo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SIEVE，一种无需训练的视觉token压缩方法。它通过跨模态残差（CMR）量化文本无法解释的视觉信息，结合文本注意力相关性和残差空间多样性来保留任务相关且互补的token，在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上仅保留11.1%的视觉token即可保持97.5%的性能，实现3.62倍prefill加速和6.02倍KV缓存缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法基于相似度引导（成对文本-视觉和视觉-视觉token相关性），但仅捕获局部层级信号，忽略了VLM整个推理过程。需要一种能利用全局推理动态的高效引导方案。

Method: 1) 提出Cross Modal Absorption (CMA)，从几何表示角度量化文本token在深层LLM中吸收视觉信息的程度；2) 提出Cross Modal Residual (CMR)，通过Tikhonov正则化最小二乘将视觉token投影到文本子空间，利用重构残差量化文本无法解释的视觉信息；3) 提出SIEVE框架，结合CMR、文本注意力相关性和残差空间多样性来压缩视觉token，无需训练。

Result: 在多种VLM架构上验证有效性。LLaVA-NeXT-7B上仅保留11.1%视觉token，保持97.5%原始平均性能，实现3.62倍prefill加速、2.49倍端到端加速和6.02倍KV-cache缩减。

Conclusion: 利用LLM深层中文本token逐渐吸收视觉信息这一现象，CMR为视觉token压缩提供了超越局部相似度信号的全局引导信息，SIEVE在压缩率与性能保持之间取得了优越的平衡。

Abstract: Abundant visual information strengthens vision-language model (VLM) perception, yet massive visual tokens raise inference costs. Existing visual token pruning methods rely on similarity-based guidance, which exploits pairwise text-vision and vision-vision token correlations for compression. However, such methods only capture local layer-level signals and overlook the whole inference process in VLM. In this paper, we revisit VLM inference and present a new efficient guidance scheme that complements similarity-based guidance. In particular, we identify a key observation: as LLM layers deepen, text tokens continuously aggregate visual information via self-attention and progressively absorb partial visual content into textual representations. To quantify this phenomenon, we propose Cross Modal Absorption (CMA) from a geometric representation perspective to measure how much visual information is absorbed by text, revealing that more visual tokens in deeper layers can be approximately explained by the text subspace. We accordingly propose Cross Modal Residual (CMR). It projects visual tokens onto the text subspace via Tikhonov regularized least squares and exploits reconstruction residuals to quantify visual information that cannot be explained by text. Finally, based on CMR, we present SIEVE, a training-free visual token compression method that combines CMR, text-attention relevance, and residual-space diversity to retain task-relevant and complementary tokens. Experiments on diverse VLM architectures verify the effectiveness of SIEVE. For instance, on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, SIEVE keeps only $11.1\%$ of visual tokens while preserving $97.5\%$ of the original average performance, achieving $3.62\times$ prefill speedup, $2.49\times$ end-to-end speedup, and a $6.02\times$ KV-cache reduction.

</details>


### [32] [SapiensID 2.0: Aligning Human Recognition Foundation Models with Human Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10497)
*Yiyang Su,Jie Zhu,Feng Liu,Anil K. Jain,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SapiensID 2.0，一个同时具备语义感知和时间感知的人体识别框架，通过从多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）迁移零样本语义知识到判别性嵌入空间，并设计Invariant Trait Alignment和Transient Noise Disentanglement解决维度不匹配问题，同时通过Kinematic Semantic Attention Head在时间窗口内跟踪语义补丁以捕捉运动签名，在行人重识别和步态识别等任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型在人体识别中主要依赖静态几何特征提取，这与人类感知方式根本不同。当前模型存在"语义盲区"问题：过度拟合瞬态噪声、未能利用不变的软生物特征，且难以捕捉时间运动签名。需要弥合这一差距。

Method: 1) 从多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）迁移零样本语义知识到判别性嵌入空间以解决软生物特征标注缺失问题；2) 提出Invariant Trait Alignment (ITA)用于蒸馏核心持久特征，Transient Noise Disentanglement (TND)用于解耦衣物等伪影，解决维度不匹配；3) 设计Kinematic Semantic Attention Head (K-SAH)，将空间注意力扩展到时间窗口，通过追踪语义补丁捕捉运动学签名，无需大规模视频数据集。

Result: 实验表明SapiensID 2.0在基于图像和视频的行人重识别与步态识别任务上均达到最先进性能，同时保持鲁棒的人脸识别能力。

Conclusion: 通过融合语义知识迁移与运动学时间建模，SapiensID 2.0弥补了现有模型与人类感知之间的鸿沟，证明了语义和时间感知结合的有效性，为人体识别领域提供了新的范式。

Abstract: While foundation models have significantly advanced human recognition across diverse modalities, they predominantly rely on static, geometric feature extraction. This approach fundamentally diverges from human perception. Consequently, current models often suffer from "semantic blindness," overfitting to transient noise while failing to leverage invariant soft biometrics, and struggle to capture temporal motion signatures. To bridge this gap, we propose SapiensID 2.0, a human recognition framework enriched with both semantic and temporal awareness. To overcome the lack of soft-biometric annotations, we transfer zero-shot semantic knowledge from Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) into a discriminative embedding space. We resolve the dimensional mismatch between these spaces using Invariant Trait Alignment (ITA) to distill core persistent traits, and Transient Noise Disentanglement (TND) to decouple artifacts like clothing. Furthermore, we design a Kinematic Semantic Attention Head (K-SAH) that extends spatial attention across temporal windows. By tracking semantic patches over time, K-SAH captures rich kinematic signatures without requiring large-scale video datasets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SapiensID 2.0 achieves state-of-the-art performance across image- and video-based person re-identification and gait recognition, while maintaining robust face recognition capabilities.

</details>


### [33] [DSAR: Dual-Stream Autoregressive Modeling of Temporal Cloth Dynamics for Photorealistic Animatable Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10500)
*Haozhong Xiong,Yao Yu,Yu Zhou,Sidan Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双流自回归框架，用于从RGB视频创建逼真且时间连贯的可动人体化身，显式建模布料物理中的时间因果性，显著提升渲染质量、时间一致性和对训练分布外姿态的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽视布料物理固有的时间因果性——当前布料状态是由前一状态通过时间演化而来，而非仅由瞬时骨骼构型决定。这种忽视导致网络学习的是姿态-外观关联而非运动演化，从而在分布外姿态上产生过度平滑外观或严重伪影。

Method: 提出双流自回归框架：(1) 几何流——从上一帧传播表面位移；(2) 状态流——将当前特征与从记忆库检索的历史状态相融合。同时引入运动自适应聚合以处理空间变化的动力学，以及自适应正则化以在平滑性与灵活性之间取得平衡。

Result: 在具有挑战性的数据集上实验表明，该方法在渲染质量、时间一致性和对训练分布外运动模式的泛化能力方面均显著优于现有方法，验证了双流时间建模能有效捕获真实布料动力学。

Conclusion: 双流时间建模是实现真实布料动态的关键，显式建模几何信息传播与隐式内部状态记忆，能够克服现有方法在泛化性和真实感上的根本局限。

Abstract: Creating photorealistic and temporally coherent animatable human avatars from RGB videos remains challenging. Current methods struggle to capture realistic cloth dynamics, producing over-smoothed appearance or severe artifacts on out-of-distribution poses. This limitation stems from a fundamental oversight: existing approaches neglect the temporal causality inherent in cloth physics, where current states emerge from previous states through temporal evolution rather than instantaneous skeletal configurations alone. Without explicit modeling of this causal structure, networks learn pose-appearance correlations instead of motion evolution, leading to poor generalization. We introduce a dual-stream autoregressive framework that explicitly models both observable geometric information and implicit internal state. The geometric stream propagates surface displacement from the previous frame, while the state stream fuses current features with historical states retrieved from a memory bank. Motion-adaptive aggregation handles spatially-varying dynamics, and adaptive regularization balances smoothness with flexibility. Experiments on challenging datasets demonstrate significant improvements in rendering quality, temporal consistency, and generalization to motion patterns beyond training distributions, validating that dual-stream temporal modeling enables realistic cloth dynamics.

</details>


### [34] [Towards Color-Faithful Low-Light Image Enhancement via Adaptive Color Debiasing and Saturation Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10512)
*Zhichen Yang,Rui Xu,Yuzhen Niu,Fusheng Li,Hui Da,Ri Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAGE框架，通过圆柱形自适应LAB色彩空间（AdaLAB）和自适应圆柱形色彩变换（AdaCCT）来同时解决低光图像增强中的色彩偏置与饱和度异常问题，在多个基准上实现了更忠实的色彩恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光成像因低信噪比和成像过程常引入色彩偏置。现有低光增强方法虽能恢复亮度，但忠实色彩还原仍具挑战，表现为整体色彩偏置以及局部欠饱和与过饱和并存。现有方法缺乏统一的色彩校正方案。

Method: 提出CAGE框架，包括两个核心组件：1) AdaLAB，一种圆柱形自适应LAB色彩空间，为均匀色彩校正提供解耦的、图像特定的基底；2) AdaCCT，自适应圆柱形色彩变换，包含RGB与AdaLAB之间的前向/逆变换。前向变换在骨干增强前通过色度平面平移与缩放抑制嵌入的色彩偏置；逆变换通过超出色域的光度补偿实现忠实的饱和度校正。

Result: 在多个基准上的广泛实验表明，CAGE能实现更忠实的色彩恢复，显著降低色彩偏置和饱和度异常，并在不同低光增强骨干网络上提供更好的整体视觉质量。

Conclusion: CAGE框架通过解耦的色彩空间设计与双阶段色彩变换，有效解决了低光增强中的色彩偏置与饱和度失真问题，为低光图像增强提供了一条色彩忠实的可行路径。

Abstract: Low-light imaging often introduces color bias caused by the low signal-to-noise ratio and the image formation process. Although recent low-light image enhancement methods have achieved strong brightness recovery, faithful color restoration remains challenging, manifesting as overall color bias together with local under- and over-saturation. To address this issue, we propose CAGE, a cylindrical color correction framework with adaptive color debiasing and gamut-harmonized saturation rectification for color-faithful low-light image enhancement. We first introduce AdaLAB, a cylindrical adaptive LAB color space that provides a decoupled and image-specific basis for uniform color correction. Building on this color space, we further develop AdaCCT, an adaptive cylindrical color transform with forward and inverse transforms for the conversion between RGB and AdaLAB color space, as well as necessary color debiasing and saturation rectification. The forward transform suppresses embedded color bias before backbone enhancement by reorganizing the chromatic distribution through chromatic-plane shifting and scaling, while the inverse transform achieves faithful saturation rectification through out-of-gamut lightness compensation. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks show that CAGE achieves more faithful color restoration, specifically reduces color bias and saturation abnormality, and delivers better overall visual quality across different low-light enhancement backbones. The code is available at https://yangzhichen763.github.io/CAGE/.

</details>


### [35] [SafeCap: Improving LVLM Safety with Image Captioning Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10513)
*Caoyuan Ma,Wenpu Liu,Weichu Xie,Tian Gu,Shilei Zhao,Lingxi Min,Shuai Dong,Yuqi Xu,Ji Zhao,Ziyue Wang,Wenzheng Chang,Taiqiang Wu,Yongfu Zhu,Wenqi Shao,Yinqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SafeCap是一个基于强化学习的多模态安全对齐框架，通过让LVLM先生成与安全相关的图像描述再生成最终答案，利用冻结的LLM对该描述的安全性判断作为奖励信号来优化策略模型，在五个多模态安全基准和六个视觉效用基准上显著提升安全性能（3.7-19.0个百分点的增益），同时保持或提升视觉效用，并在受控对比中优于安全SFT、DPO和SafeGRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）继承了语言骨干的安全对齐，但仍容易受到利用视觉输入绕过安全对齐的越狱攻击。现有的安全对齐方法（如直接拒绝监督）不足以让模型暴露与安全响应生成相关的视觉线索，因此需要一种新的对齐机制来增强模型对视觉安全信号的理解和利用。

Method: SafeCap是一个强化学习框架，核心思路是训练策略模型先生成安全相关的图像描述（self-captioning），再基于该描述生成最终答案。该描述通过一个冻结的LLM进行安全判断，将判断结果作为奖励信号来优化描述质量。这种"描述中介"的目标函数鼓励策略模型暴露与安全响应生成相关的视觉关键信息，而非仅仅依赖直接的拒绝监督。

Result: 在五个多模态安全基准和六个视觉效用基准上，SafeCap在其设计的DirectCap协议下显著提升聚合安全性能，在四种模型设置下安全平均增益达3.7-19.0个百分点，同时保持或提升视觉效用。在匹配骨干网络和数据的受控对比中，SafeCap优于安全SFT、DPO和SafeGRPO。

Conclusion: SafeCap证明了"描述中介"的强化学习机制对于多模态安全对齐的有效性，为LVLM的安全对齐提供了一种超越直接拒绝监督的新范式，能够在提升安全性的同时不牺牲甚至提升视觉效用。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that exploit visual inputs to bypass safety alignment inherited from their language backbones. We propose SafeCap, a reinforcement-learning framework that aligns LVLMs through learned self-captioning. SafeCap trains a policy model to first generate a safety-relevant image caption and then produce a final answer; the caption is further optimized by whether it enables a frozen LLM to reach a safety-aligned decision. This caption-mediated objective encourages the policy to expose visual cues relevant to safe response generation rather than relying solely on direct refusal supervision. Across five multimodal safety benchmarks and six vision-utility benchmarks, SafeCap substantially improves aggregate safety performance under its intended DirectCap protocol, with gains of 3.7-19.0 points in safety average across four model settings while maintaining comparable or improved vision utility. Under controlled comparisons on matched backbones and data, SafeCap outperforms safety SFT, DPO, and SafeGRPO, demonstrating the effectiveness of caption-mediated reinforcement learning for multimodal safety alignment.

</details>


### [36] [SparSTAR: Sparse Attention for SpaceTime AutoRegressive Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10519)
*Jongbeom Lee,Hyunwoo Yu,Jincheol Yang,Jaemin Choi,Suk-Ju Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SparSTAR，一种为InfinityStar视频生成模型定制的免训练块稀疏注意力方法。SparSTAR在昂贵尺度层和注意力头上通过前向稀疏路径选择关键块，保留必要的条件上下文，在保持全token和全精化尺度的同时提供约1.6倍端到端加速，且VBench指标和重建保真度接近稠密基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: InfinityStar通过图像和剪辑金字塔序列将视觉自回归生成扩展到视频任务，但其变化的尺度和跨剪辑上下文导致晚期尺度注意力计算代价高昂。从扩散模型或图像VAR模型直接复用稀疏模式不可靠，需要专门针对这一动态尺度与剪辑场景设计的稀疏注意力方法。

Method: SparSTAR采用免训练的块稀疏注意力，在代价高昂的尺度层和每个注意力头上，利用当前query和key激活对连续关键块进行评分，保留所需的条件上下文，并通过仅前向的稀疏路径执行所选块。方法基于三项分析指导：剪辑内跨尺度一致性、跨剪辑边界的模式持续性、以及跨遥远尺度复用时的质量退化程度。

Result: 在720p文生视频和图像生视频任务上，SparSTAR保留每个token和精化尺度，实现约1.6倍端到端加速，VBench指标和配对输出重建保真度与稠密InfinityStar接近。

Conclusion: 关键块随目标尺度变化而移动，说明在每个目标尺度重新计算块选择比复用迁移的掩码更可靠，这一发现验证了SparSTAR逐尺度重选块策略的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: InfinityStar extends visual autoregressive generation to video through a sequence of image and clip pyramids. Its changing scale and cross-clip context, however, leave late-scale attention costly and make sparse patterns reused from diffusion or image VAR models unreliable. We introduce SparSTAR, a training-free block-sparse attention method tailored to this setting. At each expensive scale and attention head, SparSTAR scores contiguous key blocks from the current query and key activations, retains required conditioning context, and executes the selected blocks through a forward-only sparse path. We analyze cross-scale consistency within a clip, pattern persistence across clip boundaries, and quality degradation as reuse spans increasingly distant scales. Across these analyses, important key blocks shift, showing that recomputing block selection at each target scale is more reliable than reusing a transferred mask. On 720p text-to-video and image-to-video generation, SparSTAR preserves every token and refinement scale while providing about a 1.6x end-to-end speedup and maintaining VBench and paired-output reconstruction fidelity close to dense InfinityStar.

</details>

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个语义感知的多模态预训练框架，显式建模表格数据的内在二维结构（特征间层级与特征内连续-离散二元性），通过重要性感知自适应掩码和软标签离散化模块，在皮肤病学与眼科数据集上达到SOTA水平。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出语义感知框架：(1) 重要性感知自适应掩码（Importance-Aware Adaptive Masking）构建无标签课程学习，优先突出诊断重要性高的特征，建模特征间层级关系；(2) 软标签离散化模块（Soft-Label Discretized Module）用稳定的分布匹配替代不稳定的数值回归，以数学方式保持序数关系，建模特征内连续-离散二元性。

Result: 在大规模皮肤病学数据集（SLICE-3D, HOP）和眼科数据集（EyePACS）上的大量实验确立了新的SOTA性能，展现出优异的鲁棒性和跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模表格数据的二维结构（跨特征层级与特征内序数关系），语义感知框架有效解决了现有方法将表格扁平化处理和连续回归不稳定的问题，在医学多模态预训练中实现更优性能与泛化。

Abstract: While vision-language models dominate medical representation learning, unstructured text lacks the dense, quantitative diagnostic phenotypes inherent in structured clinical tables. However, existing multimodal pre-training methods underutilize this potential due to semantic-agnostic designs that treat tabular inputs as flat vectors and employ unstable continuous regression objectives. To overcome this, we propose a novel semantic-aware framework explicitly modeling the intrinsic two-dimensional structure of tabular data. First, addressing the inter-feature hierarchy of varying diagnostic importance, we introduce Importance-Aware Adaptive Masking to construct a label-free curriculum prioritizing salient features. Second, addressing the intra-feature continuity-discreteness duality, we propose a Soft-Label Discretized Module that replaces unstable numerical regression with stable distribution matching, thereby mathematically preserving ordinal relationships. Extensive experiments across large-scale dermatology (SLICE-3D, HOP) and ophthalmology (EyePACS) datasets establish a new state-of-the-art (SOTA), demonstrating exceptional robustness and cross-domain generalizability.

</details>


### [38] [Rethinking Text-Based Image Retrieval in Specific Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10524)
*Jingyang Tan,Sheng Yang,Yuanpeng Chen,Jian Wang,Nianjin Ye,Chen Xing,Lanpeng Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对文本图像检索（TBIR）中现有基准假设"单一查询对应单一匹配图像"的局限性，设计了领域特定的多匹配TBIR数据引擎，并构建了包含5万张监控图像和200个查询的基准SecMM-TBIR。同时提出语义感知微调（SAFT）框架，通过语义感知软标签监督和模态内结构蒸馏来解决特定领域中对比学习严重误报负样本的问题，实验表明SAFT在SecMM-TBIR上平均获得7.8个mAP@20提升，且不损害通用域性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TBIR基准基于单一的查询-图像匹配假设构建，在通用场景中有效，但在监控等特定领域难以反映实际系统性能——一个查询往往对应多个相关候选图像。此外，特定领域中传统的对比学习会遭受严重假阴性问题，迫使模型推开语义相近的样本对，从而降低检索性能。

Method: （1）设计领域特定多匹配TBIR（DSMM-TBIR）数据引擎，并构建包含50k监控图像和200个综合查询的基准SecMM-TBIR；（2）提出语义感知微调（SAFT）框架，包含语义感知软标签监督（SASS）和模态内结构蒸馏（ISD），用于解决特定领域中的语义压缩问题。

Result: 在多种CLIP类模型上进行实验，SAFT相比标准的图像-文本对比（ITC）微调在SecMM-TBIR上平均获得7.8个mAP@20的提升，同时通用领域性能也得到改善。

Conclusion: SAFT为特定领域的TBIR任务建立了有前景的范式，解决了多匹配场景下的假阴性问题；完整的基准将公开发布以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Driven by the rapid advancement of vision-language representation learning, Text-based Image Retrieval (TBIR) has made notable progress. However, existing benchmarks are predominantly constructed on an exclusive single-match assumption between query and images. While effective in general scenarios, this assumption fails to reflect practical system performance in specific domains (e.g., surveillance), where a single query often corresponds to multiple relevant candidate images. To address this limitation, we design a Domain-Specific Multi-Match Text-based Image Retrieval (DSMM-TBIR) data engine. Leveraging this engine, we construct Security Multi-Match TBIR (SecMM-TBIR), a benchmark comprising 50k surveillance images with 200 comprehensive queries. Furthermore, we observe that vanilla contrastive learning in specific domains suffers from severe false negatives, forcing the model to push apart semantically similar pairs and thus degrading retrieval performance. We propose the Semantic-Aware Fine-Tuning (SAFT) framework to address semantic compression in specific domains, which incorporates Semantic-Aware Soft-Label Supervision (SASS) and Intra-modal Structural Distillation (ISD) to establish a promising paradigm for domain-specific TBIR tasks. Experiments across diverse CLIP-like models demonstrate that SAFT yields an average mAP@20 gain of 7.8 points on SecMM-TBIR over standard image-text contrastive (ITC) fine-tuning, while also improving general-domain performance. The entire benchmark will be released to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [39] [Dynamic Context Adapters: Efficiently Infusing History into Vision-and-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10525)
*Yuhang Song,Bor-Jiun Lin,Jiaxu Liu,Te-Chuan Chiu,Anh Nguyen,Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DCA是一种针对预训练视觉语言模型（VLMs）的动态上下文适配器，通过固定大小的动态压缩记忆保留历史语义，在不进行帧拼接的前提下为静态VLM注入时序记忆能力，实现了注意力FLOPs降低25%以上、内存节省13%，并提升了长时程任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在序列决策任务中缺乏对历史上下文的整合能力。现有方案要么直接拼接历史帧导致二次方注意力复杂度和高内存消耗，要么通过时间压缩造成严重的信息丢失。亟需一种既能保持计算效率又能保留历史语义的上下文注入方法。

Method: 提出Dynamic Context Adapter（DCA），一种面向预训练VLM的新型上下文注入方法。DCA采用固定大小的动态压缩记忆来保留历史语义，避免帧拼接，并作为桥梁将静态VLM与循环策略（recurrent policies）连接起来，使预训练模型获得记忆能力的同时保持计算效率。

Result: DCA在注意力FLOPs上实现了超过25%的降低，内存使用节省13%，同时显著提升了长时程任务的性能表现。

Conclusion: DCA通过动态压缩记忆机制有效解决了VLM在序列决策中的历史上下文整合难题，在降低计算开销的同时增强了模型的时序理解能力，为预训练VLM赋予记忆能力提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Historical context integration presents a fundamental challenge for Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in sequential decision-making tasks. Current VLMs process visual inputs independently, which creates critical limitations for downstream applications that require temporal understanding. Direct incorporation of historical frames into Transformer inputs produces quadratic attention complexity and excessive memory consumption. Existing approaches suffer from significant drawbacks: computational inflation or substantial information loss through temporal compression. To address these challenges, we introduce Dynamic Context Adapter (DCA), a novel context injection approach for pretrained VLMs. Our method employs fixed-size, dynamically compressed memory to preserve historical semantics without frame concatenation. DCA bridges static VLMs and recurrent policies and enables memory capabilities in pretrained models while maintaining computational efficiency. DCA achieves over $25\%$ reduction in attention FLOPs and $13\%$ memory savings while improving performance on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [40] [Flow Straight to Reality: Perceptually Consistent Flow Matching for Efficient Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10544)
*Sangwoo Jo,Donggeun Ko,Jayeon Kang,Youngsang Kwak,Jaehwa Kwak,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PCFlow（Perceptually Consistent Flow Matching），一个统一的图像复原框架，通过直接参数化从退化观测到干净目标的连续传输，在轻量级卷积网络上联合优化失真（distortion）和感知（perception）质量，实现高效少步推理的鲁棒图像复原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像复原方法在失真与感知之间面临根本性权衡：最小化像素级误差导致过平滑结果，而追求感知真实感常引入结构偏差。虽然后验采样或多阶段生成管线能部分平衡此权衡，但计算昂贵且架构复杂，亟需一个统一、高效且简洁的解决方案。

Method: PCFlow的核心方法包括：(1) 潜在一贯性流目标（Latent Consistency Flow objective），驱动稳定且高效的少步推理；(2) 潜在一贯性感知损失（LCPL），直接将语义约束施加于引导速度场，使动力学向视觉锐利的数据流形方向演变；(3) 冲突无梯度投影策略（conflict-free gradient projection），用于缓解结构与感知一致性之间的冲突，稳定多目标优化；(4) 轻量级纯卷积骨干网络。

Result: PCFlow在多种图像复原任务上取得了具有竞争力的性能，同时计算开销仅为传统方法的一小部分，验证了少步推理的高效性和多目标优化的稳定性。

Conclusion: PCFlow通过统一框架直接参数化退化到干净的连续传输，在不引入复杂架构的前提下有效平衡了失真与感知质量，为高效图像复原提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Image restoration is fundamentally constrained by the tradeoff between distortion and perception: minimizing pixel-wise error yields over-smoothed results, whereas optimizing for perceptual realism often introduces structural deviations. Recent approaches attempt to balance this tradeoff via posterior sampling or multi-stage generative pipelines, yet remain computationally expensive and architecturally complex. To overcome these limitations, we propose PCFlow (Perceptually Consistent Flow Matching), a unified framework that directly parameterizes a continuous transport from degraded observations to clean targets, jointly optimizing distortion and perceptual quality. While its latent consistency flow objective drives stable and efficient few-step inference, a Latent Consistency Perceptual Loss (LCPL) imposes semantic constraints directly on the guiding velocity field, steering the dynamics toward visually sharp data manifolds. Furthermore, recognizing the inherent conflict between structural and perceptual consistencies, we integrate a conflict-free gradient projection strategy to stabilize the multi-objective optimization landscape. Combined with lightweight, convolution-only backbone, PCFlow achieves competitive performance across diverse restoration tasks at a fraction of traditional computational costs.

</details>

*Ushnish Sarkar,Suvajit Patra,Bhaswar Chattopadhyay,Pranab Singha Roy,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于HamNoSys（汉堡标记系统）的细粒度手形识别基准数据集，包含15名参与者、144,000张RGB图像、160个手形类别，并使用多种模型（ResNet-18、ViT-B/16、GCN、XGBoost）在subject-dependent和LOSO两种评估协议下建立基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的手形识别研究缺乏基于音系学定义的、广泛的视觉手形类别库，且多数没有考虑跨说话人（signer-aware）的泛化评估，限制了计算手语转写、识别和翻译技术的发展。

Method: 构建了一个基于官方HamNoSys 4手形图表的160类手形数据集，包含144,000张来自15名参与者的RGB图像，类别均衡。评估上采用ResNet-18和ViT-B/16作为基于外观的模型，图卷积网络和XGBoost基于手部关键点进行评估。使用类别分层的主体相关划分和15折留一主体（LOSO）协议，并在LSWH100和ASL Fingerspelling Dataset A上进行外部验证。

Result: 主体相关基准在所有四类模型上建立了可复现的参考性能；LOSO评估显示当需要泛化到未见参与者时识别性能显著下降。在ASL Fingerspelling Dataset A上，平均LOSO top-1准确率范围为82.20%至87.40%。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一套可复现的采集、整理和互补性评估流程，为细粒度孤立手形识别研究及更可及的手语技术开发提供了标准化资源。

Abstract: Purpose: Fine-grained handshape recognition supports computational sign-language transcription, recognition, and translation, but broad, phonetically defined visual inventories with signer-aware evaluation remain limited. This work introduces a benchmark grounded in the language-independent Hamburg Notation System (HamNoSys). Methods: A balanced dataset of 144,000 RGB images was collected from 15 participants for 160 handshape classes defined by the official HamNoSys 4 Handshapes Chart. ResNet-18 and ViT-B/16 were evaluated as appearance-based models, while a graph convolutional network and XGBoost were evaluated from hand landmarks. Both a class-stratified subject-dependent split and a 15-fold leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocol were used. The same model families were additionally assessed on LSWH100 and ASL Fingerspelling Dataset A for external context. Results: The subject-dependent benchmarks established reproducible reference performance across all four model families, whereas LOSO evaluation exposed a substantial reduction when recognition was required to generalise to unseen participants. On ASL Fingerspelling Dataset A, mean LOSO top-1 accuracy ranged from 82.20% to 87.40%. Conclusion: The documented acquisition, curation, and complementary evaluation protocols pro-vide a reproducible resource for fine-grained isolated-handshape research and for developing more accessible sign-language technologies.

</details>


### [42] [$π$-SUB: A Physics-Informed Synthetic Underwater Benchmark Dataset for Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10589)
*Namritha Lasyapriya Maddali,Rajini Makam,Suresh Sundaram,Narasimhan Sundararajan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出π-SUB，一个基于物理信息的水下合成基准数据集生成框架，通过扩展经典水下成像模型，涵盖深度相关的下射辐照度、生物吸收和环境散射，覆盖全部10种Jerlov水体类型，缩小了合成数据与真实数据的差距。实验表明π-SUB在超真实性和泛化性两方面均优于现有方法（FID降低46%，UIQM提升4.18%，NIQE降低48.78%），是新一代水下图像增强方法开发的高质量基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下图像增强（UIE）方法面临合成数据与真实数据之间的域差距问题，导致在合成数据上训练的模型难以泛化到真实水下场景。缺乏能够真实模拟水下物理成像过程的高质量合成基准数据集是制约UIE发展的关键瓶颈。

Method: π-SUB框架扩展了经典水下图像形成模型，具体包括：1）引入深度相关的下射辐照度建模；2）考虑生物分辨的（biologically resolved）吸收机制；3）覆盖全部十种Jerlov水体类型的环境散射；4）包含独立可控的残余现象（residual phenomena）。基于该框架生成配对的水下合成-参考图像数据集，覆盖从浅水到深水、从沿海到远洋的各种环境。

Result: 1）超真实性评估：π-SUB的全局FID比Syrea低46%；2）泛化性评估：使用四种SOTA UIE架构（FUnIE-GAN、Pix2Pix、PUIE-Net、Phaseformer），在六个训练数据集（一个真实+五个合成）上独立训练，并在六个真实基准数据集上测试。π-SUB相比PHISWID（次优）UIQM提升4.18%，相比Syrea提升9.46%；NIQE分别降低48.78%和23.98%。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明π-SUB在超真实性和泛化性方面均显著优于现有合成数据集，可作为开发下一代水下图像增强方法的可靠基准。代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: This paper presents $π$-SUB, a physics-informed framework for generating synthetic underwater benchmark datasets that bridges the synthetic-to-real gap for Underwater Image Enhancement (UIE). The proposed framework extends the classical underwater image formation model by incorporating depth-dependent downwelling irradiance, biologically resolved absorption, and environmental scattering across all ten Jerlov water types, together with independently controllable residual phenomena. Using this framework, the $π$-SUB dataset consists of paired synthetic underwater-reference images spanning shallow-to-deep and coastal-to-oceanic environments. Extensive simulation studies have been carried out to evaluate $π$-SUB along two criteria namely hyper-realism and generalizability. For hyper-realism, $π$-SUB attains a global Frechet Inception Distance (FID) that is 46% lower than Syrea. For generalizability, four state-of-the-art UIE architectures (FUnIE-GAN, Pix2Pix, PUIE-Net, and Phaseformer) are used for comparative evaluation of $π$-SUB. These models were independently trained on six datasets including one real and five synthetic datasets and tested on six real-world benchmarks datasets. Across four UIE architectures and six real benchmark datasets, $π$-SUB improves UIQM by 4.18% over PHISWID (next best) and 9.46% over Syrea (next best), while reducing NIQE by 48.78% and 23.98%, respectively. These results establish $π$-SUB as a hyper-realistic and generalizable benchmark for developing the next generation of underwater image enhancement methods. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/airl-iisc/pi-SUB

</details>


### [43] [Rethinking Data Efficiency in Industrial Dense Prediction: Pretraining Coherence, Not Inductive Bias, Determines ViTs Low-Data Advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10590)
*Haoran Sui,Yaoyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过四个工业数据集上的受控实验，指出ViT在工业密集预测中数据效率低下的根本原因是预训练不一致性（ImageNet预训练的ViT骨干与COCO预训练的CNN颈部之间的统计不匹配），而非自注意力机制本身的缺陷，并提出轻量级AlignBlock族进行金字塔级特征重校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普遍认为ViT比CNN在工业密集预测任务中需要更多标注数据，但这种差距的根本原因尚不明确。作者希望区分是自注意力架构的内在缺陷还是预训练策略的不匹配导致了数据效率差距。

Method: 1) 在四个工业数据集上进行受控实验，系统对比ViT与CNN的少样本性能；2) 表征跨架构特征差距（ImageNet预训练ViT骨干与COCO预训练CNN颈部）；3) 提出轻量级AlignBlock家族用于金字塔级特征重校准；4) 引入"嫁接"（Graft）策略，将CNN预训练颈部权重嫁接到ViT骨干上。

Result: 在领域相近场景下（≥200样本），Swin-Graft超越YOLOv11x（703样本时mAP@50为0.973 vs 0.956）；在领域差异较大的场景下，CNN保持优势（141样本时0.900 vs 0.600 mAP@50）。嫁接的颈部权重比随机初始化颈部可带来高达2.5倍的mAP提升。

Conclusion: 数据效率差距的根本原因是预训练不一致性，可以通过轻量级特征对齐和权重嫁接来弥合，使ViT在工业密集预测中达到甚至超越CNN的少样本性能。研究揭示了一个数据效率前沿：领域近端场景下ViT占优，领域远端场景下CNN仍具优势。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are widely believed to require more labeled data than CNNs for industrial dense prediction. Through controlled experiments on four industrial datasets, we show that the data-efficiency gap stems from pretraining incoherence, which refers to the statistical mismatch between ImageNet-pretrained ViT backbones and COCO-pretrained CNN necks, rather than from inherent self-attention deficits. We characterize the cross-architecture feature gap and propose a lightweight AlignBlock family for pyramid-level feature recalibration. Our core finding empirically identifies a data-efficiency frontier: for domain-proximal scenes with >= 200 samples, Swin-Graft surpasses YOLOv11x (terminal 703-shot: 0.973 vs 0.956 mAP@50); for domain-distant scenes, CNNs retain advantage (hook 141-shot: 0.900 vs 0.600 mAP@50). Grafted neck weights yield up to 2.5x the mAP of a randomly initialized neck.

</details>


### [44] [Gaussian Sculpting: End-to-End Controllable Surface Reconstruction via Field Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10602)
*Ke Jiaxin,Juncheng Liu,Yi Wang,Zhouhui Lian,Bin Liu,Shengfa Wang,Xiangjia He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gaussian Sculpting，一个全可微的端到端框架，通过将3D高斯锚定在可演化的不同曲面上，引导符号距离场（SDF）优化而非仅在后期处理中提取表面，从而在高斯溅射框架下实现高精度表面重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽能实现实时新视角合成，但在有限视角和Gaussian原语固有非规则性下难以恢复精确表面，且产生的几何误差难以人工修正。现有方法仅在后期处理中提取表面，缺乏SDF引导，导致重建质量受限。

Method: 1) 将Gaussians锚定在可演化的可微表面上，引导SDF优化；2) 设计双层训练策略（外层优化SDF表示的几何，内层在几何固定下更新Gaussians），实现稳定梯度隔离；3) 对Gaussian参数施加约束以确保与底层表面一致性；4) 引入基于八叉树划分的多分辨率细分方案，保留细节同时降低内存消耗。

Result: 在物体级场景的实验表明，该方法能有效移除冗余表面、恢复有限视角导致的缺失结构，即使在相对较低分辨率下也能实现强重建质量。

Conclusion: Gaussian Sculpting通过将表面引导集成到训练过程中而非后处理，有效解决了3DGS在受限视角下的几何精度问题，实现了高质量且内存高效的表面重建。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently enabled real-time novel view synthesis with impressive quality. However, it struggles to recover accurate surfaces under limited viewpoints and due to the inherent irregularity of Gaussian primitives. The resulting geometric errors are notoriously difficult to correct manually. To address these issues, we propose Gaussian Sculpting, a fully differentiable end-to-end framework for high-quality surface reconstruction. Our key insight is to anchor Gaussians onto an evolving differentiable surface, allowing them to guide signed distance field (SDF) optimization instead of extracting the surface only during post-processing. To enable stable gradient isolation during joint optimization, we design a bi-level training strategy in which the outer loop optimizes the geometry represented by the SDF, while the inner loop updates the Gaussians with the geometry fixed. We further impose constraints on Gaussian parameters to ensure consistency with the underlying surface, thereby improving both geometric and appearance fidelity during optimization. In addition, we introduce a multi-resolution subdivision scheme based on octree-like partitioning to preserve fine details while reducing memory consumption. Experiments on object-level scenes demonstrate that our method effectively removes redundant surfaces, recovers missing structures caused by limited viewpoints, and achieves strong reconstruction quality even at relatively low resolutions.

</details>


### [45] [InSight-doc: Agentic Visual Perception for Long-Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10628)
*Kaican Li,Weiyan Xie,Lewei Yao,Jiannan Wu,Lanqing Hong,Yongxiang Huang,Nevin L. Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InSight-doc 是一个智能体视觉感知框架，将视觉分辨率视为自适应的推理时资源，从低分辨率开始自适应地放大到高分辨率区域获取细节证据，无需外部检索器。通过 17.9K SFT 样本和 19.2K RL 样本训练，在文档 VQA 基准上提升 4.3-16.4 个准确率点，长文档上幻觉降低超 40%，推理延迟降低 41%-68%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档理解通常需要对大量视觉丰富的页面进行推理，导致推理成本高昂且容易发生上下文退化（context rot）。现有方法缺乏对视觉分辨率的自适应利用，因此需要一种推理高效的视觉感知方案。

Method: 提出InSight-doc智能体框架，将视觉分辨率作为自适应推理时资源。从低分辨率开始，选择性放大到高分辨率区域获取细节证据，无需外部检索器。构建了包含17.9K高质量SFT样本（带区域级放大轨迹）和19.2K困难RL样本的训练语料，采用SFT+RL两阶段训练策略。

Result: InSight-doc-8B在文档VQA基准上相比基线提升4.3-16.4个准确率点；在长文档上幻觉减少超40%，推理延迟降低41%-68%的同时保持准确率优势。

Conclusion: InSight-doc证明了一种active-perception（主动感知）的训练范式可以有效改善长文档理解中准确率与效率的权衡，代码、数据集和模型均已开源。

Abstract: Long-document understanding often requires reasoning over many visually rich pages, making inference costly and prone to context rot. In this work, we propose InSight-doc, an agentic visual perception framework that treats visual resolution as an adaptive reasoning-time resource. InSight-doc starts from low resolution and selectively zooms into high-resolution regions for finer evidence, without relying on any external retriever. To train such an agent, we construct an active-perception corpus of 17.9K high-quality SFT examples with region-level zoom-in trajectories, accompanied by 19.2K hard RL examples. Through SFT+RL, InSight-doc-8B improves the baseline by 4.3--16.4 accuracy points over document VQA benchmarks. On long documents, it reduces hallucination by more than 40% and inference latency by 41%--68% while maintaining an accuracy lead. Our code, datasets, and model are released at https://github.com/m-Just/InSight-doc .

</details>


### [46] [MedUP: Awakening Unified Understanding and Perception in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10635)
*Yuan Wang,Hualiang Wang,Yixin Chen,Songtao Jiang,Shujian Gao,Jiaming Lin,Siming Fu,Jian Wu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedUP是一种医学视觉语言模型，通过UniMedTok区域分词器将掩码编码为LLM词汇表中的离散标记，在共享标记空间中原生统一感知与理解，超越现有原生、代理和双解码器Med-VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型在精确视觉感知、分割和接地（grounding）方面存在挑战。已有方法要么将区域形式化为坐标字符串，要么依赖外部模块，导致感知与理解解耦，造成区域-语言对齐的表征差距。

Method: 提出了MedUP模型，核心是UniMedTok区域分词器，将掩码编码为LLM词汇表中的离散标记，使模型能够无缝地将掩码标记与文本交错。同时构建了UniMed-Train（184万实例）语料库，涵盖文本引导分割、区域接地理解、医学VQA和基于CoT的分割任务，并引入UniMed-Bench进行统一评估。

Result: 大量实验表明，MedUP在所有任务上均优于原生、代理和双解码器Med-VLM，同时与专业分割器保持竞争力，证明了统一理解与感知建模的强大潜力。

Conclusion: 在共享标记空间中原生统一医学图像的感知与理解是可行且有效的，为医学视觉语言模型提供了一种统一的建模范式。

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-VLMs) excel at verbalizing visual content, yet precise visual perception, segmentation, and grounding remain challenging. Existing approaches either verbalize regions as coordinate strings or rely on external modules that decouple perception from understanding, creating representation gaps for region-language alignment. We present MedUP, a Med-VLM that natively unifies perception and understanding within a shared token space. At its core lies UniMedTok, a region tokenizer that encodes masks as discrete tokens in the LLM vocabulary, enabling the model to seamlessly interleave mask tokens with text. We curate UniMed-Train, a 1.84M-instance corpus spanning text-guided segmentation, region-grounded understanding, medical VQA and CoT-based segmentation, and introduce UniMed-Bench for unified evaluation. Extensive experiments show that MedUP outperforms native, agentic, and dual-decoder Med-VLMs across all tasks while remaining competitive with specialist segmentors, demonstrating the strong potential of unified understanding and perception modeling.

</details>


### [47] [Precise Top-Layer Fabric Segmentation for Fabric Destacking with Edge- and Shape-Aware Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10648)
*Wenbo Dong,Dipankar Bhattacharya,Akinari Kobayashi,Akira Seino,Fuyuki Tokuda,Xuzhao Huang,Kai Tang,Norman C. Tien,Kazuhiro Kosuge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向堆叠织物顶层分割的新型训练架构，通过引入边缘感知分支和形状感知分支来监督骨干网络，从而提升织物顶层分割的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 织物去堆叠任务需要精确分割最顶层织物层，但织物边界细微且层间视觉相似度高，现有语义分割和基于边缘的分割方法难以应对这些复杂性，限制了机器人操作性能。

Method: 扩展经典编码器-解码器框架，引入两个专门分支：边缘感知分支用于增强边界描绘，形状感知分支利用CAD模型导出的参考掩码引导网络捕捉并对齐整体织物形状，两个分支共同监督骨干网络的训练调优。

Result: 在真实织物数据集上的实验表明，该训练方法优于既定基线方法，定量结果和消融研究均验证了多分支设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 多分支监督训练架构能够有效提升堆叠织物中顶层织物的分割精度，为机器人织物操作任务提供更可靠的感知基础。

Abstract: Fabric destacking requires precise segmentation of the topmost fabric layer, a task complicated by subtle fabric boundaries and high visual similarity between fabric layers. Existing semantic and edge-based segmentation approaches often struggle with these complexities, limiting the performance of robotic manipulation for different tasks. In this work, a novel segmentation training architecture tailored for top-layer fabric segmentation in stacked fabrics is proposed. The method extends the classical encoder-decoder framework by introducing two specialized branches - an edge-aware branch and a shape-aware branch - that are used to supervise the backbone network for better tuning. The edge-aware branch enhances boundary delineation, while the shape-aware branch guides the network to capture and align the overall fabric shape with reference masks derived from Computer Aided Design (CAD) models. Experiments on a real-world fabric dataset demonstrate that the training approach outperforms established baselines, verifying the effectiveness of the multi-branch design through both quantitative results and ablation studies.

</details>


### [48] [PolypVision: A Three-Stage Hierarchical Deep Learning Framework for Classification and Segmentation of Colorectal Polyps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10649)
*Hamidreza Bolhasani,Hamidreza Rastad,Amir Mohammad Akbari,Mohammad Tashakoripour,Parnian Asadollahi,Ata Khodami,Mojgan Forootan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PolypVision，一个三阶段层次化深度学习框架，用于结直肠息肉的检测、分割和分类。框架依次完成腺瘤/增生性分类（含Paris和JNet分类）、息肉分割与切除建议、以及腺瘤亚型分类。在三个公开数据集上评估，帧分类AUC约0.99，Kvasir-SEG上检测mAP@50达94.4%，性能优于或媲美现有方法，且为设备无关的免费Web应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结直肠癌是全球癌症相关死亡的主要原因之一，多由癌前息肉发展而来。对结直肠息肉的准确检测、分割以及内镜和组织学分类对及时临床干预至关重要，现有方法缺乏统一的端到端框架。

Method: PolypVision采用三阶段层次化架构：阶段1使用带Focal Loss的EfficientNetV2-M进行息肉二分类（腺瘤/增生性），同时完成Paris和JNet分类；阶段2以阶段1骨干网络为编码器，UNet++为解码器，使用Dice和BCE损失进行息肉分割及切除方法推荐；阶段3使用EfficientNetV2-M并从阶段2迁移学习，进行腺瘤亚型分类（管状、管状绒毛状、绒毛状）。

Result: 在PolypGen、Kvasir-SEG和CVC-ClinicDB三个公开数据集上，帧分类AUC约0.99，Kvasir-SEG检测mAP@50为94.4%，优于或匹配现有最先进方法。Grad-CAM确认模型关注临床相关病变特征，且框架设备无关，可在不同内镜系统上运行。

Conclusion: 层次化、迁移学习驱动的流水线配合任务特定的损失函数，为自动化结直肠息肉分析提供了一种稳健、设备无关且具有临床意义的方案。PolypVision以免费Web应用形式公开提供。

Abstract: Colorectal cancer (CRC) remains one of the leading causes of cancer-related mortality worldwide, predominantly arising from precancerous polyps. Accurate detection, segmentation, and endoscopic and histological classification of colorectal polyps are crucial for timely clinical intervention. In this study, we present PolypVision, a three-stage hierarchical deep learning framework that sequentially performs: (Stage 1) binary classification of polyps as adenomatous or hyperplastic, with simultaneous Paris and JNet classification, using EfficientNetV2-M with Focal Loss; (Stage 2) polyp segmentation with recommended resection method using a UNet++ decoder with the Stage 1 backbone as encoder, optimized with Dice and BCE losses; and (Stage 3) adenoma subtype classification (tubular, tubulovillous, villous) using EfficientNetV2-M with transfer learning from Stage 2. Evaluated on three public datasets -- PolypGen, Kvasir-SEG, and CVC-ClinicDB -- PolypVision achieves an AUC of approximately 0.99 for frame classification and a detection mAP@50 of 94.4% on Kvasir-SEG, outperforming or matching state-of-the-art methods. Gradient-weighted Class Activation Maps (Grad-CAM) confirm that the model attends to clinically relevant lesion features. The framework is device-independent, operating across diverse endoscopic imaging systems without hardware-specific adaptation. These results demonstrate that a hierarchical, transfer-learning-driven pipeline with task-specific loss functions offers a robust, device-independent, and clinically meaningful approach to automated colorectal polyp analysis. PolypVision is freely available as a web application at https://polypvision.com, a DataBioX initiative, with a free usage tier open to all users.

</details>


### [49] [Cross-View Sequential Visual Localization with Spatio-Temporal Context Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10660)
*Jiaping Wang,Shaobo Li,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于时序上下文增强的跨视角序列视觉定位框架，通过循环跨帧模块聚合历史状态信息来增强地面特征，在CVIS数据集上将平均定位误差从3.80米降至1.57米，R@1m从8.14%提升至40.22%，并在KITTI-CVL及真实车辆实验中验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角视觉定位方法大多逐帧独立处理，未能充分利用时序信息，在动态遮挡、光照变化和重复纹理场景下精度受限。连续可靠的定位对自动驾驶至关重要，因此需要利用时序上下文来提升定位精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出时序上下文增强的跨视角序列视觉定位框架：包含循环跨帧模块，从上一状态聚合历史上下文来增强当前帧的粗粒度地面特征；增强后的特征用于卫星候选区域分类，同时通过层次化细粒度特征进行精确的局部偏移估计。

Result: 在CVIS数据集上平均定位误差从3.80米降至1.57米，R@1m从8.14%提升至40.22%；直接迁移至KITTI-CVL平均误差为2.61米，目标域微调后降至2.27米；真实车辆零样本实验平均误差2.84米，R@5m达96.86%。

Conclusion: 时序上下文增强显著提升了跨视角定位精度，该框架在公开基准和真实道路场景中均展现出可靠的部署能力，验证了时序信息在跨视角序列定位中的关键价值。

Abstract: Continuous and reliable localization is essential for autonomous driving. Cross-view visual localization matches ground images with satellite maps, providing complementary localization cues for pipelines that depend on Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) signals and high-definition (HD) maps. Most existing cross-view visual localization methods process each frame independently, leaving temporal information underused and limiting accuracy under dynamic occlusion, illumination variation, and repetitive textures. This study proposes a temporal-context-enhanced framework for cross-view sequence visual localization. The proposed recurrent cross-frame module aggregates historical context from the previous state to enhance the coarse ground feature of each current frame. These enhanced features facilitate satellite candidate-region classification, while hierarchical fine-grained features enable precise local offset estimation. On the CVIS dataset, the proposed method reduces mean localization error from 3.80 m to 1.57 m and increases R@1 m from 8.14% to 40.22%. Direct transfer to KITTI-CVL achieves a mean error of 2.61 m, with target-domain fine-tuning further reducing the mean error to 2.27 m. Zero-shot field experiments on a real-world vehicle achieve a mean error of 2.84 m and R@5 m of 96.86%. These results demonstrate that temporal context enhancement significantly improves cross-view localization accuracy and supports robust deployment on public benchmarks and real-world roads.

</details>


### [50] [Chartography: A Benchmark for Professional Chart Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10677)
*Suhaas Garre,Chris Mutty,Sushant Mehta,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Chartography基准，包含100个来自专业实践领域的图表任务，由专业人士撰写并三重验证的问题。评估30个前沿模型配置，最佳仅达45.0% pass@1，远低于现有基准的80-90%，失败集中体现在视觉感知能力上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表基准不足以衡量专业人士从图表中做出决策的能力：被柱状图、折线图和饼图格式主导，推理链较短，且已接近饱和（前沿模型已达80-90%），无法有效区分模型能力。

Method: 构建Chartography基准，包含100个任务，图表来自专业实践中的领域特定格式（医学、工程、金融、制造、科学），问题由实际阅读这些图表的专业人士撰写，并由三位额外专家独立验证。评估采用30个前沿模型配置，每任务20次得分尝试。

Result: 最佳模型配置仅达到45.0%的平均pass@1，其余配置集中在9.0-39.5%之间。失败主要集中在视觉感知层面：模型难以捕捉细微特征、误读稀疏标注轴上的数值、处理3D投影几何错误，以及违反图表编码的领域约定。

Conclusion: Chartography揭示了现有基准的饱和度问题，并表明前沿模型在专业图表理解上存在显著的能力差距，尤其在视觉感知方面。所有任务、图像、来源元数据和评估代码均已公开，为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Professionals across medicine, engineering, finance, manufacturing, and the sciences often make consequential decisions from charts. Existing chart benchmarks do not sufficiently measure this ability: they are dominated by bar, line, and pie formats, rely on shorter reasoning chains, and are nearing saturation, with frontier models already scoring 80-90%. We introduce Chartography, a benchmark of 100 tasks that pair charts drawn from professional practice, in domain-specific formats that standard chart benchmarks rarely include, with questions written by professionals who read these charts for a living and independently verified by three additional experts. In an evaluation of 30 frontier-model configurations (20 scored trials per task), the best configuration reaches only 45.0% mean pass@1; the remainder span 9.0-39.5%. Failures concentrate in visual perception: models can miss nuanced features, misread values along sparsely labeled axes, mishandle projected 3D geometry, and violate domain conventions encoded in the chart. We release all tasks, images, provenance metadata, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [51] [Bridging Severe Cross-Modal Misalignment: End-to-End Visible-Infrared Object Detection via Explicit Feature-Domain Affine Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10680)
*Qi Ming,Yuyang Wang,Mingjing Zhao,Yifan Xiao,Zhixin Guo,Zhiqiang Zhou,Peng Sun,Juan Fang,Fuqiang Yang,Xudong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出联合特征域配准和检测网络（JFRDet），用于解决可见光-红外目标检测中严重的跨模态几何错位问题，并在新构建的DVMA基准上达到SOTA性能（69.7% mAP50）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可见光-红外目标检测方法主要依赖隐式特征适应来处理弱错位场景，而对大幅偏移的几何错位问题缺乏有效解决方案，导致性能显著退化。

Method: JFRDet包含三个核心模块：（1）跨模态仿射对齐（CMAA）模块，估计图像级仿射变换以显式对齐多级特征；（2）光照引导互补融合（IGCF）模块，根据光照变化自适应地利用模态可靠性进行跨模态融合；（3）对齐质量一致性门控（AQCG）策略，通过对齐可靠性和梯度一致性调节检测监督信号以稳定联合优化。

Result: 在自建的DroneVehicle Misaligned（DVMA）基准上，JFRDet达到69.7% mAP50，实现了SOTA性能。
