Conclusion: 通过显式特征级仿射对齐与光照自适应的融合策略，JFRDet有效解决了严重跨模态几何错位下的定向目标检测问题，并提供了新基准DVMA以推动该领域研究。

Abstract: Visible-infrared object detection relies on complementary RGB and thermal cues, but its performance is often degraded by cross-modal spatial misalignment. Most existing methods rely on implicit feature adaptation to handle weakly misaligned scenarios, while large-offset geometric discrepancies remain insufficiently addressed. In this paper, we propose a Joint Feature-domain Registration and Detection network (JFRDet), an end-to-end visible-infrared oriented object detector tailored for severely cross-modal geometric discrepancies. JFRDet introduces a Cross-Modal Affine Alignment (CMAA) module to estimate an image-level affine transformation for explicit multi-level feature alignment. Note that illumination changes directly affect the reliability of RGB cues, an Illumination-Guided Complementary Fusion (IGCF) module adaptively exploits modality reliability under varying illumination conditions for cross-modal fusion. Then, an Alignment Quality-Consistency Gating (AQCG) strategy stabilizes joint optimization by modulating detection supervision according to alignment reliability and gradient consistency. We further construct DroneVehicle Misaligned (DVMA), a benchmark for evaluating visible-infrared oriented object detection under severe cross-modal geometric misalignment. The proposed JFRDet achieves 69.7\% $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ on DVMA, which represents state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance. The code and dataset will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [52] [Visual Geometry Foundation-Aware Gaussians for Single-Frame Surround-View Driving Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10682)
*Junhong Lin,Jinlong Wang,Xianda Guo,Yanlun Peng,Wei Zheng,Guoqing Liu,Hanli Wang,Tiesong Zhao,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGGD 是一个基于视觉几何先验的3D高斯泼溅框架，用于前馈式环视驾驶场景重建，通过VGGT提供多视图几何先验token，配合双路径颈部、尺度热身和混合像素-体素高斯解码器，在nuScenes单帧基准上取得最优渲染质量并改善了几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单帧环视重建因相机间重叠区域极小，面临严重的几何不稳定和渲染伪影问题。现有方法虽然依赖复杂解码器或辅助线索，但受限于上游特征孱弱的几何建模能力，未能从根本上缓解稀疏环视视图中的几何模糊性。

Method: 1）利用VGGT提供可迁移的多视图几何先验token，将几何建模从解码端前移到前端编码器；2）提出双路径颈部（Dual-Path Neck），解耦几何一致表示与外观感知表示，改善弱观测区域的外观补全；3）引入尺度热身（Scale Warmup）策略，稳定早期几何学习并抑制自车姿态变化下的尺度漂移；4）使用混合像素-体素高斯解码器生成可渲染的3D高斯场景用于新视角合成。

Result: 在nuScenes单帧环视重建基准上，VGGD在整体渲染质量上优于所有对比方法，并显著提升了几何一致性指标。

Conclusion: 借助预训练的视觉几何先验来强化上游表示，能够有效缓解稀疏环视视图中的几何模糊性，证实了将几何建模前移到前端策略的有效性，为前馈式自动驾驶场景重建提供了新思路。

Abstract: Single-frame surround-view reconstruction faces severe geometric instability and rendering artifacts due to minimal inter-camera overlap. While existing methods rely on complex decoders or auxiliary cues, they remain bottlenecked by the weak geometric capacity of upstream features. We argue that leveraging pretrained visual geometry priors strengthens upstream representations and alleviates the geometric ambiguity in sparse surround views. To this end, we propose VGGD, a visual geometry foundation-aware 3D Gaussian Splatting framework for feed-forward surround-view driving reconstruction, which shifts geometric modeling to the frontend and adapts foundation priors to the driving camera setting. First, VGGD leverages VGGT to provide transferable multi-view geometric prior tokens. Next, we introduce a Dual-Path Neck to decouple geometry-consistent and appearance-aware representations, improving appearance completion in weakly observed regions. We further apply Scale Warmup to stabilize early geometry learning and suppress scale drift under ego-pose changes. Finally, we use a hybrid pixel--volume Gaussian decoder to produce a renderable 3D Gaussian scene for novel-view synthesis. Experiments on the nuScenes single-frame benchmark show that VGGD achieves the best overall rendering quality among the compared methods and improves relative geometric consistency.

</details>


### [53] [Embedding Rotation Invariance for Provable Multi-Oriented Scene Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10684)
*Zhibin Ma,Pengwen Dai,Yi Liu,Xugong Qin,Chenyun Yu,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RISTER，一个全旋转不变的端到端场景文本识别网络，通过编码器中旋转等变的局部-全局特征提取网络和解码器中旋转不变的交叉注意力机制，从理论上保证旋转不变性，在标准和多方向基准上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋转感知方法显式估计文本方向，但缺乏理论保证，导致误差累积、计算开销大且强依赖数据。因此需要一种具有理论保证的旋转不变STR框架来解决多方向文本识别问题。

Method: 采用编码器-解码器架构：编码器端提出旋转等变局部-全局提取网络，集成深度等变卷积与自注意力，实现旋转等变特征提取并建模字符间依赖；解码器端理论证明交叉注意力机制的旋转不变性，据此构建旋转不变文本解码器，将视觉特征以旋转不变方式映射为输出文本。

Result: RISTER在标准和多方向基准上均取得SOTA性能，在通用多方向数据集上比第二名模型准确率高出4.0%，且不增加推理计算开销，也不依赖数据驱动的方向校正。

Conclusion: RISTER通过理论保证的旋转不变性设计，解决了现有旋转感知方法误差累积、计算开销大、强依赖数据等问题，为多方向场景文本识别提供了更鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-oriented text is ubiquitous in real-world scenes and remains a major challenge for scene text recognition (STR). Existing rotation-aware methods explicitly estimate text orientation. However, due to the lack of theoretical guarantees, they are prone to error accumulation, increased computational cost, and strong reliance on data. In this work, we incorporate rotation invariance into the STR framework to address these limitations. Specifically, we adopt an encoder-decoder architecture, embedding rotation equivariance in the encoder and rotation invariance in the decoder to construct a fully rotation-invariant network. On the decoder side, we first identify and prove the rotation-invariant property of the cross-attention mechanism and use it to formulate a rotation-invariant text decoder that maps visual features to output text in a rotation-invariant manner. On the encoder side, we propose a rotation-equivariant local-global extraction network that integrates deep equivariant convolutions with self-attention, enabling rotation-equivariant feature extraction while modeling inter-character dependencies and preserving fine-grained visual details. By integrating the encoder and decoder, we obtain an end-to-end Rotation-Invariant Scene Text Recognition network (RISTER). RISTER provides rotation invariance with theoretical guarantees, enhancing robustness on multi-oriented samples without introducing additional inference computation or relying on data-driven orientation correction. Experiments show that RISTER achieves state-of-the-art performance on both standard and multi-oriented benchmarks, surpassing the second-best model by 4.0 percent in accuracy on the general multi-oriented dataset.

</details>


### [54] [MMArt A Multi-Perspective Multimodal Dataset for Visual Art Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10706)
*Shuai Wang,Wangyuan Ding,Yixian Shen,Jia-Hong Huang,Stevan Rudinac,Monika Kackovic,Nachoem Wijnberg,Marcel Worring*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MMArt，一个包含74,234幅WikiArt画作的大型多视角数据集，每幅画作同时带有叙事、形式、情感、历史四种独立标注视角及一个统一的融合标题。通过生成与检索两种互补性分析，证明各视角蕴含独特信息，任何单一视角都无法在所有任务中胜任，从而直接验证了多视角设计理念的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型对艺术的理解停留在表面内容描述层面，缺乏深度的形式分析、有依据的历史诠释和情感刻画。作者认为这一局限不仅是模型问题，更是数据集问题：现有艺术数据集均为单一视角资源，没有任何数据集能同时为同一批艺术作品提供叙事、形式、情感和历史四个维度的标注，因此无法支撑模型进行全面的艺术理解。

Method: 作者构建了MMArt大规模多视角数据集，包含74,234幅WikiArt画作，每幅画配有四种独立标注视角（叙事、形式、情感、历史）及一个整合的统一标题，标注来源包括专用视觉-语言模型和人工标注，并通过互补性质控进行评估。随后设计了两种互补性分析：生成分析（检验不同视角描述对构图风格与情感信息的还原能力）和判别式检索分析（检验各视角在检索任务中的判别力），并辅以留一法（leave-one-out）分析来验证各视角的不可替代性。

Result: 生成分析表明形式分析描述最能保留构图风格，历史描述在重建图像中携带强烈情感信号；判别式检索分析揭示了任务不对称性：叙事描述驱动检索（R@1=44.0%），而形式描述虽然重建能力最强，但在检索尺度上几乎不具判别力（R@1=7.8%）。留一法分析进一步证实历史描述是两个任务中最不可替代的视角。综合结果表明没有任何单一视角能胜任所有任务。

Conclusion: 两项互补性分析共同证明单一视角不足以为所有艺术理解任务提供充分支撑，这直接验证了MMArt多视角数据集设计的必要性。该数据集、代码及相关资源已公开，为后续多视角艺术理解研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models demonstrate impressive general visual understanding, yet their art interpretation remains shallow: they describe surface content but struggle with formal analysis, grounded historical interpretation, or affective characterization. We argue this is not only a model but also a dataset limitation. Existing art datasets are single perspective resources, where no dataset provides narrative, formal, emotional, and historical perspectives simultaneously for the same artworks. We introduce MMArt, a large-scale dataset of 74,234 WikiArt paintings, each annotated with four independently annotated perspectives plus a harmonized unified caption, produced by specialized vision-language models or human annotation and validated through complementary quality evaluations. Two complementarity analyses establish that perspectives encode genuinely distinct information. A generative analysis shows that formal analysis descriptions best preserve compositional style, and historical descriptions carry strong affective signal in reconstructed images. A discriminative retrieval analysis reveals task-asymmetry: narrative descriptions drive retrieval (R@1 = 44.0%), while formal descriptions, strongest for reconstruction, are nearly nondiscriminative at retrieval scale (R@1 = 7.8%). Leave-one-out analysis further confirms that historical descriptions are the least replaceable perspective across both tasks. Together, the two analyses establish that no single perspective suffices for all tasks, directly motivating MMArt multi-perspective design. The dataset, code, and additional information are available at https://shuaiwang97.github.io/MMArt/.

</details>


### [55] [Self-Geometry: GT-Free and Plug-and-Play Test-Time Adaptation for Geometrically Consistent 3D Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10708)
*Seokhyun Youn,Dahyeon Kye,Sung-Ho Bae,Jihyong Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Self-Geometry，一种即插即用的测试时自适应（TTA）框架，通过显式多视图几何约束（利用2D像素对应作为伪真值）直接优化视觉基础模型（VFM），无需每场景优化即可改善其姿态和几何估计的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型（VFM）在单次前向传播中同时预测深度、相机姿态和点图，具有良好的泛化能力，但预训练过程中缺乏显式多视图几何一致性约束（如光束法平差），导致预测结果存在几何不一致性。先前工作依赖模型输出（如点图、特征）的隐式自一致性进行测试时优化，但在预训练VFM高度不准的场景下性能提升有限。

Method: Self-Geometry包含三个核心组件：(1) 几何解耦优化（Geometric Disentanglement Optimization），结合多视图一致性损失与对极一致性损失，并通过梯度解耦防止梯度冲突；(2) 帧角邻居采样器（Frame Angular-Neighbor），基于SO(3)测地距离选择视图对以轻量地施加约束；(3) 轻量测试时自适应（Lightweight TTA），通过LoRA调整VFM参数。

Result: 在六个VFM（VGGT、π³、DA3-Giant/Large/Base/Small）和四个基准（7Scenes、ETH3D、ScanNet++、HiRoom）上，姿态估计和几何估计均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 显式多视图几何约束比隐式自一致性更能有效提升VFM在测试时的姿态与几何估计性能，且作为即插即用模块可适用于多种VFM架构。

Abstract: Recent Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) predict depth, camera pose, and pointmap in a single forward pass without per-scene optimization, achieving strong generalization. However, enforcing explicit multi-view geometric consistency, e.g., through bundle adjustment, is computationally costly and is thus not imposed during VFM pretraining, so such inconsistency can arise. To address this, implicit self-consistency derived from model outputs (e.g., pointmaps, features), though enforced at test-time in prior work, delivers inherently limited performance gain, especially on scenes where the pretrained VFM is highly inaccurate. In contrast to this implicit signal, we propose Self-Geometry, a plug-and-play test-time adaptation pipeline that directly imposes explicit multi-view geometric constraints using 2D pixel correspondences as pseudo ground-truth. Our proposed Self-Geometry consists of Geometric Disentanglement Optimization, which combines Multi-View Consistency and Epipolar Consistency losses with Gradient Disentanglement to prevent gradient conflict; Frame Angular-Neighbor, a view sampler based on SO(3) geodesic distances for lightly imposing these constraints; and Lightweight TTA, which adapts VFMs via LoRA. Our method achieves consistent improvements in both pose and geometry estimation across six VFMs (VGGT, $π^3$, DA3-Giant/Large/Base/Small) and four benchmarks (7Scenes, ETH3D, ScanNet++, HiRoom).

</details>


### [56] [Compact Feed-Forward 3D Gaussians via Saliency-Guided Primitive Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10712)
*Tim-Felix Fassch,Jochen Kall,Cyrill Stachniss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结构感知的合并管线，将前馈3D高斯泼溅方法产生的逐像素高斯原语合并为紧凑、内容自适应的稀疏高斯集，仅用1/20的高斯数量即可保持视觉质量，同时支持推理时的质量-效率灵活权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式3D高斯泼溅变体虽然能从稀疏视图快速重建，但生成的逐像素原语高度冗余，导致表示效率低下。现有方法在减少原语数量时往往以牺牲质量或鲁棒性为代价，缺乏一种能保持质量同时大幅压缩表示的后处理方案。

Method: 通过自适应超像素分割（由显著性图引导，纹理区域细分割、均匀区域粗分割）将空间相干且外观相似的高斯分组为变大小簇；利用学习编码器将每个簇压缩为紧凑潜在表示；基于几何重叠和特征相似性通过学习合并器匹配并整合跨视图的表示；最后通过细节层次解码器以可控分辨率生成最终高斯，实现推理时的质量-效率权衡。该管线作为与骨干无关的后处理模块，可应用于任何前馈方法。

Result: 仅用逐像素方法1/20的高斯数量即可在视觉质量上大幅保留，比此前旨在减少原语数量的方法获得更好且更鲁棒的质量，同时提供高度紧凑的表示并能高效渲染。

Conclusion: 该合并管线是一种骨干无关的后处理方法，能够充分利用现有前馈方法的优势，在不牺牲质量的前提下显著压缩高斯表示，为3D场景重建提供灵活高效的紧凑表示方案。

Abstract: 3D scene reconstruction, modeling, and rendering are highly relevant for numerous tasks, and 3D Gaussian splatting has become a standard choice in this context. Its feed-forward variants provide fast reconstruction from sparse input views but often produce per-pixel primitives, leading to highly redundant and thus inefficient representations. We present a structure-aware merging pipeline that takes per-pixel primitives from any feed-forward method and consolidates them into a compact, content-adaptive Gaussian set while largely retaining visual quality at just $\frac{1}{20}^\text{th}$ of the Gaussians of a per-pixel method. We group spatially coherent Gaussians of similar appearance into variable-size clusters via adaptive superpixel segmentation guided by a saliency map, which allocates fine segments to textured regions and coarse segments to homogeneous areas. We compress each cluster into a compact latent representation through a learned encoder, then match and consolidate representations across views based on geometric overlap and feature similarity via a learned merger. A level-of-detail decoder then produces the final Gaussians at a controllable resolution, enabling a flexible quality-efficiency trade-off at inference. As a post-processing module, the pipeline is backbone-agnostic, leveraging the strengths of existing feed-forward methods. This leads to better and more robust quality than achieved by previous approaches that target a reduction in primitive count, while providing a highly compact representation, that can be rendered efficiently.

</details>


### [57] [Grid-Preserving Knowledge Distillation: Transferring Convolutional Inductive Bias to Vision Transformers under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10723)
*Junyong Choi,Cheolhyeon Park,Jaehoon Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: iBKD是一种针对数据稀缺场景下ViT训练的蒸馏框架，通过保留卷积归纳偏置的空间网格结构沿完整迁移路径传递，使未修改的ViT在所有训练后无需任何推理开销即可超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉Transformer在训练数据稀缺时性能不如卷积网络。虽然从CNN教师蒸馏卷积归纳偏置是有效的解决方案，但通用的特征蒸馏方法在此场景下迁移效果甚微，因为其池化、展平和logit空间投影等操作丢弃了编码局部性和平移等变性的空间网格结构。与卷积学生不同，ViT无法自行重建这种结构。

Method: 提出iBKD蒸馏框架，核心模块为Inductive Bias Attention Module（IBAM）。该模块将每个学生层通过学习权重聚合到教师网格上，用通道注意力和可变形空间注意力增强结构线索，并通过卷积交叉注意力在网格与网格之间（而非token集合之间）注入这些线索。模块仅在训练期间使用。

Result: 在7个Transformer骨干和6个数据稀缺基准上，iBKD超越了局部性引导方法和通用知识蒸馏基线，且优势随训练数据减少而扩大。

Conclusion: 通过在整个迁移路径上保持空间网格结构，iBKD能有效地将卷积归纳偏置蒸馏给ViT学生，且部署模型为未修改的ViT，无推理开销，在数据稀缺场景下表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Vision Transformers underperform convolutional networks when training data is scarce, and distilling convolutional inductive biases from a CNN teacher is an effective remedy that leaves the deployed model unchanged. General-purpose feature distillation, however, transfers little in this setting. The pooling, flattening, and logit-space projections it inherits from CNN to CNN pipelines discard the spatial grid in which locality and translation equivariance are encoded, and unlike a convolutional student, a ViT cannot rebuild that structure on its own. In this paper, we propose iBKD, a distillation framework that preserves the grid along the entire transfer path. Its core module, the Inductive Bias Attention Module, aggregates every student layer onto the teacher grid with learned weights, sharpens structural cues with channel and deformable spatial attention, and injects them through convolutional cross-attention that operates between grids rather than between token sets. The module is used only during training, so the deployed model is an unmodified ViT with no inference overhead. Across seven Transformer backbones and six data-scarce benchmarks, iBKD outperforms both locality-guidance methods and general knowledge distillation baselines, and its margin widens as training data shrinks.

</details>


### [58] [InterPruner: Interactive Structured Pruning via Taylor-Implicit Criterion and Language-Prior Modulator for Multimodal Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10724)
*Qi Ming,Zihan Yang,Shaoguang Huang,Si Sun,Hanqing Zhang,Nanqing Liu,Jiahui Lv,Juan Fang,Aleksandra Pizurica*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterPruner是首个针对RGB-红外目标检测器的交互式结构化通道剪枝框架，通过Taylor-Implicit准则、模态交互冗余分析器和场景先验通道锚定三个模块，在剪枝50%通道的情况下甚至在FLIR数据集上实现了0.6%的mAP提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态目标检测（尤其是RGB-红外范式）中的并行特征提取器在提供丰富多模态信息的同时引入了大量通道冗余和计算开销。现有剪枝方法仅针对单模态骨干网络设计，忽略了跨模态交互和动态场景级冗余，无法有效处理多模态检测器的冗余问题。

Method: InterPruner框架包含三个核心组件：1) 泰勒隐式准则（TIC），利用高阶泰勒展开和隐函数定理量化通道重要性；2) 模态交互冗余分析器（MIRA），通过互补偿性评估识别冗余通道；3) 场景先验通道锚定（SPCA），利用语言先验作为语义锚点衡量通道-场景相关性，实现动态通道重要性估计。

Result: 在RGB-红外目标检测数据集上的广泛实验表明，InterPruner在保持高性能的同时实现剪枝，在FLIR数据集上剪枝50%通道时甚至取得了0.6%的mAP提升。

Conclusion: InterPruner首次探索了RGB-红外检测的跨模态通道剪枝问题，证明了交互式剪枝框架能够有效降低多模态检测器的冗余，同时保持甚至提升检测性能，为未来多模态模型压缩研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Multimodal object detection proves effective in remote sensing, especially the RGB-Infrared paradigm. The parallel feature extractors provide rich multimodal information for robust detection, yet introduce substantial channel redundancy and computational overhead. Existing pruning methods can reduce channel redundancy, but they are designed for unimodal backbones, overlooking cross-modal interactions and dynamic scene-wise redundancy. In this paper, we propose InterPruner, the first interactive structured channel pruning framework for RGB-infrared object detectors. Specifically, we first derive a Taylor-Implicit Criterion(TIC) to quantify channel importance via high-order Taylor expansion and the implicit function theorem. Then, a Modality Interaction Redundancy Analyzer (MIRA) identifies redundant channels via mutual compensability assessment. Finally, a Scene-Prior Channel Anchor (SPCA) uses language priors as semantic anchors to measure channel-scene relevance for dynamic channel importance estimation. Cross-modality channel pruning for RGB-Infrared detection is yet unexplored. Extensive experiments on RGB-infrared object detection dataset demonstrate that InterPruner maintains high performance with negligible degradation. Specifically, it even achieves a 0.6% mAP increase on the FLIR dataset when pruning 50% of the channels. Code will be available on GitHub to facilitate future work.

</details>


### [59] [Rethinking LLM Verification: Evidence Structure, Uncertainty, and Selective Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10725)
*Uma Ranjan,Kunal Tilaganji,Aditya Koul,Anurag Mahipal,Dashpreet Singh,Hriday Rana,Manan Jain,Sidharth Gupta,Ajo Babu George,Vineeth Balasubramanian,Nagarajan Natarajan,Amit Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段框架用于医疗假设验证中的多项选择题设置，通过仅在模型弃权时进行有针对性的本体论接地（ontology grounding）来管理覆盖率-准确率权衡。实验表明弃权并非随机而是反映真实不确定性，在GPT-5.5和DeepSeek-R1上分别将问题级准确率提升9.6个百分点、假设级准确率提升4.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在医疗应用中往往依赖捷径而非系统性推理，带来安全隐患。允许模型在不确定时弃权可以提高可靠性，但引入了覆盖率与准确率之间的权衡问题。作者希望管理这种权衡，并利用弃权信号来指导选择性推理改进。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：第一阶段模型先进行假设验证，检测到不确定性时选择弃权；第二阶段仅对弃权的样本应用有针对性的本体论接地（ontology grounding）进行推理精化，从而在不构建显式知识图谱的情况下达到类似图谱级性能。实验在MedReason和MedQA数据集上进行，使用GPT-5.5和DeepSeek-R1两个前沿模型。

Result: 框架在问题级准确率上从82.9%提升至92.5%（提高9.6个百分点），假设级准确率从92.0%提升至96.2%（提高4.2个百分点）。弃权预测与更低的置信度相关联，证明弃权反映了真实的不确定性。

Conclusion: 弃权可以被重新用作选择性推理精化的控制信号，以有目标的方式应用本体论接地，从而在无需显式知识图谱构建的情况下达到知识图谱级别的性能，有效管理覆盖率-准确率权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often rely on shortcuts rather than systematic reasoning, raising safety concerns in medical applications. Allowing models to abstain when uncertain improves reliability but introduces a coverage accuracy tradeoff. We propose a two-stage framework for medical hypothesis verification in multiple-choice settings that manages this tradeoff through targeted ontology grounding, applied only when the model abstains. We show that abstention is not random but reflects genuine uncertainty, with abstained predictions associated with lower confidence. Across two frontier models (GPT-5.5, accessed via the Azure OpenAI API, and DeepSeek-R1), the proposed framework improves question-level accuracy by 9.6 percentage points (82.9% to 92.5%) and hypothesis-level accuracy by 4.2 percentage points (92.0% to 96.2%). Our experiments conducted on MedReason and MedQA show that abstention can be repurposed as a control signal for selective reasoning refinement, achieving knowledge-graph-level performance without explicit knowledge graph construction.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Pixels: From Video Priors to 4D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10744)
*Zihao Liu,Xiaolong Shen,Zhenglin Zhou,Ruijie Quan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent-to-4D，一种直接从视频扩散模型的去噪隐空间生成4D场景的方法，绕过RGB重建，利用共享VAE的视频模型隐变量作为与预训练4D解码器交互的通用接口，仅用约1K条重建视频训练，单个检查点即可跨多个同VAE族的视频扩散变换器迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D生成方法要么先用视频模型生成RGB视频再用独立4D模型重建，导致分布不匹配和误差传播；要么将4D预测绑定到特定视频生成器，更换生成器或条件形式时需重新训练。作者希望找到一种可复用的接口，使隐空间直接用于显式4D预测，避免上述两类问题。

Method: 提出直接隐编码到4D（latent-to-4D）生成范式。核心思想是利用共享VAE的视频模型的去噪最终隐变量作为接口，将其与预训练4D解码器的token网格对齐，并通过帧级与全局时空注意力进行精炼。训练数据仅需约1K条现有重建视频剪辑。

Result: 在Text4D-200和I4D-200基准上，Latent-to-4D相较匹配的同隐空间Wan+4RC级联方法，在投影式DINO-F1指标上分别提升2.88-3.45和5.81个点；人工评测中在几何质量、时间稳定性和整体质量方面均更受偏好。单个检查点可跨多个同VAE族的视频扩散变换器直接迁移，无需重训练。

Conclusion: 共享VAE的视频模型去噪隐空间可以作为通用的4D预测接口，Latent-to-4D证明了这一范式的有效性，以更少的数据和单一检查点实现跨生成器迁移，性能超越传统RGB级联方法。

Abstract: 4D generation synthesizes dynamic 3D scenes from conditions such as text or images. Existing methods either reconstruct generated RGB videos with a separate 4D model or adapt a particular video generator to predict geometry directly. The former suffers from distribution mismatch and error propagation, whereas the latter ties 4D prediction to a specific generator and may require retraining when the generator or conditioning regime changes. We ask whether the final denoised latents of video models that share a variational autoencoder (VAE) can instead provide a reusable interface to explicit 4D prediction. Building on this insight, we introduce direct latent-to-4D generation and instantiate it as Latent-to-4D, which bypasses RGB by aligning a video latent with the token grid of a pretrained 4D decoder and refining it through frame-wise and global spatiotemporal attention. Trained on roughly 1K existing reconstruction clips, a single checkpoint transfers unchanged across multiple video diffusion transformers within the same VAE family. On Text4D-200 and I4D-200, Latent-to-4D surpasses matched same-latent Wan+4RC cascades in projection-based DINO-F1 by 2.88--3.45 and 5.81 points, respectively, while also being preferred by human raters for geometry, temporal stability, and overall quality.

</details>


### [61] [Where To Look? : Causal Tracing of Vision Encoders in VLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10758)
*Naren Kumar S,Tirth Bhatt,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过因果追踪方法研究视觉语言模型中视觉信息对答案的驱动机制，发现高因果性的视觉token往往位于目标区域之外，且在不同模型和干扰设置下呈现一致的规律，表明强多模态性能并不等同于空间局部化的因果表示，并进一步揭示了外观线索与视觉结构理解之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型能够以惊人的精度描述图像，但一个更基本的问题仍未得到解答：究竟是哪些视觉信息驱动了模型的回答？这一根本性问题尚缺乏系统的因果分析。

Method: 采用因果追踪（causal tracing）方法，系统地识别对模型答案贡献最大的视觉token，并在多种视觉语言模型及多种干扰（corruption）设置下进行跨模型、跨设置的对比分析；同时设计实验移除外观线索（appearance cues），考察模型是否仍能保留视觉结构信息。

Result: 研究发现高因果性的视觉token经常位于目标区域之外；这一模式在更大型的视觉语言模型和不同干扰设置下保持一致；当外观线索被移除时，视觉线索被模型利用来理解视觉结构；实验揭示了"看到"、"使用"与"推理"视觉结构之间的差距。

Conclusion: 强多模态性能并不必然意味着空间局部化的因果表示。本文提供了一个因果框架，用于研究现代视觉语言模型中视觉信息如何被转换、保留并最终被使用，揭示了视觉感知、视觉利用与视觉结构化推理之间的本质鸿沟。

Abstract: Vision-language models can describe an image with remarkable accuracy, yet a more fundamental question remains unanswered: what visual information actually drives their answers? In this work, we investigate this question through causal tracing, and we observe that highly causal vision tokens often lie outside the target region. Extending the analysis to larger vision-language models reveals a similar pattern across models and corruption settings, suggesting that strong multimodal performance does not necessarily imply spatially localized causal representations. We further investigate: can these models preserve visual structure when appearance cues are removed? and find that visual cues are exploited to understand visual structures. Together, our experiments expose a gap between seeing, using, and reasoning over visual structure, and provide a causal framework for studying how visual information is transformed, preserved, and ultimately used by modern vision-language models.

</details>


### [62] [FADE: From Passive Verification to Active Discovery in Counterfactual Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10764)
*Fufangchen Zhao,Jinhu Fu,Jiachen Lei,Jiahong Wu,Xiangxiang Chu,Danfeng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FADE框架，一种用于反事实视频理解（counterfactual video understanding）的训练方法。FADE采用"证据优先、两阶段训练"范式：(1) 证据内化的监督微调使模型基于关键视觉异常进行预测；(2) 渐隐锚点强化学习逐步移除文本引导，迫使模型独立发现并解释证据。同时提出一个管道将现有MCQ数据集转换为对齐的MCQ、开放问答和描述任务。实验表明，基于Qwen3-VL-8B的FADE在DualityVidQA-test和IPV-Bench上达到三个任务的SOTA严格配对分数，超越GPT-5.6，且在从约束MCQ转向无约束OQA和描述任务时表现出显著鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实视频理解的MCQ基准存在关键缺陷：问题和候选选项会无意中泄露目标事件，将核心挑战从主动发现退化为文本引导的验证。这使得模型的真实反事实发现与解释能力被高估。作者旨在构建一个训练框架，使模型能够真正主动发现反事实视频中的关键证据并解释其成因，而非依赖文本线索进行匹配。

Method: FADE采用两阶段训练范式。第一阶段为证据内化的监督微调（evidence-internalized SFT），将模型预测锚定于决定性视觉异常。第二阶段使用渐隐锚点强化学习（fading-anchor reinforcement learning），逐步移除训练过程中的文本引导信号，迫使模型自主发现并解释反事实证据。此外，作者设计了一个无需额外数据整理的管道，可将现有MCQ数据集转换为三种对齐的任务格式：MCQ、开放问答（OQA）和标题描述（captioning），以全面评估反事实发现能力。

Result: 以Qwen3-VL-8B为基线，FADE在DualityVidQA-test和IPV-Bench三个任务上均获得SOTA的严格配对分数，性能超越GPT-5.6。从MCQ过渡到OQA和描述任务时，FADE的性能保留率分别为90.4%和67.4%，远高于GPT-5.6的48.1%和30.7%，展示了在无约束条件下的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FADE是一个简单而有效的反事实视频理解训练框架，通过证据内化与渐隐锚点策略，使模型在无文本引导下仍能保持强大的反事实发现和解释能力。作者希望该框架能为未来无约束反事实视频理解研究提供一个坚实的基线。

Abstract: Counterfactual video understanding evaluates whether models grasp physical and commonsense regularities. However, existing multiple-choice question (MCQ) benchmarks inadvertently leak target events through their questions and candidate options. This reduces the core challenge from active discovery to text-guided verification. In this paper, we present FADE, an effective training framework for counterfactual discovery and explanation. Our method is built on an evidence-first, two-stage training paradigm. First, evidence-internalized supervised fine-tuning grounds the model's predictions in decisive visual anomalies. Second, we apply a fading-anchor reinforcement learning strategy that progressively removes textual guidance, compelling the model to independently discover and explain evidence. To rigorously evaluate this capability, we also introduce an effective pipeline that converts existing MCQ datasets into aligned MCQ, open-ended question answering (OQA), and captioning tasks without requiring additional data curation. Our simple approach yields strong results. Using Qwen3-VL-8B as the baseline, FADE achieves state-of-the-art strict paired scores across all three tasks on DualityVidQA-test and IPV-Bench, outperforming GPT-5.6. In specific, when transitioning from constrained MCQs to unconstrained OQA and captioning, our model demonstrates remarkable robustness. Its performance retention is 90.4% and 67.4% on DualityVidQA-test-substantially higher than the 48.1% and 30.7% retained by GPT-5.6. We hope this simple framework can serve as a solid baseline for future research in unconstrained counterfactual video understanding.

</details>


### [63] [MVTrack: Ultrafast Appearance-Free Moving Object Tracking from Compressed Bitstreams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10790)
*Iñaki Erregue,Kamal Nasrollahi,Sergio Escalera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVTrack是一种直接在H.264码流上运行的超快速运动物体追踪器，利用运动向量场检测（MVDet）和轻量级运动学关联模块（MVLink）实现追踪，在VIRAT数据集上相比YOLO26n参数量减少60倍、FLOPs减少40倍、CPU延迟降低8.6倍，证明仅凭压缩视频数据即可实现准确的监控追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频追踪器在大规模部署时受限于基于RGB的物体检测器的计算成本，像素重建和RGB检测的链路严重限制了追踪系统的可扩展性。

Method: MVTrack包含两个核心组件：MVDet，一种针对运动向量场的轻量级检测器；以及MVLink，一个极简的运动学关联模块。整个系统直接操作H.264码流中的运动向量数据，绕过了像素重构步骤。

Result: 在VIRAT数据集上，MVTrack在所有指标上优于YOLO26n，同时参数量减少60倍、FLOPs减少40倍、CPU延迟降低8.6倍。

Conclusion: 压缩视频数据（如运动向量）本身已足够支持准确且可扩展的监控追踪任务，无需进行像素重建，为大规模视频追踪部署提供了一种高效方案。

Abstract: Deploying modern video trackers at scale is bottlenecked by the computational cost of RGB-based object detectors. To this end, we present MVTrack, an ultrafast tracker for moving objects that operates directly on H.264 bitstreams. MVTrack combines MVDet, a lightweight detector for motion vector fields, with MVLink, a minimalist kinematic association module. On VIRAT, MVTrack outperforms YOLO26n while using 60$\times$ fewer parameters, requiring 40$\times$ fewer FLOPs, and reducing CPU latency by 8.6$\times$. These results demonstrate that compressed video data alone can enable accurate and scalable surveillance tracking, thereby bypassing the need for pixel reconstruction.

</details>


### [64] [E$^3$mo-Bench: A Scalable Benchmark for Multimodal Evoked and Expressed Emotion Understanding via Bayesian Pairwise Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10796)
*Lancheng Gao,Ziheng Jia,Shengyan Li,Zixuan Xing,Jiarui Wang,Huiyu Duan,Xiongkuo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出E³mo-Bench，一个包含12,314个问答对、覆盖2,524个视频的可扩展多模态情感理解基准，通过3个互补任务评估引发表情情感与表达情感的理解，并提出了贝叶斯配对对齐方法高效扩展VAD维度标注，以及E³mo-Score这一免训练智能体聚合多模型判断来提升VAD估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立地考察引发表情情感（evoked emotions）和表达情感（expressed emotions），或者局限于粗粒度、不完整的情感表征，缺乏能够综合评估这两类情感且具有细粒度情感描述的基准。

Method: 构建E³mo-Bench基准，包含3个互补任务（情感感知、开放词汇识别、效价-唤醒-支配VAD评估）；提出贝叶斯配对对齐（Bayesian Pairwise Alignment）方法，将稀疏低负担的成对判断聚合成锚定参考的VAD估计；开发E³mo-Score，一个免训练的智能体，聚合五模型委员会（five-model committee）的互补判断来改进VAD估计。

Result: 大量实验验证了所提出框架的有效性，并揭示了引发表情情感与表达情感范式之间显著的性能偏斜（performance skew），同时发现MLLMs在细粒度识别和维度评估方面仍存在持续性缺陷。

Conclusion: 这些发现为推进多模态情感智能指明了清晰的方向，表明需要在细粒度情感识别和维度化评估方面进一步提升多模态大语言模型的能力。

Abstract: Understanding both expressed and evoked emotions is critical for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to achieve comprehensive affect-aware interactions. However, existing benchmarks typically examine expressed and evoked emotions in isolation or are constrained to coarse-grained and incomplete affective characterizations. To bridge this gap, we introduce E$^3$mo-Bench, a scalable benchmark comprising $12{,}314$ question-answer pairs across $2{,}524$ videos with predefined affective perspectives. It evaluates evoked and expressed emotion understanding via $3$ complementary tasks: emotion perception, open-vocabulary recognition, and valence-arousal-dominance (VAD) assessment. To efficiently scale reliable continuous annotations, we propose Bayesian Pairwise Alignment, which aggregates sparse, low-burden pairwise judgments into anchor-referenced VAD estimates. Furthermore, we develop E$^3$mo-Score, a training-free agent that aggregates complementary judgments from a five-model committee to improve VAD estimation. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of our framework and expose a pronounced performance skew between evoked and expressed emotion paradigms. These findings, coupled with MLLMs' persistent deficits in fine-grained recognition and dimensional assessment, chart a clear course for advancing multimodal emotional intelligence.

</details>


### [65] [Beyond Fixed Luminance: Towards Panchromatic and Orthochromatic Image Colorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10798)
*Swarnim Maheshwari,Syed Imam Ali,Vineeth N. Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与亮度无关（luminance-agnostic）的图像着色框架，将着色问题重新定义为基于基础图像编辑模型的全RGB图像编辑任务，并引入混合灰度目标函数，使模型同时适应标准亮度和历史正色（orthochromatic）灰度形成条件，在标准灰度输入上保持竞争力，在正色输入上显著更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像着色系统大多在Lab空间中操作，通过保留输入亮度通道并预测色度通道来完成着色。这种固定亮度设计限制了亮度变化能力，且当灰度形成方式偏离自然图像亮度（如历史正色摄影中胶片对红光不敏感导致灰度形成方式不同）时，方法会变得不可靠。因此需要一种对灰度形成方式更鲁棒的着色框架。

Method: 提出亮度无关的着色框架，将着色任务公式化为使用基础图像编辑模型进行的完整RGB图像编辑问题。引入混合灰度目标（mixed grayscale objective），在训练中同时使用标准亮度灰度化和红不敏感的灰度形成（模拟正色摄影条件）两种灰度生成方式训练模型，以弥合现代全色（panchromatic）与历史正色（orthochromatic）条件之间的差距。

Result: 在COCO、ImageNet以及多实例基准上进行实验，结果表明所提方法在标准灰度输入上与前人方法竞争力相当，在正色灰度输入上显著更加鲁棒；定性比较和人类研究显示该方法产生的可见颜色伪影更少。

Conclusion: 将着色建模为全RGB图像编辑任务并采用混合灰度训练目标，可以有效克服固定亮度设计带来的局限，使着色系统对各种灰度形成方式（包括历史正色摄影）具有更强的适应性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Most image colorization systems operate in $Lab$ space by predicting chroma ($ab$) while preserving an input-derived luminance channel ($L$). While effective on standard benchmarks, this fixed-luminance design restricts brightness changes and becomes unreliable when grayscale formation deviates from natural-image luminance, as in historical orthochromatic photography. We propose a luminance-agnostic colorization framework that formulates colorization as full-RGB image editing using a foundation image-editing model. To bridge modern panchromatic and historical orthochromatic conditions, we introduce a mixed grayscale objective that trains the model under both standard luminance grayscale and a red-insensitive grayscale formation. Experiments on COCO, ImageNet, and a multi-instance benchmark show that our method is competitive on standard grayscale inputs and substantially more robust under orthochromatic inputs, with qualitative comparisons and a human study indicating fewer visible color artifacts.

</details>


### [66] [Evaluating Semantic and Spatial Guidance for Foundation Model Segmentation of Small-Scale PV in Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10801)
*Roni Blushtein-Livnon,Tal Svoray,Osher Rafaeli,Michael Dorman,Itay Fischhendler,Havazelet Yahel,Emir Galilee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了视觉-语言基础模型SAM3在不同提示策略（文本、几何、混合）下对偏远离网地区小型光伏（PV）系统的遥感影像分割性能，发现提示策略是决定模型行为的主导因素：空间引导显著优于文本提示，混合提示达到最高精度和稳定性，且仅需数百个标注样本即可获得大部分性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离网地区的光伏时空数据对理解能源采用过程至关重要，但目前缺乏此类数据。虽然遥感影像自动分割有望解决这一问题，但住宅光伏系统因尺寸小、分布稀疏而面临严重的目标-背景类别不平衡。视觉-语言基础模型提供了基于提示的数据高效范式，但不同类型的提示（语义vs空间）对分割效果的相对贡献尚不明确。

Method: 在大型离网农村地区的多时相航空影像上，系统比较了SAM3在文本提示、几何提示和混合提示三种策略下的光伏分割性能，涵盖不同监督水平、训练策略、空间分辨率和成像条件下的评估，并在另外三个数据集上进行验证。

Result: 提示策略是控制模型行为的主导因素。文本提示表现最差且对监督和成像条件最敏感；空间引导显著提升分割精度和鲁棒性；混合提示达到最高精度和稳定性，表明语义与空间信息互补。仅需数百个标注样本即可获得大部分性能提升，展现了强数据效率。迁移学习总体影响有限，仅在有限监督下对文本提示有适度改善。

Conclusion: 提示策略是SAM3适应、鲁棒性和泛化能力的关键决定因素，突出了可提示基础模型在数据受限的离网地区进行可扩展光伏制图的潜力。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal PV data are essential for understanding adoption processes in off-grid regions, yet such data remain largely unavailable. Automated segmentation of remote sensing (RS) imagery offers a promising solution; yet, residential PV systems remain challenging targets because of their small size and sparse distribution, resulting in severe target-background imbalance. Vision-language foundation models (FMs) provide a data-efficient paradigm through prompt-based semantic and spatial guidance, but the relative contribution of different prompt types remains unclear. We systematically evaluate SAM3 for small-scale PV segmentation in RS imagery by comparing textual, geometric, and hybrid prompting, under varying supervision levels, training strategies, spatial resolutions, and imaging conditions. Multi-temporal aerial imagery from a large off-grid rural region serves as a study site, with findings validated across three additional datasets. Prompting strategy emerged as the dominant factor governing model behavior. Textual prompting consistently produced the lowest performance and showed the greatest sensitivity to supervision and imaging conditions. In contrast, spatial guidance substantially improved both segmentation accuracy and robustness. Hybrid prompting achieved the highest accuracy and stability, indicating that semantic and spatial guidance provide complementary information. Most performance gains were achieved with only a few hundred annotated samples, demonstrating strong data efficiency. Transfer learning had limited overall impact, with only modest improvements observed for textual prompting under limited supervision. Overall, our findings establish prompting strategy as a key determinant of SAM3 adaptation, robustness, and generalization, highlighting the potential of promptable FMs for scalable PV mapping in data-constrained off-grid regions.

</details>


### [67] [BPG: Balancing Plasticity and Generalization for Domain Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10804)
*Qiang Wang,Songlin Dong,Shaokun Wang,Jizhou Han,Xiang Song,Chenhao Ding,Yuhang He,Yihong Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BPG统一框架解决域增量学习（DIL）中参数隔离范式的两大挑战：通过BPG-Adapter根据域特征可分离性动态确定适配器隐藏维度，以及BPG-Inference在测试时集成多个域专用模型的软混合策略。在DomainNet、CDDB、CORe50上达到SOTA平均精度，遗忘率低至0.22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数隔离范式的DIL方法通常对每个新域采用统一的适配器结构（"一刀切"），导致要么学习能力不足，要么参数冗余浪费。此外，测试时域ID判定错误也会影响性能。

Method: BPG包含两个互补组件：(1) BPG-Adapter——基于域特定的特征可分离性动态确定每个域适配器的隐藏维度；(2) BPG-Inference——在测试阶段采用软域混合策略，整合多个域专用模型，缓解域ID错选问题。

Result: 在DomainNet、CDDB和CORe50三个基准上，BPG一致优于均匀适配器方法和硬域选择策略，达到SOTA平均精度，在DomainNet上将遗忘率降至0.22%。

Conclusion: BPG通过动态适配器容量分配和软域混合推理，有效解决了DIL中容量自适应和域选择鲁棒性两大问题，为参数隔离范式提供了更优的统一框架。

Abstract: Deep neural networks excel in various tasks but struggle to generalize across evolving data distributions, leading to significant performance degradation under domain shifts. Domain incremental learning (DIL) addresses this challenge by enabling models to continuously adapt while retaining prior knowledge. Among existing DIL approaches, the parameter-isolation paradigm achieves state-of-the-art performance. However, these methods often adopt a one-size-fits-all approach to adapt to new domains, resulting in either insufficient learning capacity or redundant parameters. In this work, we propose BPG, a unified framework that addresses both challenges through two complementary components: BPG-Adapter, which dynamically determines each domain's adapter hidden dimension based on domain-specific feature separability, and BPG-Inference, a soft domain mixture strategy that integrates multiple domain-specific models at test time, mitigating domain ID misselection. Experimental results on DomainNet, CDDB, and CORe50 demonstrate that BPG consistently outperforms uniform adapter-based approaches and hard domain selection strategies, achieving state-of-the-art average accuracy while reducing forgetting to as low as 0.22% on DomainNet.

</details>


### [68] [Fast and Memory-Efficient Wavelet Convolutions via I/O-Aware Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10805)
*Amit Aflalo,Shahaf E. Finder,Roy Amoyal,Eran Treister,Oren Freifeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对WTConv（小波卷积）参考实现中严重的HBM内存带宽瓶颈问题，建立了I/O模型分析其瓶颈成因，并提出三种代数重构方法（片上重组Haar分析蝶形、将多级合成级联折叠为单次闭式变换、将可学习的通道缩放烧结进卷积权重），实现了I/O感知的融合实现，将建模的HBM流量降低约2.55倍，训练速度最多提升4.35倍，峰值内存使用减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WTConv作为标准卷积的替代方案，能指数级扩展感受野但参数保持线性增长，然而其参考实现因过量的HBM数据搬运而严重受限于内存带宽，导致实际运行速度甚至慢于其所替代的深度卷积，实用性受限。

Method: 首先建立WTConv的I/O模型以刻画内存瓶颈；然后基于该模型提出三种代数重构：1) 将廉价的Haar分析蝶形操作在片上重算，避免数据往返HBM；2) 将多级合成级联折叠为单个按输出坐标位索引的闭式变换通路；3) 将可学习的逐通道缩放因子折入卷积权重中。三者结合形成I/O感知的融合实现，大幅降低HBM流量。

Result: 在WTConvNeXt配置下跨分解层级和多种张量形状进行评估，该重构将建模的HBM流量降低约2.55倍，相对参考实现训练速度最高提升4.35倍，峰值内存占用约减半，且算术运算量保持相当。

Conclusion: 该重构在完全保留WTConv优势的同时显著降低了执行时间和内存占用，消除了此前限制其实际效率的系统开销，使WTConv在实践中的可行性大幅提升。

Abstract: Wavelet convolution (WTConv) has emerged as an increasingly popular drop-in replacement for standard convolutions, expanding a network's receptive field exponentially with the number of decomposition levels while keeping the parameter count linear. However, its reference implementation is severely memory-bound due to excessive data movement through high-bandwidth memory (HBM). We develop an I/O model of WTConv to characterize this bottleneck and use it to guide three algebraic reformulations: (1) recomputing the inexpensive Haar analysis butterfly on chip, (2) collapsing the multi-level synthesis cascade into a single closed-form pass indexed by output-coordinate bits, and (3) folding learned per-channel scales into the convolution weights. Together, these reformulations enable an I/O-aware fused implementation that substantially reduces HBM traffic. We evaluate the WTConvNeXt configuration across decomposition levels and a broad range of tensor shapes. Despite performing comparable arithmetic, the reference WTConv is substantially slower than the depthwise convolution it replaces. Our reformulation reduces modeled HBM traffic by approximately $2.55\times$, yielding up to a $4.35\times$ training speedup over the reference while roughly halving peak memory usage. Thus, our reformulation preserves the benefits of WTConv while substantially reducing its execution time and memory footprint, removing the systems overhead that previously limited its practical efficiency.

</details>


### [69] [Modelling Geographic Atrophy Progression using Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10807)
*Simone Sarrocco,Paul Friedrich,Florentin Bieder,Christina Bornberg,Philippe Valmaggia,Peter Maloca,Philippe Cattin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出使用隐式神经表征（INRs）对年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）晚期干性阶段的地理萎缩（GA）进展进行个体层面建模，在低数据量环境下同时生成过去和未来的FAF图像及GA分割结果，并在病灶面积MAE和DICE分数上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚期AMD（干性）以不可逆的地理萎缩区为特征，但目前对晚期AMD进展的理解有限，因为其进展高度个体化。现有的纵向FAF图像采集主要在图像层面评估病灶增长，缺乏对个体层面疾病演变的精准建模能力。

Method: 采用隐式神经表征（INRs）在个体层面建模GA进展，在低数据设置下同时生成FAF图像和GA分割结果，覆盖过去和未来的时间点。

Result: 在与对比模型的比较中，该方法在不同场景下均达到具有竞争力的分割质量，GA病灶面积获得最低的平均绝对误差（MAE），DICE分数最高，且不牺牲FAF图像质量。

Conclusion: INRs能够有效建模晚期AMD中GA的个体化进展，在低数据环境下同时提供准确的FAF图像生成和GA分割，为理解疾病演变和临床监测提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Age-related Macular Degeneration (AMD) is the major cause of blindness in the Western world. Its late dry phase is characterised by irreversible atrophic areas, namely Geographic Atrophy (GA). Longitudinal Fundus Autofluorescence (FAF) image acquisitions are currently the main tool for assessing lesion growth over time at the image level. However, due to its highly individualised progression, the evolution of late AMD remains poorly understood. In this work, we propose using Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) to model GA progression at the individual level in a low-data setting. Our approach generates both FAF and GA segmentation at both past and future time points. Among the comparison models, our method achieves competitive segmentation quality across different scenarios, yielding the lowest Mean Absolute Error (MAE) for the GA lesion area and the highest DICE score, without sacrificing FAF image quality. The code is available at https://github.com/SimoneSarrocco/ga-progression-with-inrs.

</details>


### [70] [MIRA: Medical Image Reflection for Agentic Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10827)
*Shengzhi Wang,Jun Yang,Kai Wu,Xiaozhong Ji,Yiwen Ye,Ziyang Chen,Mingliang Xiong,Wen Fang,Mingqing Liu,Mengyuan Xu,Miaoxuan Shan,Caiyan Liu,Bin He,Qingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIRA（Medical Image Reflection for Agentic Diagnosis），一个面向医学影像自主诊断的智能体框架，通过动态调用图像处理与网络检索工具，并引入反思性验证机制来判断工具使用是否必要及证据是否支持当前假设。MIRA采用两阶段训练策略：先用工具增强的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）数据引擎构建监督微调轨迹，再用强化学习结合在线反思性原则演化优化决策。在9个医学视觉推理基准上平均得分64.73，比Qwen3-VL-8B基线提升7.44分，工具使用判断准确率从56.2%提升至73.8%，有害判断从8.9%降至1.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉智能体虽然能够调用工具检查图像和检索外部知识，但不加选择地使用工具可能引入噪声或误导性证据。可靠诊断不仅需要获取额外观察，还需要验证工具动作是否必要以及获得的证据是否支持当前假设，因此需要一种能够自主搜寻证据并进行反思性验证的诊断框架。

Method: MIRA采用两阶段训练策略：（1）工具增强的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）数据引擎，探索多样化诊断假设，联合验证视觉定位准确性和语义一致性，构建监督微调轨迹；（2）强化学习阶段，通过在线反思性原则演化改进决策——将失败案例蒸馏为候选原则，仅保留能在留出集上提升回报的原则。框架动态调用缩放、接地、指向、旋转、测量等图像处理操作以及网络搜索，并评估所获证据的相关性与一致性。

Result: 在9个医学视觉推理基准上，MIRA平均得分为64.73，较Qwen3-VL-8B骨干模型提升7.44分。工具使用判断的有用性从56.2%提升至73.8%，有害判断从8.9%降至1.6%。定性分析表明MIRA能够重新检查证据、纠正过早结论并适应调整工具使用策略。

Conclusion: MIRA通过反思性证据验证机制有效提升了医学视觉诊断的可靠性，证实了"判断工具使用必要性并验证证据一致性"对智能体诊断的重要性，为医学视觉智能体的可靠部署提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Medical visual agents can use tools to inspect images and retrieve external knowledge, but indiscriminate tool use may introduce noisy or misleading evidence. Reliable diagnosis therefore requires not only acquiring additional observations, but also verifying whether tool actions are necessary and whether the resulting evidence supports the current hypothesis. We introduce MIRA (Medical Image Reflection for Agentic Diagnosis), a medical visual diagnostic framework for autonomous evidence search and reflective verification. MIRA dynamically invokes image-processing operations, including zooming, grounding, pointing, rotation, and measurement, as well as web search, while evaluating the relevance and consistency of the acquired evidence. We develop MIRA through a two-stage training strategy. First, a tool-augmented Monte Carlo Tree Search data engine explores diverse diagnostic hypotheses and jointly verifies visual grounding accuracy and semantic consistency to construct supervised fine-tuning trajectories. Second, reinforcement learning further improves decision-making through online reflective principle evolution: failure cases are distilled into candidate principles, and only principles that improve held-out rollout rewards are retained. Across nine medical visual reasoning benchmarks, MIRA achieves an average score of 64.73, improving its Qwen3-VL-8B backbone by 7.44 points. It also increases useful tool-use judgments from 56.2% to 73.8% and reduces harmful judgments from 8.9% to 1.6%. Qualitative analyses show that MIRA can re-examine evidence, correct premature conclusions, and adapt its tool-use strategy. Project page: https://MIRA-VL.github.io/

</details>


### [71] [UniProbe: A Learnable Token-Level Hallucination Detector for Large VLMs using Multi-Structural Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10835)
*Dvir Samuel,Guy Bar-Shalom,Fabrizio Frasca,Ethan Fetaya,Yftah Ziser,Gal Chechik,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniProbe是一种轻量级、统一的可学习幻觉检测器，从单个前向传播中构建冻结LVLM的计算轨迹异构图（节点为图像块、查询token和生成token，边为注意力权重），通过GNN、ViT、GRU三种结构感知模块交替处理空间、关系与序列证据，实现token级幻觉定位，并支持流式解码时的幻觉感知重采样（将目标幻觉最多降低55%，延迟仅1.06倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）虽在视觉推理和对话上表现出色，但常产生与视觉输入不符的幻觉内容。有效缓解需要token级别的定位以进行精准干预，而现有检测器存在三大缺陷：需要昂贵的全模型微调、依赖忽略模型生成过程的外部验证器、或将内部信号简化成孤立特征和手工统计而丢弃空间、序列和关系结构。因此需要一个能保留并利用完整计算结构的轻量级检测器。

Method: UniProbe从冻结LVLM的单个前向传播中提取异质计算轨迹，构建以图像块、查询token和生成token为节点的有向图，注意力权重编码节点间关系。检测器采用交替的结构感知模块：GNN捕捉关系证据、ViT处理2-D视觉几何、GRU建模响应顺序，三者交错使空间、关系和序列证据在检测过程中持续交互。此外提出流式变体用于幻觉感知解码（检测并重采样幻觉token），以及自适应策略使检测器与LVLM自身生成分布对齐。

Result: 在多种LVLM主干网络上，UniProbe在token级和物体级幻觉检测上均达到最先进水平。在解码过程中，它将物体幻觉降低高达55%，而延迟仅为标准生成的1.06倍，即在几乎不增加延迟的情况下显著抑制幻觉。

Conclusion: UniProbe证明通过轻量级可学习检测器建模冻结LVLM的完整计算轨迹（而非依赖外部验证器或全模型微调），即可实现高效的token级幻觉检测与缓解，且流式解码变体在极小延迟代价下大幅减少幻觉，为LVLM幻觉治理提供了一个实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) achieve impressive visual reasoning and dialogue capabilities, yet frequently hallucinate content unsupported by the visual input. Effective mitigation requires token-level localization, enabling targeted intervention without discarding the entire response. Existing detectors require expensive full-model fine-tuning, rely on external verifiers that ignore the model's generation process, or reduce internal signals to isolated features and hand-crafted statistics, discarding spatial, sequential, and relational structure. We introduce \textbf{UniProbe}, a lightweight, unified, learnable detector that models a frozen LVLM's heterogeneous computational trace from a single forward pass. UniProbe constructs a directed graph over image patches, query tokens, and generated tokens, with attention weights encoding their relations. It processes this trace with alternating structure-aware modules: a GNN for relational evidence, a ViT for 2-D visual geometry, and a GRU for response order. Interleaving them allows spatial, relational, and sequential evidence to interact throughout the detector. We further develop a streaming variant for hallucination-aware decoding, which detects and resamples hallucinated tokens during generation, and a self-adaptation strategy aligning the detector with the LVLM's own generations. Across diverse LVLM backbones, UniProbe achieves state-of-the-art token-level and object-hallucination detection. During decoding, it reduces object hallucinations by up to 55\% at $1.06\times$ the latency of standard generation.

</details>


### [72] [PolyLayout: Hierarchical VLM-Guided Layout Generation Beyond Rectangular Rooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10838)
*Yutong Jiang,Zahra Atashgahi,Carlos Soto Garcia Delgado,Ruben Brokkelkamp,Davide Zanutto,Efşan Sökmen,Shahin Shahkarami*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向家居零售场景的混合层次化框架，用于生成物理上合理的3D房间布局。该框架将生成过程解耦为三个阶段：（1）功能区家具聚类与区域内精细摆放；（2）基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的宏观路径规划，在不同多边形边界内锚定聚类区域和独立家具；（3）基于规则的碰撞自由微观布局优化，并尊重建筑约束。系统在工业级目录和多样化的不规则真实世界拓扑上进行了评估，在保持较低延迟和良好几何合规性的前提下获得了最高的感知合理性，并能扩展到现有方法无法原生支持的不规则边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界与工业界之间在3D房间布局生成上存在显著鸿沟：现有方法主要关注家具摆放的算法策略，忽视了真实家庭中普遍存在的非矩形几何形状以及严格的门窗约束，难以直接支撑家居零售等实际商业应用。作者旨在弥合这一差距，设计一个能够应用于可扩展空间规划场景的零售专用系统。

Method: 提出一个混合的、层次化的三阶段框架：首先进行功能家具聚类与区域内精细放置；其次利用视觉语言模型（VLM）引导宏观路径规划，将聚类区域和独立家具锚定到多样化的多边形边界中；最后通过基于规则的优化实现无碰撞的微观布局，并满足建筑结构约束。系统在工业级目录数据和代表性的不规则真实拓扑上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统获得了最高的感知合理性（perceptual plausibility），同时保持了良好的几何合规性（geometric compliance），且延迟相对较低。此外，系统能够扩展到现有方法不原生支持的不规则边界场景。

Conclusion: 该混合层次化框架有效弥合了学术研究与工业应用之间的差距，通过功能聚类、VLM驱动的宏观路由和规则优化相结合，在真实零售场景中实现了兼顾质量、合规性和效率的3D房间布局生成，尤其是在非矩形和不规则拓扑的适用性方面取得了突破。

Abstract: Generating physically plausible 3D room layouts is essential for home furnishing retail, enabling customers to visualize products in their own homes and confidently make purchasing decisions. However, a gap exists between academic research and real-world application: existing solutions primarily focus on algorithmic strategies for furniture placement, largely neglecting the non-rectangular geometries and strict door/window constraints prevalent in real homes. To bridge the gap, we introduce a hybrid, hierarchical framework tailored for retail, specifically designed to support scalable spatial planning applications. Our system decouples generation into three stages: (1) functional furniture clustering and fine-grained intra-zone placement; (2) macro-routing guided by a vision-language model (VLM) to anchor both these clustered zones and any remaining standalone furniture within diverse polygonal boundaries; and (3) rule-based optimization for collision-free micro-arrangements that respect architectural constraints. We evaluate our system on production-scale catalogs and a representative set of irregular real-world topologies. Our results show that our approach attains the highest perceptual plausibility while maintaining good geometric compliance at relatively low latency, and extends to irregular boundaries that existing methods do not natively support.

</details>


### [73] [The GENEA Challenge 2026: A Large-Scale Disentangled Evaluation of Speech-Driven Gesture Generation on the Seamless Interaction Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10839)
*Rajmund Nagy,Silvia Arellano García,Hendric Voss,Mihail Tsakov,Taras Kucherenko,Youngwoo Yoon,Gustav Eje Henter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告了第四届GENEA挑战赛的结果，对五个语音驱动手势生成系统进行了大规模人工评估，采用解耦评估方法分别评测动作质量和语音对齐，并新增了语义手势生成任务和文本不匹配评估方法。共进行了四项大规模用户研究，收集了来自869名测试者的超过23,000次投票。结果表明数据集的真实动作捕捉片段在所有维度上都远优于参赛系统，尤其是语义表达方面几乎所有系统均未能生成具有语义表现力的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音驱动的手势生成系统虽然发展迅速，但以往评估方法常将动作质量和语音对齐混淆在一起，难以准确衡量系统各维度的真实表现。此外，已有挑战赛缺乏对语义手势生成和双人对话交互能力的系统评估，因此需要设计更精细的解耦评估方法并引入新的评估任务。

Method: 采用解耦评估方法论将动作质量与语音对齐分开评测；引入双人失配研究以隔离倾听和回应对话者的效果；新增基于Grounded Gestures数据子集的语义手势生成任务及文本失配评估方法。共运行四项大规模用户研究。

Result: 过滤后的数据集片段在动作真实感研究中以68-95%的成对胜率大幅超过所有提交系统；语音对齐研究中动作捕捉片段的顶线得分为62%，最佳提交仅32%，其余仅略高于0%；双人研究中动作捕捉顶线为65%，但所有提交均未显著高于随机水平；语义失配评估中数据集手势具有高度表现力（匹配率79%），但最佳提交仅获得8%的恰当性得分。

Conclusion: 当前语音驱动手势生成系统在动作质量、语音对齐、双人交互响应和语义表达等各维度上与真实动作捕捉数据仍存在巨大差距，尤其语义手势生成和对话响应能力严重不足，该领域仍需进一步研究突破。所有数据和输出将公开以促进可复现性和后续研究。

Abstract: This preprint presents the results of the fourth GENEA Challenge, a large-scale human evaluation of five speech-driven gesture-generation systems trained by participating teams on the Seamless Interaction dataset of dyadic conversations. As in the 2023 GENEA Challenge, we used a disentangled evaluation methodology to assess motion quality and speech alignment without confounding between the two, and performed a dyadic mismatching study to isolate the effect of listening and reacting to the interlocutor. We additionally introduce a new semantic gesture-generation task and a text-mismatching evaluation methodology using the Grounded Gestures subset of the data. In total, we ran four large-scale user studies, collecting over 23,000 votes from 869 test-takers.
  In the motion-realism study, the dataset's filtered segments had substantially higher motion quality than all challenge submissions (68-95% pairwise winrate). In the speech-alignment study, the motion-capture segments provided a conceptual ceiling at 62% alignment score, with the top submission significantly behind at 32% and the rest only slightly above the 0% expected of an input-independent system. In the dyadic study, motion capture again set the ceiling at 65% appropriateness score, but no submission scored substantially above chance, indicating that the systems could not yet respond to the interlocutor. Finally, the semantic mismatching evaluation found highly expressive gestures in the dataset (test-takers identified the matching transcript 79% of the time), yet almost all submissions failed to generate semantically expressive motion, with the best achieving only an 8% appropriateness score.
  The collected votes and outputs will be made publicly available at https://genea-workshop.github.io/2026/challenge/ to facilitate reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [74] [Multi-View Relational Distillation for Spatial Reasoning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10864)
*Kiet T. Nguyen,Hanbo Shim,Jinwoo Kim,Seunghoon Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多视图关系蒸馏（MVRD）方法，通过蒸馏跨视图的patch-wise余弦相似度而非教师特征本身来增强VLMs的几何空间理解，在提升空间推理能力的同时保持视觉-语言对齐，且参数量和延迟远低于特征融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在图像和视频理解上表现出色，但其视觉-空间表征在几何上具有脆弱性，导致在具身AI、机器人和自动驾驶等空间推理任务中失效。现有方法存在缺陷：空间QA微调可能固化虚假视觉表征；与几何grounded视觉模型特征融合会大幅增加推理时的模型尺寸；直接匹配教师特征的知识蒸馏会破坏视觉与文本表征的预训练对齐。

Method: 提出多视图关系蒸馏（MVRD），不再蒸馏教师特征本身，而是蒸馏跨视图间的patch-wise余弦相似度。这种关系信号编码了几何对应关系，足以支撑空间理解，同时使学生表征保持欠定（underdetermined），从而能够维持其预训练的视觉-语言空间。

Result: 在多种代表性VLM上，MVRD显著提升了视觉空间推理能力，超越了监督微调和特征蒸馏方法，接近特征融合方法的性能但所需额外参数和延迟大幅降低。MVRD使视觉表征更具几何性同时保留语言对齐，并泛化到3D场景理解任务（如目标定位、密集描述和问答）。

Conclusion: MVRD证明了通过关系蒸馏而非特征蒸馏可以有效增强VLMs的几何空间能力，在保持视觉-语言对齐的同时显著提升空间推理性能，为具身AI等下游任务提供了一条高效、轻量的几何grounding路径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong image and video understanding, yet their visual-spatial representations remain geometrically fragile, leading to failures in spatial reasoning needed for embodied AI, robotics, and autonomous driving. Prior approaches to geometry grounding either fine-tune VLMs on spatial question answering, which can perpetuate spurious visual representations, or fuse features from large geometry-grounded vision models, which substantially increases model size at inference. Knowledge distillation from geometry-grounded vision models offers an alternative, but directly matching multi-view teacher features can disrupt the pretrained alignment between visual and textual representations, degrading object- and language-semantic capabilities. We propose multi-view relational distillation (MVRD), which distills patch-wise cosine similarities across views instead of the teacher features themselves. These relations encode geometric correspondences adequate for spatial understanding, while leaving the student representation underdetermined, allowing it to remain close to its pretrained vision- language space. Across representative VLMs, MVRD improves visual-spatial reasoning, outperforming supervised fine-tuning and feature distillation while approaching feature fusion methods with considerably fewer added parameters and lower latency. We show that MVRD makes visual representations more geometric while retaining language alignment, and generalizes to 3D scene understanding tasks such as object grounding, dense captioning, and question answering.

</details>


### [75] [NullEdit: Stealthy Image Protection via VLM Condition Redirection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10870)
*Weiyao Huang,Liqin Wang,Ziqi Sheng,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NullEdit是一种针对现代图像编辑器的推理时防御方法，通过抑制编辑请求使输出保持自然且保留源图像内容，同时不产生明显篡改痕迹或身份替换，有效防御未经授权的图像篡改行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑防御方法存在两难：要么通过明显破坏使编辑失效从而暴露保护痕迹，要么允许编辑但导致身份或参考内容漂移，无法真正阻止编辑行为本身。需要一种隐蔽且无害的防御方案。

Method: NullEdit针对VLM在条件化下游DiT主干之前，由参考图像和指令联合形成的表示进行重定向。利用正常编辑锚点和无编辑锚点，将VLM表示向无编辑方向引导；同时通过跨提示梯度平均将保护迁移到未见的指令上。

Result: 在Step1X-Edit和Qwen-Image-Edit上，针对CelebA-HQ和VGGFace2数据集，NullEdit相比SOTA基线平均降低了0.813的EditReward IF分数，同时保持主体身份和源内容。

Conclusion: NullEdit通过隐蔽的无操作防御策略成功抑制了恶意编辑请求，保持输出自然且源保留，同时实现了跨指令的泛化保护能力。

Abstract: Modern image editors combine vision-language models (VLMs) with diffusion transformer backbones to modify a single reference image according to instructions without fine-tuning. This capability also enables unauthorized manipulation of publicly released images. Existing inference-time defenses either invalidate edits through conspicuous corruption, thereby exposing the protection, or allow them to proceed with identity or reference content drift, thereby failing to prevent the editing behavior itself. We instead target a stealthy and harmless no-op in which the requested edit is suppressed, the output remains natural and source-preserving without conspicuous artifacts or identity replacement, and harmful semantics requested by malicious instructions are absent. We propose NullEdit, which targets the VLM representation jointly formed from the reference image and instruction before it conditions the downstream DiT backbone. Using normal-edit and no-edit anchors, NullEdit redirects this representation, while cross-prompt gradient averaging transfers protection to held out instructions. Across Step1X-Edit and Qwen-Image-Edit on CelebA-HQ and VGGFace2, NullEdit reduces the EditReward IF score by 0.813 on average relative to the SOTA baseline while preserving subject identity and source content.

</details>


### [76] [ConfTriage: A Calibration-Aware LLM Triage Framework for Pulmonary Nodule Malignancy with Selective Specialist Deferral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10885)
*Md Rabiul Islam,Samir Abdaljalil,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ConfTriage框架，证明仅通过自然语言描述的肺结节特征，通用大语言模型（LLM）即可作为校准分诊层，对76.5%的病例进行零样本推理，仅将低置信度病例转交给专科深度学习模型，在LIDC-IDRI数据集上达到88.22%的F1分数和0.92的AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的肺结节恶性预测依赖基于图像的专科深度学习模型，需要大量标注影像数据和任务专属训练。作者旨在探索通用大语言模型是否仅通过自然语言呈现的标准结节属性即可作为校准的分诊层，以降低对大规模标注数据和任务专属训练的需求。

Method: ConfTriage基于三大支柱：以语言作为模态（仅使用结节属性的自然语言描述）、以校准作为安全机制（通过LLM概率的L1校准误差控制风险）、以及选择性专科深度学习回退（对低置信度病例转交专科DL模型）。理论上证明了有限样本组合误差界和oracle不等式；实验上在LIDC-IDRI上对五个前沿LLM进行七路输入消融研究。

Result: 七路输入消融表明自然语言描述主导诊断信号，而低级图像统计信息几乎无诊断价值。ConfTriage达到88.22%的F1分数和0.92的AUC，76.5%的病例由零样本LLM推理直接解决，仅不确定病例转交专科DL模型。

Conclusion: 临床上有意义的诊断信息可以通过结构化的放射学描述被捕获，并被校准后的LLM用于选择性转诊。该框架为通用LLM预测与专科AI模型结合提供了实用的医疗决策支持路径。

Abstract: Pulmonary nodule malignancy prediction typically depends on image-trained specialist deep learning (DL) models that require substantial annotated imaging data and task-specific training. We investigate whether a generalist large language model (LLM), reading only a faithful natural-language rendering of standard nodule attributes, can serve as a calibrated triage layer. We propose ConfTriage, a confidence-calibrated method built on three pillars: language as the modality, calibration as the safety mechanism, and a selective specialist DL backstop for low-confidence cases. We prove two guarantees: a finite-sample combined-error bound yielding an explicit per-threshold operational certificate, and an oracle inequality showing that excess risk over the Bayes-optimal deferral classifier is controlled by the L1 calibration error of the LLM probability. A controlled seven-way input ablation across five frontier LLMs on LIDC-IDRI shows that natural-language descriptions dominate the diagnostic signal, while low-level image statistics are essentially diagnostically vacuous. ConfTriage achieved an F1 score of 88.22% and an AUC of 0.92, resolving 76.5% of cases using zero-shot LLM inference alone and referring only uncertain cases to the specialist DL backstop. These results demonstrate that clinically meaningful diagnostic information can be captured through structured radiological descriptions and leveraged by calibrated LLMs for selective referral. The framework suggests a practical pathway for combining generalist LLM prediction with specialist AI models in medical decision-support systems. Source code is publicly available at https://github.com/rabiul-ai/ConfTriage.

</details>


### [77] [GESTO: Human-Centric Spatio-Temporal Memory for Reasoning in Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10886)
*Ermanno Bartoli,Buwei He,Dennis Rotondi,Sebastian Koch,Federico Tombari,Kai O. Arras,Patric Jensfelt,Yixi Cai,Iolanda Leite*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GESTO是一种将持久化4D场景图与两级层次结构（原子人-物交互与目标驱动事件）耦合的时空记忆系统，从RGB-D观测流中自动提取并锚定交互到场景实体，支持以活动为中心的时空推理，在标准基准和新增查询上接近使用真值输入的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D场景图保留了物体和位置的历时信息但缺少活动结构；而活动表示要么未锚定在持久化3D场景中，要么依赖外部提供的事件边界和物体关联。机器人需要在人类环境中记忆物体存在的位置以及人们如何使用它们、个体交互如何组合成目标导向的活动，因此需要一个同时包含活动结构和持久场景锚定的时空记忆。

Method: GESTO将持久化4D场景图与两级活动层次（原子人-物交互层和目标驱动事件层）结合。从RGB-D观测流中自动提取带时间戳的交互，将其锚定到持久场景实体，将交互分组为事件，并利用事件上下文细化不确定的物体关联。通过关系感知的工具调用代理对记忆进行以活动为中心的时空推理查询。

Result: 在标准分类（text、binary、time）上分别取得0.71、0.75、0.70的得分，接近使用真值事件和物体锚定输入的方法，并显著优于不提供这些输入时相同的推理框架；在新增的Space2Event和Event2Space查询上分别取得0.73和0.75。消融实验表明，层次化事件结构与上下文感知的锚定细化提供互补收益。

Conclusion: 层次化事件结构和上下文感知的锚定细化互补，支持以活动为锚定的层次化记忆，用于动态人类环境中的回顾性推理。

Abstract: Robots operating in human environments need memories that capture not only what objects exist and where, but also how people use them over time and how individual interactions compose into goal-directed activities. Existing 4D scene graphs preserve object and place histories but omit activity structure, whereas activity representations are either not grounded in persistent 3D scenes or rely on externally provided event boundaries and object associations. We present GESTO (Grounded Event and Spatio-Temporal memOry), a spatio-temporal memory that couples a persistent 4D scene graph with a two-level hierarchy of atomic human--object interactions and goal-driven events. From an RGB-D observation stream, GESTO automatically extracts timestamped interactions, grounds them to persistent scene entities, groups them into events, and uses event context to refine uncertain object associations. A relation-aware tool-calling agent queries the resulting memory for activity-centric spatio-temporal reasoning. We evaluate GESTO on the reproducible text, binary, and time categories of an existing benchmark, together with 40 new Space2Event and Event2Space queries. GESTO achieves scores of 0.71, 0.75, and 0.70 on the standard categories, approaching a method supplied with ground-truth event and object grounding, while substantially outperforming the same reasoning framework when these inputs are removed. It further achieves 0.73 and 0.75 on Space2Event and Event2Space queries. Ablations show that hierarchical event structure and context-aware grounding refinement provide complementary benefits, supporting activity-grounded hierarchical memory for retrospective reasoning in dynamic human environments.

</details>


### [78] [Sensor-Informed Per-Point Covariance for Structured-Light 3D Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10888)
*Sehoon Tak,Jae-Sang Hyun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于传感器信息的一阶方法，利用实验测得的相位精度以及标定的相位到深度/3D映射，为条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）重建的每个点构建3×3协方差场，以准确反映测量过程中的各向异性不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的点云不确定度模型通常假设为各向同性常数或仅从局部表面几何推断，未能显式反映测量过程本身。在条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）中，相位噪声通过标定重建传播会生成强烈的各向异性3D不确定性，因此需要一种能够真正反映传感器测量模型的逐点协方差估计方法。

Method: 提出一种传感器信息驱动的一阶（first-order）不确定性传播方法：利用实验测得的相位精度作为输入，结合标定的相位-深度映射和相位-3D映射，构建逐点的3×3协方差场。方法将协方差分解为秩1的相位诱导主协方差部分，以及通过拟合横向图像空间扰动尺度得到的满秩补偿项，从而获得完整的协方差矩阵。

Result: 在固定成像条件下的重复平面实验中，主导协方差方向与观察光线方向高度一致；主导相位诱导不确定度尺度与标量深度不确定度相一致。在G-ICP配准中，所提协方差相比恒定各向同性模型显著提升了配准性能，并提供了与传统基于几何的协方差互补的传感器衍生不确定性表示。

Conclusion: 该方法能够从传感器测量过程出发生成物理意义明确、各向异性的逐点协方差场，在配准等下游任务中优于传统各项同性模型，且与几何基协方差形成互补，为FPP三维重建的概率性处理提供了更精确的不确定性表征。

Abstract: Per-point uncertainty models are important in structured-light 3D reconstruction for probabilistic registration, fusion, and quality assessment. In practice, however, point-cloud covariances are often modeled as isotropic constants or inferred from local surface geometry and therefore do not explicitly reflect the measurement process. This is a limitation in fringe projection profilometry (FPP), where phase noise propagates through calibrated reconstruction and produces strongly anisotropic 3D uncertainty. This paper presents a sensor-informed first-order method for constructing a per-point 3 x 3 covariance field from experimentally measured phase precision and calibrated phase-to-depth and phase-to-3D mappings. The formulation separates a rank-1 phase-induced covariance from an effective full-rank completion obtained by incorporating fitted lateral image-space perturbation scales. Repeated-plane experiments under fixed imaging conditions show close alignment of the dominant covariance direction with the viewing ray, and consistency between the dominant phase-induced uncertainty scale and scalar depth uncertainty. In G-ICP registration, the proposed covariance substantially improves over a constant isotropic model while providing a sensor-derived uncertainty representation complementary to conventional geometry-based covariances.

</details>


### [79] [VIDS-Seg: Towards Reliable Uncertainty Quantification in Pediatric Cardiac Ultrasound Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10903)
*Paul Fischer,Ece Ozkan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIDS-Seg框架，在VIDS基础上扩展了变分推断方法，使其适用于密集图像分割任务。该模型在成人心脏超声数据上训练，零样本迁移到儿科数据，在不牺牲分割精度的前提下，显著提升了不确定性估计与分割错误的空间对应性，从而实现OOD条件下的"静默失败"检测，无需重新训练或额外标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习部署中，模型在分布偏移（尤其是训练数据中代表性不足的子群）下可能静默失效。以儿科为例，在成人队列上训练的模型可能在儿童身上表现不佳且无任何预警。由于重新训练通常不可行，在推断阶段检测此类失败成为关键的临床需求。

Method: 基于VIDS（分布偏移下的变分推断）框架，VIDS-Seg在轻量级预测头（prediction head）上应用摊销变分推断，使得自适应的OOD感知先验（adaptive, OOD-aware prior）在密集图像分割任务中变得可计算可训练。评估场景为左心室超声图像分割：在成人队列（EchoNet-Dynamic）上训练，在儿科队列（EchoNet-Pediatric）上零样本评估。

Result: 在所有年龄段分层中，VIDS-Seg在分割精度上与竞争性基线相当，同时生成的不确定性图与分割错误的空间对应性显著更高。该优势在对所有基线应用温度缩放后依然保持。下游应用中，VIDS-Seg产生的射血分数估计更准确、更稳定，且在婴儿亚组中能更可靠地检测心脏功能障碍。

Conclusion: OOD感知的不确定性量化可以作为部署分割模型的实用安全层，在无需重训练或额外标注数据的情况下，检测代表性不足子群中的静默失败。

Abstract: Reliable clinical deployment of machine learning requires models that know when they are likely to fail, particularly for subgroups underrepresented in training data. A common case is pediatric care, where models trained on adult cohorts can silently under-perform on children with no indication that something has gone wrong. As retraining with labeled pediatric data is often infeasible, detecting such failures at inference time is a critical clinical need. Building on the VIDS (Variational Inference under Distribution Shifts) framework, we introduce VIDS-Seg, which applies amortized variational inference over a lightweight prediction head to make this adaptive, OOD-aware prior tractable for dense image segmentation. We evaluate VIDS-Seg on left ventricular segmentation in echocardiography, a setting where pediatric anatomy differs systematically from the adult population most segmentation models are trained on, training on an adult cohort (EchoNet-Dynamic) and evaluating zero-shot on a pediatric cohort (EchoNet-Pediatric). Across all age strata, VIDS-Seg matches competitive baselines in segmentation accuracy while producing substantially higher spatial correspondence between predicted uncertainty and segmentation error, an advantage that persists even after applying temperature scaling to all baselines. Downstream, it yields more accurate and stable ejection fraction estimates and more reliable detection of cardiac malfunction in the infant subgroup. Our results indicate that OOD-aware uncertainty quantification can serve as a practical safety layer for deployed segmentation models, enabling detection of silent failures in underrepresented subgroups without retraining or additional labeled data.

</details>


### [80] [Order Matters: LVLMs as Judges for Temporal Reasoning in Image Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10908)
*Martina Ianaro,Guilherme Fernandes,Maurizio Gabbrielli,Joao Magalhaes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态评估系统在视觉叙事时序判断上的结构性缺陷，指出现有LVLM评估器因因果掩码和旋转位置编码等架构偏见，存在系统性位置不对称（首因和近因效应），导致其在成对时序辨别任务上发生灾难性崩溃。研究呼吁超越快照式评估指标，推动面向时序感知的统一逻辑结构评估范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式多媒体的评估面临"判断危机"：人类天然能够综合理解故事的时序与逻辑流，而自动评估系统对序列连续性"盲视"，无法区分连贯叙事与随机打乱或矛盾的序列。现有依赖LVLM作为评判者的范式在架构层面存在根本局限。

Method: 通过一系列诊断性探针实验，系统性地揭示LVLM在点式评分与成对时序辨别之间的性能鸿沟，并定位其中的系统性位置不对称（首因效应和近因效应），从架构层面分析因果掩码和旋转位置编码等机制如何导致这些偏见。

Result: 研究发现：模型在孤立点式评分上表现看似合格，但在成对时序判别上出现灾难性崩溃；帧的位置对模型判断的影响常大于其语义一致性；该问题在性质上是结构性的而非数据稀缺所致。

Conclusion: 当前基于Transformer的评判模型从根本上不适合长程视觉推理。研究呼吁多模态社区超越以快照为中心的指标，建立时序感知的评估范式，将视觉序列视为统一逻辑结构而非无序帧的集合。

Abstract: As generative multimedia evolves from static image synthesis to complex, interleaved visual narratives, a foundational bottleneck has emerged: the judgment crisis. While human perception naturally synthesizes the temporal and logical flow of a story, automated evaluation systems remain largely "blind" to sequential continuity, often failing to distinguish between a coherent narrative and a semantically shuffled or contradictory sequence. This work identifies a critical structural gap in current multimodal evaluation paradigms, arguing that the reliance on Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) as judges is fundamentally limited by architectural biases. Our analysis reveals a profound performance dichotomy: while models may appear competent in isolated pointwise scoring, they suffer a catastrophic collapse when required to perform pairwise discrimination of temporal order. We demonstrate that this is not merely a data-scarcity issue but a structural one. Through a series of diagnostic probes, we uncover systematic positional asymmetries, specifically primacy and recency effects, where a model's judgment of a story is significantly influenced by the placement of a frame, often more than by its semantic consistency. These biases, potentially rooted in causal masking and rotary embeddings, suggest that current transformer-based judges are inherently ill-equipped for long-form visual reasoning. By exposing these blind spots, we challenge the multimedia community to move beyond snapshot-centric metrics and instead pioneer Temporally-Aware Evaluation paradigms that treat visual sequences as unified logical structures rather than unordered collections of frames.

</details>


### [81] [Temporally Grounded Compositional Camera Motion Understanding via Geometric Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10932)
*Dazhao Du,Shiyan Du,Jian Liu,Yongjian Yu,Bohai Gu,Tao Han,Hualuo Liu,Eric Liu,Yujia Zhang,Xi Chen,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CamChoreo基准和CamDistill方法，将相机运动理解从片段级标注推进到时间定位的组合式识别，在不增加推理开销的情况下匹配直接注入3D几何特征的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型处理相机运动理解时通常对整个视频片段分配一个或多个标签，忽略了真实相机运动的两个关键特性：运动可在镜头内变化，以及多个运动可同时发生。这限制了对相机运动的细粒度理解能力。

Method: 本文提出两点贡献：(1) CamChoreo基准，包含4,229个真实单镜头视频片段，由专家标注时间分段，使用20个方向感知标签的紧凑词汇表，近半数分段包含复合相机运动；(2) CamDistill方法，在训练期间将冻结的3D基础模型的几何知识蒸馏到轻量级相机令牌中，推理时无需运行昂贵的3D模型。

Result: CamDistill在无需推理时运行3D教师模型的情况下，达到了与直接特征注入（CamInject基线）相当的精度，同时显著降低了推理开销。实验表明当前MLLM难以识别这种细粒度、组合式的运动，因为其视觉编码器更强调语义内容而非几何证据。

Conclusion: CamChoreo和CamDistill共同将相机运动理解从片段级标注推进到时间定位的组合式识别，为空间智能和可控视频生成领域提供了基准和方法支持。

Abstract: Understanding camera motion is fundamental to video perception, with applications in spatial intelligence and controllable video generation. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide a natural interface for this task, but existing work typically assigns one or more labels to an entire clip. Such clip-level recognition overlooks two defining properties of real camera motion: it can change within a shot, and multiple movements can occur simultaneously. We therefore formulate camera-motion understanding as temporally grounded, compositional recognition, which requires a model to localize motion-consistent intervals and identify every movement active within each interval. We introduce CamChoreo, a benchmark of 4,229 real single-shot clips with expert-annotated temporal segments. Its annotations use a compact vocabulary of 20 direction-aware labels, and nearly half of the segments contain compound camera motion, with multiple movement primitives active simultaneously. Recognizing such fine-grained, compositional motion is hard for current MLLMs, whose visual encoders emphasize semantic content rather than the geometric evidence on which camera motion depends. Directly injecting features from a frozen 3D foundation model addresses this gap, but requires running the expensive geometry model on every input; we refer to this baseline as CamInject. We instead propose CamDistill, which distills the same geometric knowledge into lightweight camera tokens during training and removes the 3D model at inference. CamDistill matches the accuracy of direct feature injection without running the 3D teacher at inference. Together, CamChoreo and CamDistill advance camera-motion understanding from clip-level labeling to temporally grounded, compositional recognition. Project page: https://ddz16.github.io/cammotion.github.io/.

</details>


### [82] [SafeCA: Safe Cross-Attention Localization and Regulation for Text-to-Video Jailbreak Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10933)
*Siyuan Liang,Yupeng Qiu,Junfeng Fang,Rong-Cheng Tu,Jiaxing Huang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SafeCA是一种基于交叉注意力特征空间的防御机制，通过注意力稳定分析、能量归一化掩蔽和语义适配器来防御T2V模型的越狱攻击，将攻击成功率降低约20%，推理开销仅增加0.1秒且保持语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T2V模型在现实部署中易受越狱攻击而产生有害内容，已有的防御方法（输入过滤或重建）存在高计算延迟和语义扭曲的问题，需要一种更高效且不损害语义的防御方案。

Method: 首次对干净样本与越狱样本在交叉注意力特征空间进行系统性分析，发现累积分离效应和线性可分离性递增趋势。基于此提出SafeCA：1）通过单次推断的注意力稳定性分析识别关键防御区域和值；2）利用能量归一化的注意力掩蔽缓解异常激活；3）引入轻量级语义空间适配器重定向异常语义流；4）通过反向传播特征异常信号到输入提示词来检测和抑制恶意token。

Result: 在主流的T2V模型上将越狱成功率降低约20%，推理开销几乎可忽略（+0.1秒），并保持良好的文本-视频语义一致性。

Conclusion: SafeCA提供了一种架构级别的、可部署的T2V生成模型保护范式，弥补了传统防御方法在效率和语义保真度上的不足。

Abstract: Text-to-Video (T2V) generative models are vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in real-world deployment, leading them to produce harmful or inappropriate content. Existing defense approaches mainly rely on input filtering or reconstruction, which not only incur high computational latency but also tend to distort semantics. To address these issues, we experimentally and systematically analyze the differences between clean and jailbreak samples in the cross-attention feature space, revealing for the first time a cumulative separation effect and a progressively increasing trend of linear separability between the two during the diffusion process. Based on this insight, we propose SafeCA, a feature-level defense mechanism for safe cross-attention localization and regularization. Firstly, we identify key defensive regions and values through attention stability analysis using cross-attention features collected from clean prompts within a single inference. Secondly, SafeCA mitigates anomalous activations via attention masking with energy normalization and introduces a lightweight semantic-space adapter to redirect abnormal semantic flows. Furthermore, we detect and suppress potentially malicious tokens by back-propagating feature anomaly signals to the input cue words, thereby enhancing the deployability of the defense in commercial models. Experimental results show that SafeCA reduces the jailbreak success rate by about 20% on mainstream T2V models, adds almost no inference overhead (+0.1s), and maintains good text-video semantic consistency. Overall, SafeCA provides an architecture-level, deployable protection paradigm for T2V generation models.

</details>


### [83] [GS-CPE: Unified 6-Degree-of-Freedom Camera Pose Estimation via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10938)
*Huaiyuan Weng,Chul Min Yeum,Su-Min Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GS-CPE，一种基于3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)的由粗到精6自由度相机姿态估计框架，统一了几何粗估计与3DGS变形精修，在室内外多个基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉定位领域已取得显著进展（从场景坐标回归到直接相机姿态回归），但如何在鲁棒泛化与高精度之间取得平衡仍然具有挑战性。

Method: GS-CPE采用由粗到精的两阶段框架：(1) 粗阶段：通过检索引导的几何姿态估计在3DGS场景表示上进行粗略姿态估计；(2) 精修阶段：在多尺度优化框架中最小化遮挡感知的掩膜RGB重投影目标，并采用自适应重渲染进行姿态精修。

Result: 在7Scenes、Cambridge Landmarks、FAST-LIVO2以及自定义数据集等室内外基准上的大量实验表明，GS-CPE在精度和泛化能力上均持续优于现有方法，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: GS-CPE通过将几何粗估计与基于3DGS的鲁棒精修有效结合，实现了精度与泛化能力的统一提升，验证了3DGS表示在相机姿态估计中的潜力。

Abstract: Despite substantial progress in visual localization, from scene coordinate regression to direct camera pose regression, achieving both robust generalization and high accuracy remain challenging. This study introduces GS-CPE (Gaussian Splatting based Camera Pose Estimation), a coarse-to-fine framework for 6-DoF camera pose estimation that unifies geometry-based coarse pose estimation with robust 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) warping based pose refinement. GS-CPE first estimates a coarse pose via retrieval-guided geometric pose estimation on a 3DGS scene representation, then refines it by minimizing a visibility aware masked RGB warping objective in a multi-scale optimization framework, with adaptive re-rendering. Extensive experiments on indoor and outdoor benchmarks including 7Scenes, Cambridge Landmarks, FAST-LIVO2 datasets, and a custom dataset demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, consistently outperforming in both accuracy and generalization.

</details>


### [84] [Mixture-of-Experts-based Entropy Model for Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10947)
*Jonas Brenig,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将Mixture of Experts（MoE）方法引入学习型图像压缩领域，提出基于MoE的熵模型（MoEE），使模型能够根据输入图像有选择性地激活所需参数子集，在Kodak数据集上相比VVC实现了-16.85%的BD-Rate提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端学习型图像压缩方法已取得显著进展并超越传统方法，但现有模型对所有输入使用全部参数，缺乏针对不同图像内容的自适应能力。与此同时，MoE方法在NLP和计算机视觉任务中表现出色，因此作者希望将MoE引入图像压缩，使模型能够按需激活参数子集，在保证压缩效率的同时提升模型的自适应性和效率。

Method: 提出MoEE（MoE-based Entropy Model），即在学习型图像压缩的熵模型中引入Mixture of Experts机制。MoE思想的核心是让模型仅激活与当前输入图像相关的部分专家参数，而非对所有输入都使用完整的参数集，从而实现参数的自适应选择和高效利用。

Result: 在Kodak数据集上取得了相比VVC（Versatile Video Coding）标准-16.85%的BD-Rate改进，表明该方法在压缩效率上显著优于当前最先进的传统视频编码标准。

Conclusion: 将MoE引入学习型图像压缩的熵模型是一种有效的方法，能够通过参数的条件激活机制在保证良好率失真性能的同时提升模型效率，为后续基于条件计算的学习型图像压缩研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Learned image compression has seen significant progress in recent years with the development of end-to-end learned models that achieve better compression efficiency than state-of-the-art conventional methods. Recently, Mixture of Experts (MoE) approaches have seen promising results in NLP and computer vision tasks. In this paper, we introduce the MoE approach to learned image compression. We propose a MoE-based Entropy model (MoEE) for learned image compression, allowing the model to selectively activate only the subset of parameters required for the input image. Our model achieves a BD-Rate improvement over VVC of -16.85% on the Kodak dataset.

</details>


### [85] [StreamFlow: Dynamic Memory Flows for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10949)
*Muxin Fu,Yifan Zhang,Wentao Zhang,Fangming Guo,Qian Chen,Guibin Zhang,Shuicheng Yan,Bo An*

Main category: cs.CV
