Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文档介绍了一个基于FPGA的数字合成器项目，该合成器用于专业音频应用，通过数字逻辑近似生成正弦波形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号合成在电子领域无处不在，正弦波形用于时钟、信号传输、反馈控制等关键功能。数字合成是使用数字逻辑准确近似正弦波形的方法，需要硬件实现来满足专业音频等应用需求。

Method: 通过FPGA硬件实现数字合成器，将数字化近似生成的正弦波形转换为模拟域（或直接用于数字域），用于专业音频应用。

Result: 成功在FPGA中实现了一个适用于专业音频应用的数字合成器，能够在特定频率下高精度地近似生成正弦波形。

Conclusion: 该项目验证了基于FPGA的数字合成器在专业音频领域的可行性，为数字信号合成提供了一种可硬件实现的方案。

Abstract: Signal synthesis is used in every aspect of the electronics world, where sinusoidal waveforms are used to perform functions such as clocking, signal transmission, feedback controls, and other applications. Digital synthesis is the method of approximating sinusoidal waveforms using digital logic, where the waveform is approximated to an accurate degree at a specific frequency which can be either implemented digitally or converted into the analog domain for use elsewhere. This project details the creation of a digital synthesizer commonly used for professional audio applications through the implementation of hardware in an FPGA.

</details>


### [119] [CARB: A Characterization-Guided Framework for CNN Inference Cost Prediction and Deployment Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10506)
*Linh Nguyen,Zhixin Pan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文对13419种CNN配置在RTX 5090和RTX 3080两个GPU平台上进行了工作负载特征化研究，发现能耗、延迟和峰值内存呈现截然不同的扩展行为，并提出CARB级联混合集成模型和三阶段部署筛选流程，实现R²约0.99的三目标联合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着CNN模型部署到资源受限的GPU平台，预部署阶段对推理成本（能耗、延迟、峰值内存）的准确估算越来越关键。现有方法依赖FLOPs、延迟测量或单设备性能分析作为能耗代理，忽略了架构设计与硬件负载之间的非线性交互。

Method: 在RTX 5090和RTX 3080两个GPU平台上对13419个CNN配置进行GPU遥测驱动的负载特征化研究，分析能耗、延迟和内存的扩展行为及跨平台迁移性差异；基于特征化发现，开发CARB级联混合集成模型联合预测三个目标，并设计两阶段部署筛选工作流。

Result: 能耗和延迟在高计算需求下差异达3倍；能耗和延迟需要平台特定模型，而内存跨平台迁移性良好；CARB在所有三个目标上实现R²约0.99；两阶段筛选流程能在数秒内消除90%以上的候选配置。

Conclusion: 能耗、延迟和内存展现出本质不同的扩展行为，需要针对性的预测策略；提出的CARB模型和两阶段筛选工作流能够有效支持大规模CNN设计空间的部署前评估与Pareto优先排选，并经真实硬件验证。

Abstract: Accurate pre-deployment estimation of CNN inference cost--energy, latency, and peak memory--is increasingly critical as models are deployed on resource-constrained GPU platforms. Existing approaches rely on FLOPs, latency measurements, or single-device profiling as energy proxies, overlooking the non-linear interactions between architectural design and hardware load. We present a workload characterization study of 13 419 CNN configurations on two GPU platforms (RTX 5090 and RTX 3080) under GPU telemetry, revealing that energy, latency, and memory exhibit fundamentally distinct scaling behaviors: energy and latency diverge by 3x under high computational demand, and cross-GPU transferability differs by target--energy and latency require platform-specific models while memory transfers well across the two tested platforms. Building on these characterization findings, we develop CARB, a cascade-blended ensemble that jointly predicts all three targets with R2 ~0.99, and a two-stage deployment screening workflow that eliminates over 90% of candidates in seconds, reducing large design spaces to a Pareto-prioritized shortlist validated against real hardware.

</details>


### [120] [Adaptive Matrix Multiplication for Dynamic Shapes on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10803)
*Yuhang Zhou,Jiang Peng,Qianyu Jiang,Zhibin Wang,Xinghui Tian,Jianwei Zhou,Songxiang Zhu,Jingyi Zhang,Junsong Wang,Chen Tian*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出AdaptCore，一个用于Ascend NPU上通用高性能矩阵乘法（MatMul）的自适应框架，通过将算子优化解耦为空间分块和指令编排，结合可组合优化库与确定性分析性能模型，实现O(1)开销的运行时调度，在80,000种输入形状上平均加速1.85倍，端到端模型最大加速1.48倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MatMul面临由高度动态的张量形状驱动的"泛化危机"，在Ascend NPU上尤为严重，因为其显式控制的架构和严格的物理约束使得现有GPU中心的优化方法失效，亟需针对该硬件平台的通用高性能MatMul优化方案。

Method: AdaptCore将算子优化系统性地解耦为空间分块（spatial tiling）和指令编排（instruction orchestration）两个层面。首先将动态形状映射为硬件感知的2D分块分类体系，以平衡片上容量限制与多核并行度；其次集成可组合优化库与确定性分析性能模型，通过数学评估硬件状态的变更，主动选择并缓存最优实现，从而实现O(1)开销的运行时派发。

Result: 在80,000种输入形状上实现1.85倍平均加速，在代表性端到端模型上相对于高度调优的厂商原生库（ACLNN）实现最高1.48倍加速。

Conclusion: AdaptCore通过硬件感知的自适应设计有效解决了Ascend NPU上动态形状导致的MatMul泛化危机，验证了将空间分块与指令编排解耦并用分析模型驱动主动优化选择的框架思路在特定硬件架构上的有效性。

Abstract: Matrix Multiplication (MatMul) faces a "generalization crisis" driven by highly dynamic tensor shapes. This crisis is particularly acute on Ascend NPUs, where explicitly controlled architectures and strict physical constraints render existing GPU-centric optimizations ineffective. To resolve this, we propose AdaptCore, an adaptive framework for universally high-performance MatMul on Ascend NPUs. AdaptCore systematically decouples operator optimization into spatial tiling and instruction orchestration. It first maps dynamic shapes into a hardware-aware 2D tiling taxonomy to balance on-chip capacity limits and multi-core parallelism. Furthermore, it integrates a composable optimization library with a deterministic analytical performance model. By mathematically evaluating hardware state mutations, AdaptCore proactively selects and caches optimal implementations, enabling O(1) overhead runtime dispatching. Evaluations demonstrate that AdaptCore delivers a remarkable 1.85x mean speedup across 80,000 input shapes, and achieves up to a 1.48x acceleration in representative end-to-end models over the highly-tuned native vendor library (ACLNN).

</details>


### [121] [You Only Charge Once 2.0 : A End-to-End Analog Computing-in-Memory Architecture with Reconfigurable Switched Capacitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11116)
*Zihao Xuan,Yewen Li,Jia Chen,Wei Xuan,Xiao Huo,Fengbin Tu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Charge-CIM是一种基于开关电容电荷重分布的模拟存内计算（ACiM）加速器，通过将输入转换、模拟MAC、加权移位加法和读出量化统一到同一电容阵列中，消除了独立的ADC开销和中间ADC调用，在DNN基准测试中实现ADC能耗降低91.7%，能效提升2.7倍，吞吐量提升2.0倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ACiM加速器深受"ADC墙"问题困扰——模数转换器消耗大量能量和面积，且位切片执行会反复调用转换器。现有方案（低分辨率读出或时间复用）要么损失输出保真度，要么引入串行化开销，需要一种既能降低转换成本又不牺牲性能的新方案。

Method: Charge-CIM采用开关电容电荷重分布作为统一计算与转换基底，同一电容阵列完成输入转换、模拟MAC、加权移位加法和读出量化；同时引入差分读出路径，在ADC量化过程中合并成对的部分和，实现紧凑高效的阵列集成，并搭配数据流架构支持。

Result: 在从CNN到Transformer的DNN基准测试集上，Charge-CIM相比最先进的电荷域CIM加速器，ADC能耗降低91.7%，能量效率提升2.7倍，吞吐量提升2.0倍。

Conclusion: Charge-CIM证明了以电荷重分布为统一基底可以有效打破"ADC墙"瓶颈，为ACiM加速器提供了一种高能效、高吞吐的解决方案，适用于从CNN到Transformer等多种DNN工作负载。

Abstract: Analog Computing-in-Memory (ACiM) accelerates deep neural networks by keeping weights inside memory arrays and executing dot products in the analog domain. However, modern ACiM accelerators are often limited by the "ADC wall": analog-to-digital converters consume a large fraction of energy and area, while bit-sliced execution repeatedly invokes these converters. Existing designs reduce this cost with low-resolution readout or time multiplexing, but they either lose output fidelity or introduce serialization overhead.
  Charge-CIM addresses this bottleneck by using switched-capacitor charge redistribution as a unified computing and conversion substrate. The same capacitor fabric performs input conversion, analog MAC, weighted shift-and-add, and readout quantization, reducing both standalone converter overhead and intermediate ADC invocations. A differential readout path further combines paired partial sums during ADC quantization, providing a highly compact and energy-efficient solution for array integration. With dataflow architecture support, we evaluated Charge-CIM on a suite of DNN benchmarks, from CNNs to Transformer models, and experimental results show that Charge-CIM reduces ADC energy by 91.7% under our evaluation setup and improves energy efficiency by 2.7x and throughput by 2.0x compared to the state-of-the-art charge-domain CIM accelerator.

</details>


### [122] [When and Where Faults Matter: A Study of Transient Errors in CKKS Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11147)
*Vattana Chan,Matías Mazzanti,Karthik Swaminathan,Augusto Vega,Esteban Mocskos,Radha Venkatagiri*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究了未优化CKKS同态加密方案中服务器端同态乘法运算的错误特性，发现错误在密文分量c0和c1中的时序与位置对最终FHE输出的正确性有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态加密作为一种隐私保护加密范式，允许直接在加密数据上进行计算而无需解密。理解FHE计算中错误的产生机制对于评估方案的实用性和安全性至关重要，特别是CKKS方案中服务器端同态乘法的错误行为尚未被充分研究。

Method: 针对未优化的CKKS（Cheon-Kim-Kim-Song）方案，聚焦于服务器端同态乘法运算，系统分析了错误在密文分量c0和c1中出现的时间和位置对最终FHE输出正确性的影响。

Result: 研究发现错误在密文分量c0和c1中的时序（timing）和位置（location）会显著影响最终FHE输出的正确性，表明错误的分布模式而非仅仅错误数量决定了解密结果的可靠性。

Conclusion: CKKS方案中同态乘法的错误影响高度依赖错误在密文分量中的出现时间和位置，这对FHE系统的错误管理和参数选择具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Homomorphic Encryption (HE) is a privacy-preserving encryption paradigm that enables computation directly on encrypted data without requiring decryption. In this paper, we study errors in fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) computations, with a particular focus on server-side homomorphic multiplication in the unoptimized CKKS (Cheon--Kim--Kim--Song) scheme. We show that both the timing and the location of errors in the ciphertext components \(c_0\) and \(c_1\) have a significant impact on the correctness of the final FHE output.

</details>


### [123] [On the Sensitivity to Errors in Homomorphic Computing: Single Transient Bit-flip Client-side Error Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11155)
*Matías Mazzanti,Vattana Chan,Karthik Swaminathan,Augusto Vega,Esteban Mocskos,Radha Venkatagiri*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文分析了同态加密（HE）系统对比特级故障的敏感性，聚焦CKKS方案，识别出同态乘法是最易出错的操作，并揭示了故障如何通过该操作传播和放大，从而暴露了HE部署中关键的鲁棒性漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HE依赖噪声注入保证安全，但这也引入了内在的错误敏感性。硬件和软件引发的故障可能绕过传统检测机制，导致静默数据损坏。因此需要系统性分析HE对比特级故障的敏感性，以推动更鲁棒的HE部署。

Method: 聚焦CKKS方案（用于AI和机器学习工作负载的近似算术），从比特级故障注入的角度，分析故障在HE管线中的传播与放大行为，并特别关注同态乘法运算的错误敏感性。

Result: 识别出同态乘法是实际HE管线中最具错误敏感性的操作，并刻画了故障如何通过同态乘法传播和放大，暴露了关键鲁棒性漏洞。

Conclusion: HE系统面临比特级故障的严重鲁棒性威胁，特别是同态乘法操作，亟需开发更具弹性的HE部署方案以应对潜在的静默数据损坏风险。

Abstract: Homomorphic Encryption (HE) enables computation on encrypted data without decryption and is a key primitive for privacy-preserving computation in sensitive domains such as healthcare, finance, and government. Its security relies on noise injection, which introduces intrinsic error sensitivity and raises concerns about the fault tolerance of HE systems, as hardware- and software-induced faults can evade traditional detection mechanisms and lead to silent data corruption.
  In this work, we analyze the sensitivity of HE to bit-level faults, focusing on the CKKS (Cheon--Kim--Kim--Song) scheme widely used for approximate arithmetic in AI and machine learning workloads. We identify homomorphic multiplication as the most error-sensitive operation in practical HE pipelines and characterize how faults propagate and amplify through it, exposing a critical robustness vulnerability and motivating the need for more resilient HE deployments.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-IV: Developmental Topology Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09997)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Transformer Geometry Observatory (TGO-IV)，一个基于持久同调（Persistent Homology）的拓扑框架，用于分析Transformer表示在层间前向传播过程中的演化规律，通过构建Vietoris-Rips单纯复形并追踪其持久拓扑签名，揭示原始输入表示何时以及如何演化为任务相关的特征表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer可解释性研究主要分析孤立层或整个网络的表示，缺乏对跨层中单个表示及其流形的发育演化过程的分析。如何回答"Transformer如何学习"这一重要问题需要理解原始输入表示在层间如何逐步转化为任务相关特征表示，而当前局部几何属性分析不足以揭示这一全局拓扑层面的演化过程。

Method: TGO-IV采用拓扑数据分析框架，从token级表示点云构建Vietoris-Rips单纯复形，并利用持久同调(Persistent Homology)追踪拓扑签名在Transformer各层间的演化。框架包含六种互补的拓扑观测工具：持久图(Persistence Diagrams)、条形码图(Barcode Diagrams)、Betti曲线(Betti Curves)、持久景观(Persistence Landscapes)、瓶颈距离(Bottleneck Distance)和Wasserstein距离，从而实现对其前向传播过程中表示点云全局拓扑的多维度综合分析。

Result: 该方法为识别Transformer中表示发生关键拓扑转变的层提供了理论工具，能够建立表示从原始输入到任务相关特征的演化趋势，通过拓扑签名的一致性比较（瓶颈距离与Wasserstein距离）在层间量化表示结构的变形程度，从而识别出与任务相关性建立相关的关键层。

Conclusion: TGO-IV是首个将持久同调系统地应用于跨层Transformer表示演化的可解释性框架，通过全局拓扑视角补充了现有局部几何分析方法，为深入理解Transformer内部学习机制提供了新途径，能够辅助隔离关键层或建立跨层演化趋势。

Abstract: Transformers have had a profound impact on the world of language processing and computer vision. As efforts to answer the million-dollar question of ``How does a Transformer learn?" have been increasing, existing interpretability studies primarily analyze representations at isolated layers or the network as a whole, while the developmental evolution of individual representations and its manifolds across transformer layers remains underexplored. With this work, we aim at providing a comprehensive analysis of the evolution of representations as the representation point cloud transforms across the layers; thereby attempting to isolate layers or establish a trend which comes closer to justifying how and when raw input representations evolve into task-relevant feature representations. Thus, Transformer Geometry Observatory-TGO-IV introduces a topological framework for analysing the evolution of Transformer representations through the lens of Persistent Homology. Rather than studying local geometric properties alone, TGO-IV constructs Vietoris--Rips simplicial complexes from token-level representation point clouds and investigates the evolution of their persistent topological signatures across Transformer layers. The proposed framework comprises complementary topological observatories including Persistence Diagrams, Barcode Diagrams, Betti Curves, Persistence Landscapes, Bottleneck Distance, and Wasserstein Distance, enabling a comprehensive analysis of how the global topology of representation point clouds develops throughout the forward pass.

</details>


### [125] [Uncertainty-Aware Ensemble Deep Randomized Neural Networks for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10007)
*M. Sajid,A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,P. N. Suganthan,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出直觉模糊深度随机向量功能链接网络（IF-dRVFL）和集成版本（IF-edRVFL），通过结合直觉模糊理论对训练样本赋予自适应权重，以提升模型对噪声和离群点的鲁棒性。在UCI和KEEL数据集上的实验验证了其优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度随机神经网络（如dRVFL和edRVFL）对所有训练样本一视同仁地处理，缺乏对噪声和离群点的区分能力；同时，污染特征在隐藏层中的传播会损害模型的决策性能。因此，需要一种能够自适应区分干净样本、噪声样本和离群点的鲁棒框架。

Method: 提出IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL框架，将直觉模糊理论融入深度RVFL模型。具体而言：1）在核空间中利用样本邻域信息，同时考虑每个样本的隶属度与非隶属度；2）隶属度基于样本到其类质心的距离计算，非隶属度基于局部邻域内的样本异质性量化；3）利用这两个度量对训练样本分配自适应权重，从而有效区分干净、噪声和离群数据点。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上，分别在无高斯噪声和有高斯噪声的条件下进行了广泛实验，结果表明IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL在鲁棒性和准确性上均优于现有的SOTA模糊和非模糊方法。

Conclusion: 提出的直觉模糊深度RVFL框架通过自适应样本加权有效增强了深度随机神经网络对噪声和离群点的鲁棒性，为处理真实世界含噪数据提供了新的有效方案，且源代码已公开。

Abstract: The current state-of-the-art (SOTA) deep randomized neural networks, such as deep Random Vector Functional Link (dRVFL) and ensemble deep RVFL (edRVFL), treat all training samples uniformly, which limits their robustness and effectiveness when applied to real-world datasets containing noise and outliers. Furthermore, the propagation of contaminated features across hidden layers negatively influences the decision-making capability of these models. To overcome these limitations, we propose intuitionistic fuzzy dRVFL (IF-dRVFL) and intuitionistic fuzzy edRVFL (IF-edRVFL) frameworks that enhance model robustness. The proposed models unify intuitionistic fuzzy theory to exploit sample neighborhood information in the kernel space by jointly considering membership and non-membership degrees for each sample. Membership degrees are computed based on the distance of samples from their respective class centroids, while non-membership degrees quantify sample heterogeneity within local neighborhoods. These measures are employed to assign adaptive weights to training samples, enabling effective discrimination among clean, noisy, and outlier data points. Extensive experiments conducted on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, with and without the presence of Gaussian noise, demonstrate the superiority of the proposed IF-dRVFL and IF-edRVFL models over existing SOTA fuzzy and non-fuzzy approaches. The source code is available at https://github.com/mtanveer1/IF-edRVFL.

</details>


### [126] [CurveFP: Rational-Radix Logarithmic Datatypes with Closed Products for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10010)
*Ye Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CurveFP 是一种封闭乘积码本（closed-product codebook）数值格式家族，通过交错对数曲线与紧凑块缩放分布量化幅值，使得乘积运算变为简单的符号异或和整数索引更新，从而实现 FP8 级数值行为、七比特推理和更简单的乘积路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低精度数据类型（如 FP8）大多只优化标量保真度，但其乘积所引发的算术操作并未改变，导致乘积路径复杂、运算开销高。作者希望设计一种算术协同设计的格式，使乘积运算本身代数封闭且更简单，同时保持与 FP8 相当的数值质量。

Method: CurveFP 采用交错对数曲线将量化幅值分布在紧凑块缩放之下；通过有理基数（rational radix）调节动态范围与局部分辨率之间的权衡；统一的曲线索引保证所有非零乘积在代数上封闭（即乘积仍落在同一码本内）。乘积形成为精确的符号异或和整数索引更新，并由推导出的有限相位计数决定累加调度。具体实例化包括 CurveFP eight（E4C3/E5C2，用于训练）和 CurveFP seven（E3C3，用于紧凑部署）。

Result: CurveFP seven 在四个 7B–9B 模型上以更少一个元素比特的优势击败了张量级 FP8 困惑度，并保持在原生质量的 1.32% 以内。CurveFP eight 在全部 36 组前向/反向 GEMM 对比中降低了操作数 NMSE。在三个匹配的 128.3M 参数三元组上，每个模式均完成 3B token 预训练；CurveFP eight 达到均值 BF16 推理困惑度 22.5366（FP8 为 22.5407），且在所有三个种子中格式引入的惩罚更低。36 单元下游矩阵中，CurveFP eight 训练的检查点在全部 12 组种子-格式比较中 WikiText-103 困惑度更低，PG-19 和任务差异混合。

Conclusion: CurveFP 作为一种算术协同设计，成功结合了 FP8 级别的数值行为、七比特推理能力以及显著简化的乘积路径，证明了通过重构低精度格式的乘积代数而非仅优化标量保真度，可以获得更好的效率与质量平衡。

Abstract: Low-precision datatypes reduce language-model cost, but most formats optimize scalar fidelity while leaving the arithmetic induced by their products unchanged. We introduce CurveFP, a closed-product codebook family that distributes quantized magnitudes across interleaved logarithmic curves under compact block scales. A rational radix tunes dynamic range against local resolution, while uniform curve indices make every nonzero product algebraically closed. Product formation becomes an exact sign XOR and integer-index update, and a derived finite phase count determines the accumulation schedule. We instantiate this algebra as CurveFP eight E4C3/E5C2 for training and CurveFP seven E3C3 for compact deployment. In evaluation, CurveFP seven beats tensor-wise FP8 perplexity on four 7B--9B models with one fewer element bit and stays within 1.32\% of native quality. CurveFP eight lowers operand NMSE in all 36 paired forward and backward GEMM comparisons. Across three matched 128.3M-parameter triplets, every mode completes 3B-token pretraining per seed; CurveFP eight reaches mean BF16-inference perplexity 22.5366 versus 22.5407 for FP8 and incurs a lower format-induced penalty in all three seeds. A 36-cell downstream matrix finds lower WikiText-103 perplexity for the CurveFP eight-trained checkpoints in all 12 seed-format comparisons, with mixed PG-19 and task deltas. Together, these results establish CurveFP as an arithmetic co-design that combines FP8-class numerical behavior, seven-bit inference, and a substantially simpler product path.

</details>


### [127] [Sheaf-Based Federated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10016)
*Gabriele D'Acunto,Enrico Grimaldi,Valeria Avino,Mario Edoardo Pandolfo,Leonardo Di Nino,Sergio Barbarossa,Paolo Di Lorenzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning (SFRL)，一个无需共享全局潜在空间的异构联邦表示学习框架，通过学习可学习的层流限制映射和流形约束的几何对齐正则化器，在异构联邦系统中实现全局一致性的涌现，并配套设计了Sheaf-FRL去中心化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦系统要求学习代理在数据分布、感知模态、模型架构、潜在维度和局部学习目标存在差异的情况下，仍能学习和交换有信息量的表示。现有方法大多假设存在共享的全局潜在空间，这与实际异构场景不符，需要一种不依赖共享潜在空间假设的通用框架。

Method: SFRL通过可学习的层流限制映射(learnable sheaf restriction maps)构造流形约束的几何对齐正则化器，与局部目标联合优化。通过正交变换和等距嵌入实现相邻潜在表示的对齐，并由层流拉普拉斯算子诱导的二次粘合正则化项强制对齐。惩罚项在少量共享试点样本上计算以保证可扩展性和通信效率。Sheaf-FRL算法交替进行局部模型的梯度更新和边级限制映射的闭式Procrustes更新。

Result: 在语义通信背景下的协作分类任务中，Sheaf-FRL在局部分布偏移的不同水平下，相比基线方法在局部和通信后分类准确率上均有提升，并且对潜在空间维度压缩表现出更强的鲁棒性。同时在确定性和随机设置下证明了收敛到一阶驻点。

Conclusion: SFRL成功地在异构联邦系统中通过可学习层流几何实现表示对齐，不依赖共享潜在空间假设，兼具理论收敛保证和实际性能优势，为异构联邦学习提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated systems require agents to learn and exchange informative representations despite differences in data distributions, sensing modalities, model architectures, latent dimensionalities, and local learning objectives. To address this challenge, we propose Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning (SFRL), a general framework that jointly optimizes local objectives with a manifold-constrained geometric alignment regularizer based on learnable sheaf restriction maps. Unlike most existing approaches, SFRL does not assume a shared global latent space. Instead, global consistency emerges from the alignment of neighboring latent representations through orthogonal transformations and isometric embeddings. This alignment is enforced by a quadratic gluing regularizer induced by the sheaf Laplacian, whose learnable restriction maps adapt the geometry to the observed data. The penalty is evaluated on a small set of shared pilot samples, ensuring scalability and communication efficiency. We develop a decentralized algorithm for solving SFRL, termed Sheaf-FRL, which alternates between gradient updates of the local models and closed-form Procrustes updates of the edge-wise restriction maps. We further establish convergence of Sheaf-FRL to first-order stationary points in both deterministic and stochastic settings. As an application, we consider a cooperative classification task in the context of semantic communication, under model and data heterogeneity. Our results show that Sheaf-FRL outperforms baseline approaches in terms of local and post-communication classification accuracy across different levels of local distribution shift and exhibits greater robustness to latent-space dimensionality compression.

</details>


### [128] [DOCSCHISEL: Adaptive Tool Documentation Optimization Framework for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10037)
*You Lu,Kun Zhang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究揭示了LLM智能体工具文档中信息字段的异质性及其效果对任务域、LLM骨干和智能体范式的强依赖性，并提出DocsChisel——一个自适应的工具文档优化框架，通过分析失败执行轨迹来迭代地添加、删除和精炼每个工具的信息字段，在提升任务成功率（较原始文档95.89%，较基线75.15%）的同时保持较低的时间和token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦于提升LLM智能体的工具调用能力，而将工具文档视为固定输入。尽管有少数工作尝试通过重写或压缩来优化工具文档，但关于工具文档中的信息如何在不同设置下影响智能体性能，仍然缺乏系统性的理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究工具文档信息字段对不同任务域、LLM骨干和智能体范式的影响。

Method: 首先开展大规模实证研究，分析现有工具文档中信息字段的异质性，并系统评估不同信息字段在不同任务域、LLM骨干和智能体范式下的有效性。基于实证发现，提出DocsChisel框架：通过分析目标LLM智能体的失败执行轨迹来识别与文档相关的问题，然后针对每个工具迭代地执行添加、删除和精炼信息字段三类优化操作，从而自适应地优化工具文档。

Result: 实证研究揭示了工具文档信息字段的显著异质性，且不同信息字段的有效性高度依赖任务域、LLM骨干和智能体范式。DocsChisel在多个设置下相较于原始工具文档将任务成功率提升95.89%，相较现有最优基线（EasyTool和DRAFT）平均提升75.15%，同时优化时间和token开销保持可控。

Conclusion: 不存在一种固定的工具文档能够跨多样化的智能体设置通用泛化，工具文档优化需要针对具体场景自适应进行调整。DocsChisel通过失败轨迹驱动的迭代优化框架，为LLM智能体的工具文档优化提供了一种高效且有效的自适应解决方案，该方案显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on external tools to accomplish complex real-world tasks, making tool documentation a critical grounding resource for LLM agents. Existing studies mainly focus on improving the tool-use capabilities of LLM agents, while largely treating tool documentation as a fixed input. Although several recent works attempt to optimize tool documentation through rewriting or compression, little is known about how the information contained in tool documentation affects agent performance across different settings.
  To bridge this gap, we conduct a large-scale empirical study on tool documentation for LLM agents. Our study reveals substantial heterogeneity in the information fields provided by existing tool documentation. Moreover, the effectiveness of different information fields is highly dependent on the task domain, LLM backbone, and agent paradigm, indicating that no fixed tool documentation can consistently generalize across diverse agent settings.
  Motivated by these findings, we propose DocsChisel, an adaptive tool documentation optimization framework for LLM agents. DocsChisel analyzes failed execution traces of a target LLM agent to identify documentation-related issues, and iteratively optimizes tool documentation by adding, removing, and refining information fields for each tool. We evaluate DocsChisel against two state-of-the-art baselines, i.e., EasyTool and DRAFT. Experimental results show that DocsChisel improves the task success rate of LLM agents by 95.89% over the original tool documentation and by 75.15%, on average, over existing baselines, while incurring limited optimization time and token overhead

</details>


### [129] [FlowScout: From Execution Feedback to Reliable Tool-Using Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10039)
*Shuo Hao,You Lu,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowScout是一个执行引导的框架，用于从历史任务解决记录中自动生成工具集成的智能体工作流。它通过挖掘通用工具协调骨架构建初始工作流，并利用蒙特卡洛树搜索结合执行反馈优化工作流拓扑，在四个任务领域上相比基线显著提升工具调用正确率和执行质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动生成智能体工作流的方法主要生成以LLM为中心的工作流，其中真实工具执行被LLM节点抽象和模拟，限制了生成工作流的可用性和稳定性。高质量智能体工作流的构建仍然主要依赖人工，需要大量领域专业知识。

Method: FlowScout将智能体工作流表示为包含LLM节点、工具调用节点和依赖边的有向图。首先从历史记录中挖掘通用的工具协调骨架来构建初始工作流，然后通过蒙特卡洛树搜索结合执行反馈来优化工作流拓扑结构。

Result: 在四个代表性任务领域上与PM4Py、ReAct和AFlow三个基线对比，FlowScout生成的工作流在工具调用正确性上至少提升92.69%，执行质量上至少提升17.66%，同时多次运行间的性能波动更低。

Conclusion: FlowScout通过执行引导的框架实现了工具集成智能体工作流的自动生成，克服了现有LLM中心化工作流的局限性，显著提升了生成工作流的可靠性和稳定性。

Abstract: Agentic workflows have become an important abstraction for building reliable LLM-based automation systems by organizing large language models (LLMs), tools, and control logic into explicit execution structures. However, constructing high-quality agentic workflows remains largely manual and requires substantial domain expertise. Recent studies have explored automatic agentic workflow generation from historical task-solving records, but they mainly produce LLM-centric workflows, where real tool executions are abstracted and simulated by LLM nodes, limiting the usability and stability of generated workflows. To address these limitations, we propose FlowScout, an execution-guided framework for generating tool-integrated agentic workflows from historical task-solving records. Specifically, FlowScout represents an agentic workflow as a directed graph composed of LLM nodes, tool-calling nodes, and dependency edges. It first mines a common tool coordination skeleton from historical records to construct an initial workflow, and then refines the workflow topology through Monte Carlo tree search guided by execution feedback. We evaluate FlowScout on four representative task domains and compare it with three baselines, i.e., PM4Py, ReAct and AFlow. Experimental results show that agentic workflows generated by FlowScout improve tool invocation correctness by at least 92.69% and execution quality by at least 17.66% over the baselines, while achieving lower performance variation across repeated runs.

</details>


### [130] [UserToolBench: A User-Profile-Hidden Benchmark for Personalized Decision Making in Tool-Use LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10042)
*Xuexiong Yin,Zechuan Chen,Yongsen Zheng,Yuxiang Zhang,Jingyuan Yang,Bin Wang,Yubin Wang,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UserToolBench，一个用于评估工具使用LLM个性化决策能力的基准测试，包含10个用户画像、36个工具集、1,065轮对话、170个独特工具，覆盖信息缺失、单工具和多工具三种任务类型。实验表明当前强工具使用LLM在个性化委托方面仍有困难，多工具协调、缺失约束推断和长时程行为一致性是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注画像召回、风格模仿、通用工具使用或响应级个性化，缺乏对工具使用LLM在代表用户做出决策场景下的个性化能力评估。需要一个新的基准来测试模型能否从交互历史中推断潜在的用户偏好、识别何时需要澄清，并在信息不完整的情况下生成与用户一致的工具调用轨迹。

Method: 从隐私脱敏的真实交互痕迹构建基准，结合结构化人物画像、公共API风格工具生态和长时程多轮轨迹。基准包含10个用户画像、36个工具集、1,065轮对话、170个独特工具，并设计了三种评估任务类型：信息缺失场景、单工具场景和多工具场景。

Result: 实验表明当前强工具使用LLM在个性化委托任务上仍存在明显困难。多工具协调、缺失约束推断和长时程行为一致性是主要瓶颈，模型在需要基于用户潜在偏好做出正确决策的任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 个性化评估应超越"输出听起来是否像用户"的表面标准，转向验证LLM是否能为所代表的用户做出正确的决策。现有模型在个性化委托方面仍有显著差距，需要改进的方向包括多工具协调能力、缺失约束的推断能力以及长时间跨度的行为一致性保持。

Abstract: Tool-use LLMs are increasingly asked to act on users' behalf, but existing benchmarks usually focus on profile recall, style imitation, generic tool use, or response-level personalization. We introduce UserToolBench , a benchmark for personalized decision making in tool-use LLMs. UserToolBench tests whether a model can infer latent user preferences from interaction history, recognize when clarification is needed, and produce user-aligned tool-call trajectories under incomplete information. The benchmark is built from privacy-sanitized real interaction traces and combines structured persona profiles, public API-style tool ecosystems, and long-horizon multi-turn trajectories. It includes 10 user profiles, 36 tool sets, 1,065 turns, 170 unique tools, and evaluation-focused task types covering lack-of-information, single-tool, and multi-tool settings. Experiments with strong tool-use LLMs show that current models still have difficulty with personalized delegation. Multi-tool coordination, missing-constraint inference, and long-horizon behavioral consistency remain major bottlenecks. These results suggest that personalization evaluation should move beyond asking whether outputs sound user-specific and instead ask whether LLMs make correct decisions for the users they represent.

</details>


### [131] [Finding the Signal in the Spam: Jointly Learning Rewards and Worker Reliability from Pairwise Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10045)
*Kaustubh Shivshankar Shejole,Tanish Agarwal,Arpit Agarwal,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从成对比较中联合学习物品奖励与工人能力（可靠性）的问题。作者采用Boltzmann-rational模型（扩展Bradley-Terry-Luce模型以包含工人能力），通过引入Polya-Gamma潜变量将逻辑似然转换为条件高斯形式，推导出基于EM的算法（BoRa_EM），并将其化简为矩阵感知问题以获得理论收敛保证。实验表明该方法在真实和合成数据集上优于多个基线，且对垃圾工人和对抗性工人具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对比较学习在许多领域（推荐系统、社会选择、大语言模型微调）中广泛应用，但实际场景中比较数据通常由众包工人提供，而这些工人往往因缺乏领域知识或出于利益最大化（刷单/垃圾行为）而不可靠。现有方法大多假设工人是可靠的，忽略了工人能力差异对比较质量的影响，因此需要探索能否在联合学习物品奖励的同时学习工人能力（胜任度）。

Method: 采用Boltzmann-rational成对比较模型，该模型在Bradley-Terry-Luce模型基础上引入工人胜任度参数。为在该模型下进行学习，作者引入Polya-Gamma潜变量将逻辑似然转化为条件高斯形式，从而推导出EM算法。这一转换使E步骤中的Q函数得到简化，并将整个问题归结为一个矩阵感知（matrix sensing）问题，进而为算法提供了理论收敛保证。实验在真实与合成数据集上进行，并与多个基线方法对比。

Result: 在真实世界和合成数据集上的大量实验表明，该算法在多个基线上展示了明显优势，并且对垃圾工人（spammers）和对抗性工人（adversarial workers）均表现出强鲁棒性。理论方面，通过矩阵感知的转化建立了算法的收敛保证。

Conclusion: 通过Polya-Gamma潜变量技巧和EM框架，能够有效地在成对比较学习中联合估计物品奖励与工人能力，提升在实际众包场景（存在不可靠工人）下的学习效果和鲁棒性，并提供了公开代码与数据以促进复现和后续研究。

Abstract: The problem of learning from pairwise comparisons has been widely studied across many domains such as recommendation systems, social choice, and more recently, fine-tuning large language models. In this problem, the goal is to learn item rewards based on pairwise comparisons between them. In many scenarios, these comparisons are elicited from crowdworkers using platforms such as Amazon Mechanical Turk, Scale AI, etc. However, crowdworkers are often unreliable due to limited domain knowledge or revenue-maximizing (spamming) behavior. In this work, our goal is to understand whether worker reliability (competency) can be learned jointly with item rewards. To this end, we adopt the Boltzmann-rational model for pairwise comparisons, which extends the Bradley-Terry-Luce model by incorporating worker competencies. We derive an EM-based algorithm for learning under this model by introducing Polya-Gamma latent variables to transform the logistic likelihood into a conditionally Gaussian form, enabling tractable optimization and leading to a simplified $Q$ function in the E-step of the algorithm. This technique allows us to reduce our formulation to a matrix sensing problem, using which we establish theoretical convergence guarantees for our algorithm. We conduct extensive experiments on real-world and synthetic datasets. These experiments demonstrate the advantages of using our algorithm over several baselines and confirm its strong robustness to both spammers and adversarial workers, highlighting its practical effectiveness in realistic crowdsourcing and reward learning settings. The code and data is publicly available at https://github.com/KaustubhShejole/BoRa_EM.

</details>


### [132] [Detecting Soft Skills in ML Engineering Roles CVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10046)
*Aidin Azamnouri,Nouran Ayad,Justus Bogner,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过LLM流水线从300份人工策展的简历中提取软技能（区分显式列出与隐式叙述），并针对需求端文献中的13个可证伪假设用效应量进行检验（家族误差控制）。结果支持11个假设、部分支持1个、反驳1个。关键发现：候选人以叙述而非关键词方式披露软技能（比例约3:1），在雇主最看重的领导力、协调、指导能力上叙述占比达88-96%；资历使阐述领导力的几率提高近三倍；软件工程师阐述领导力的比例仅为同龄人的一半；基于关键词的筛选系统会系统性漏检技术候选人的软技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于ML工程中软技能的知识几乎全部来自需求侧（招聘广告、调查、面试官访谈），缺少对候选人自身如何表达这些能力的供给侧研究；且已有简历挖掘工作基于关键词无法捕捉叙述性技能，且仅做描述性频率排名，未检验组间差异是否超出抽样变异。作者旨在同时填补这两个空缺。

Method: 构建一个包含300份简历、平衡覆盖三种角色（ML工程师、数据科学家、软件工程师）的语料库；设计LLM流水线提取显式列出与隐式叙述的软技能，并针对人类标注的真值验证；将需求侧文献的主张转化为13个可证伪假设（角色签名、资历递进、披露风格）；用效应量在家族误差控制下进行假设检验。

Result: 13个假设中11个得到支持、1个部分支持、1个被反驳。候选人以叙述而非关键词方式披露软技能，比例约为3:1，且在雇主最看重的领导力、协调、指导能力上叙述占比最高（88-96%）。资历使阐述领导力的几率提升近三倍。软件工程师阐述领导力的比率仅为其他角色的二分之一，而该能力在既往文献中被假设为普遍存在。技术候选人确实会表述软技能，但基于关键词的筛选会系统性地遗漏这些表述。

Conclusion: 候选人侧数据能够证实或反驳需求侧的说法而非仅作示例性说明。现有基于关键词的简历筛选手段会系统性低估技术候选人的软技能，因为在最关键的软技能上候选人倾向于以叙述方式而非关键词列表来披露。这一发现对招聘筛选工具的设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Soft skills shape collaboration among ML engineers, data scientists, and software engineers building ML-enabled systems, yet what we know about them comes almost entirely from the demand side. Job advertisements, surveys, and hiring manager interviews capture what employers ask for. How candidates themselves articulate these competencies has not been studied, and existing CV-mining work is both keyword-based, so it cannot see skills conveyed through narrative, and descriptive, reporting frequency rankings without testing whether group differences exceed sampling variation. We close both gaps. Using a balanced corpus of 300 curated CVs spanning the three roles, we extract explicitly listed and implicitly narrated soft skills with an LLM-based pipeline validated against a human-annotated ground truth, a distinction that existing extractors were not designed to make. We then convert the demand-side literature's claims into 13 falsifiable hypotheses about role signatures, seniority progression, and disclosure style, and test them with effect sizes under family-wise error control, so that candidate-side data can corroborate or contradict the demand-side account rather than merely illustrate it. Eleven hypotheses are supported, one partially, and one refuted. Candidates disclose soft skills through narrative rather than keyword lists by roughly three to one, and most so for the competencies employers value most: leadership, coordination, and mentoring (88-96% narrative). Seniority nearly triples the odds of articulating leadership. That competency, assumed universal in prior work, is articulated by software engineers at half the rate of their peers. Technical candidates do articulate soft skills, but a keyword-based screening systematically misses them.

</details>


### [133] [Physics-Informed Machine Learning in Prognostics and Health Management: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10047)
*Christopher Braun,Julian Raible,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述212项研究，梳理了物理信息机器学习（PIML）在故障预测与健康管理（PHM）领域的应用。作者提出四类分类框架（观测偏置、归纳偏置、学习偏置和混合方法），并按PHM任务进行二次分类。综述发现PIML方法在各类别中均显著优于传统基线模型，但文献集中于锂电池和轴承，且多为特定问题定制方案。尽管PIML提供了切实的预测性能提升，但关于改善泛化性、因果推断和可解释性等固有局限的声称缺乏充分证据支持。未来研究应聚焦于可迁移的设计模式、集成策略基准测试以及适用于在线部署的轻量级不确定性感知模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的机器学习模型在PHM领域存在鲁棒性差、泛化能力弱、无法推断因果关系以及缺乏可解释性等固有局限。物理信息机器学习通过将先验物理知识融入ML流程，有望缓解这些局限。作者旨在系统性地梳理PIML在PHM中的应用现状，评估其实际收益与局限性，并识别未来研究方向。

Method: 采用系统文献综述方法，筛选并分析212篇相关研究。提出一个四类分类方案：观测偏置（observational bias）、归纳偏置（inductive bias）、学习偏置（learning bias）和混合方法（hybrid approaches），并进一步按PHM任务（如诊断、预测、健康评估等）对研究进行分类分析。

Result: 在所有四类PIML方法中，研究一致表明其预测性能优于传统基线模型，覆盖广泛的资产类型。然而，文献分布高度不均，主要集中在锂离子电池和轴承两大资产领域，且多倾向于针对特定问题的解决方案，缺乏通用性和可迁移性。此外，关于PIML能够改善泛化性、因果推断和可解释性等朴素ML模型固有缺陷的说法，现有证据尚不充分。

Conclusion: PIML在PHM领域已展现出切实的性能收益，但其理论优势尚未被充分验证。未来研究应优先关注：可迁移的设计模式开发、对不同物理知识集成策略的系统性基准比较，以及开发轻量级且稳健的不确定性感知模型，以支持真实场景中的在线部署。

Abstract: In modern industry, keeping complex systems reliable, safe, and efficient hinges on Prognostics and Health Management (PHM). Machine Learning (ML) has largely driven advancements in diagnostics and prognostics, yet purely data-driven models face inherent limitations, such as poor generalization, an inability to infer causal relationships, and a lack of interpretability. Physics-Informed Machine Learning (PIML) helps mitigate these limitations by incorporating prior physical knowledge directly into the ML pipeline, thereby fostering growing interest in its application to PHM. This work investigates how PIML is being leveraged in the context of PHM through a systematic literature review of 212 studies. The review introduces a four-class classification scheme, consisting of observational bias, inductive bias, learning bias, and hybrid approaches, and further categorizes studies by PHM task. Across all four classes, the reviewed studies consistently demonstrate improved predictive performance over conventional baselines across a broad range of assets, although the literature is heavily skewed toward lithium-ion batteries and bearings, and dominated by problem-specific solutions. Overall, the review indicates that physics-informed approaches already provide tangible benefits, whereas claims of improvements concerning some of the aforementioned limitations lack sufficient supporting evidence. Future research should prioritize transferable design patterns, benchmarks comparing integration strategies, and uncertainty-aware models that are lightweight and robust enough for online deployment in real-world settings.

</details>


### [134] [Observational Policy Ranking for SMB Financial Guidance from Multi-Action Accounting Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10050)
*Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam,Vikas Raturi,Kamalika Das,Xiang Gao,Kratika Gupta,Ruocheng Guo,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Ananya Pramod,Sricharan Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出协变量调整残差策略学习（CAR-PL），用于从多动作会计日志中为中小企业提供客观的财务指导排序，在85,078条公司-月度观测上比较了多种策略学习方法，验证了基于观测证据的个性化财务策略排序的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业需要及时的财务指导，但历史会计日志记录的是自我选择且常常共现的业务变更，而非随机化的建议，这使得直接基于历史数据学习最优财务策略面临观测性偏差和动作共现的挑战。

Method: 提出Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning (CAR-PL)，一种逐动作的R-learner，直接作用于多热编码日志，并通过观测支持度进行正则化；与uplift T-Learner、保守的上下文价值模型、零样本LLM及非个性化基准在独立公司划分的测试集上进行比较，采用共享的模型辅助评分规则。

Result: CAR-PL在毛利润点估计上最高（0.084），T-Learner在收入上最高（0.085），上下文价值模型在速动比率上最高（0.062）。在匹配的公司聚类比较中，CAR-PL和T-Learner在两个增长KPI上无显著统计差异，但CAR-PL选择34个类别中的33个且选择分布更均匀；仅使用结果模型评分时各KPI领先者不变。

Conclusion: 研究支持从多动作会计日志中对中小企业财务指导进行目标特定的客观排序，CAR-PL在不同KPI上表现出优于或并列于其他方法的表现，且选择的动作覆盖面更广、分布更均匀。

Abstract: Small and medium-sized businesses need timely financial guidance, yet historical accounting logs record self-selected and often co-occurring business changes rather than randomized recommendations. We formulate this setting as observational policy ranking: from pre-decision financial information, a policy selects one of 34 ledger-derived business-change categories for a target financial KPI. Using 85,078 company-month observations from 7,505 firms, we introduce Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning (CAR-PL), an action-wise R-learner that operates directly on multi-hot logs and regularizes selection by observational support. We compare CAR-PL with an uplift T-Learner, a conservative contextual value model, a zero-shot LLM, and non-personalized references on company-disjoint held-out firms under a shared model-assisted scoring rule. CAR-PL has the highest Gross Profit point estimate (0.084), the T-Learner has the highest Revenue point estimate (0.085), and the contextual value model has the highest Quick Ratio point estimate (0.062). CAR-PL and the T-Learner are not statistically separated on either growth KPI in matched company-clustered comparisons, while CAR-PL selects 33-34 categories and produces less concentrated selections across the catalog. Outcome-model-only scoring retains the same KPI-level point-estimate leader or top pair, and category rankings remain similar when the all-zero treatment reference is replaced by the most common training co-action pattern. These findings support objective-specific ranking of SMB financial guidance from multi-action accounting logs.

</details>


### [135] [ChronoSSM: Training for Temporally Aware Representations in Autoregressive State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10120)
*Adrien Schoen,Nachiketa Ratnakar Patil,Arjun Bhagoji,Francesco Bronzino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoSSM，一种联合建模事件与时间戳的自回归状态空间模型(SSM)，通过共享骨干网络和组合的token与时间生成目标进行训练。在四个领域（跨越密集和部分时间戳监督）的实验中，联合训练一致地使冻结表征中的间隔信息更可恢复，且不会系统性地降低内容生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代序列模型（从Transformer到SSM）通常只训练预测"发生了什么"，而将"何时发生"视为次要问题。在事件带有显式时间信息的数据挖掘场景中，这种分离限制了时间推理、异常检测和事件时间线的忠实重建。现有的两阶段策略先训练事件预测模型再用冻结表征训练时间模型，隐含假设事件预测优化出的表征已包含足够的时间结构，但这一假设未必成立。

Method: ChronoSSM是一个自回归状态空间模型，使用共享骨干网络联合建模事件和事件间时间戳（inter-arrival）。训练目标由token生成目标和时间生成目标组合构成，使时间监督直接更新共享骨干网络。论文对比了联合训练（时间监督更新骨干）与两阶段训练（使用冻结事件表征学习时间）两种模式。

Result: 在涵盖密集和部分时间戳监督的四个领域中，联合训练一致地使间隔信息在冻结表征中更可恢复，且没有对自回归内容生成质量造成系统性退化。结果表明时间监督可以产生更具时间信息量的表征，而不会实质性损害自回归事件建模。

Conclusion: 联合学习事件与时间戳是可行且有利的：时间监督能够提升表征的时间信息量，同时不牺牲内容生成性能，克服了两阶段方法的隐含假设局限。

Abstract: Modern sequence models, from Transformers to State Space Models, have enabled powerful generative modeling across diverse domains, yet they are typically trained to predict what happens while treating when it happens as a secondary concern. In data-mining settings where events are associated with explicit timing information, this separation can limit temporal reasoning, anomaly detection, and faithful reconstruction of event chronology. A common strategy is to treat timing as an auxiliary signal, training a separate timing model using representations learned solely for event prediction. However, this two-stage approach implicitly assumes that representations optimized for event prediction already contain sufficient temporal structure.
  We introduce ChronoSSM, an autoregressive State Space Model (SSM) that jointly models events and timestamps with a shared backbone trained using combined token and temporal generation objectives. We compare the joint regime, where temporal supervision updates the backbone, with the two-stage regime, where timing is learned only using the frozen event representations. Across four domains spanning dense and partial timestamp supervision, joint training consistently makes inter-arrival information more recoverable from frozen representations without any systematic degradation in content-generation quality overall. Our results show that temporal supervision can produce more temporally informative representations without materially degrading autoregressive event modeling.

</details>


### [136] [Procedural Fairness Failures in RLHF from Preference Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10126)
*M P V S Gopinadh,Karthik Kamuju,Kummari Avinash,John Joshua,Srinivasa Raju Rudraraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出RLHF因偏好聚合导致程序公平性失败（多数群体偏好主导、少数群体偏好被系统性低估），提出偏好感知RLHF（PA-RLHF）在奖励学习阶段按偏好模式分离优化，显著提升对齐准确率并缩小群体间对齐差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLHF假设偏好同质，将异质偏好聚合为单一奖励模型，导致程序公平性失败——多数群体的偏好主导奖励学习，少数群体的偏好被系统性低估。这种结构性缺陷即使在受控、无噪声的环境中也会出现，并可能在多步决策的大模型与智能体系统中被放大。

Method: 提出Preference-Aware RLHF（PA-RLHF），在奖励学习阶段将优化过程按偏好模式（preference modes）进行分离，而非将所有偏好信号聚合为单一奖励模型，从而保留不同的偏好信号。在受控环境中进行实验验证。

Result: PA-RLHF将整体对齐准确率从46.9%提升至67.9%，并将最佳与最差对齐群体之间的公平性差距从15.9个百分点缩小至9.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 程序公平性失败可能源于奖励学习的结构性设计选择，而非数据噪声或恶意偏见。这为LLM和智能体系统在顺序决策中偏置奖励模型可能加剧不公平性的问题提供了直接启示。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) aggregates heterogeneous preferences into a single reward model, assuming preference homogeneity. When preferences are heterogeneous, this aggregation induces a procedural fairness failure where majority preference groups dominate reward learning while minority preferences are systematically under-represented. This work defines procedural fairness in alignment as preserving distinct preference signals during reward modeling and shows that standard RLHF violates this via preference averaging. Preference-Aware RLHF (PA-RLHF) is introduced, separating optimization across preference modes at the reward learning stage. In a controlled setting, PA-RLHF improves overall alignment accuracy from 46.9% to 67.9% and reduces the fairness gap between best and worst aligned groups from 15.9 to 9.6 percentage points. These results show that procedural fairness failures in alignment can arise from structural design choices in reward learning, even in controlled, noise-free settings, with direct implications for large language models and agentic systems, where biased reward models can compound inequities across sequential decisions.

</details>


### [137] [SeFoRA: Sketch-Aggregated Federated Low-Rank Adaptation with Heterogeneous Client Ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10144)
*Yue Xia,Tayyebeh Jahani-Nezhad,Mayank Bakshi,Rawad Bitar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SeFoRA，一种基于草图聚合（sketch-aggregated）的联邦LoRA算法，通过让每个客户端传输其本地更新的线性草图来实现服务器端的直接聚合，缓解双线性不匹配问题，并在秩同质场景（SeFoRA-Ho）下证明以O(1/T)速率收敛到一阶驻点邻域，在RoBERTa-Large的GLUE基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦参数高效微调与LoRA结合时面临两个独特挑战：(1) 客户端可能使用不同的LoRA秩，导致因子矩阵维度不兼容，无法直接聚合；(2) 因子的逐项平均存在双线性不匹配（bilinear mismatch）问题。现有方法无法同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出SeFoRA算法，每个客户端将本地LoRA更新编码为线性草图（linear sketch）传输给联邦服务器，服务器在小规模子空间中直接聚合。具体包括：1）通过草图聚合缓解双线性不匹配；2）允许在全模型的低维子空间中进行聚合，降低通信开销；3）引入秩同质版本的SeFoRA-Ho，支持适配器的直接聚合。

Result: 证明SeFoRA-Ho在秩同质设置下以O(1/T)速率收敛到一阶驻点（first-order stationary point）的邻域。在RoBERTa-Large上对GLUE数据集进行微调实验，结果表明所提算法优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SeFoRA通过草图聚合策略有效解决了联邦LoRA中秩不一致和双线性不匹配两大难题，既保证了理论收敛性，又在实际微调任务中展现出超越现有方法的性能，为联邦参数高效微调提供了新思路。

Abstract: We consider federated parameter efficient fine-tuning of large neural networks with low-rank adaptation (LoRA,~Hu et al.\ 2022). Combining LoRA with federated PEFT introduces challenges absent from either setting alone: clients may use different LoRA ranks, making their factor matrices dimension-incompatible, and factor-wise averaging suffers from a bilinear mismatch. We propose SeFoRA, a sketch-aggregated federated LoRA algorithm in which each client transmits a linear sketch of its local updates, enabling direct aggregation at the federator. As a result, SeFoRA alleviates the bilinear mismatch, and allows for aggregation in a small subspace of the full model. We introduce a rank-homogeneous version called SeFoRA-Ho which allows for direct adapter aggregation in this setting. We prove convergence to a neighborhood of the first-order stationary point at rate $\cO(1/T)$ for the rank-homogeneous setting. Numerical experiments on fine-tuning RoBERTa-Large on GLUE datasets show how our algorithms outperform the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [138] [The Evaluation Protocol Determines the Result: An Independent Reproduction of LeWorldModel on TwoRoom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10145)
*Joyjeet Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对LeWorldModel潜世界模型进行了独立复现。通过约25美元租用算力及一台笔记本CPU评估，达到94.0%的目标达成率（超过作者发布checkpoint的84.0%），并复现了位置探针Pearson r=0.9988的成绩。但发现结果高度依赖于四个未在配置文件中记录的实现约定；按公开发布配置复现会导致预测器无法收敛。此外揭示了评价协议本身存在争议（不同的目标偏移和步数预算导致14%至84%的巨大差异），以及两个普适性发现：单步预测误差不能预测长时程规划成功，批归一化层可将验证损失虚增300倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在检验LeWorldModel潜世界模型的可复现性及其报告结果的可信度。作者通过独立重实现，考察公开配置是否足以支撑论文宣称的性能，同时揭示评价协议和实现细节中的模糊性对结果的决定性影响。

Method: 使用约25美元租用算力进行独立重实现，在同一协议下与作者发布的checkpoint对比；通过变更目标构建方式、偏移设置和步数预算等系统性地测试评价协议的敏感性；分析了三个跨checkpoint以考察预测误差与规划成功的关系，并检查批归一化层对验证损失的影响。

Result: 独立复现达到94.0%目标达成率（优于作者checkpoint的84.0%），并直接复现了表示学习结果。但复现需要四个未公开的配置约定；仅按发布配置则预测器无法收敛。评价协议高度不稳定：目标偏移/步数预算不同导致14%-84%的差异，改变目标构造方式使同一checkpoint从84%跌至8%。预测误差与短时程成功单调相关但与长时程成功无关；批归一化导致验证损失虚增达300倍。

Conclusion: LeWorldModel的核心结果并非稳健可复现——其性能高度依赖于未公开的实现细节和易受干扰的评价协议。作者建议研究者不应以单步预测误差作为长时程规划能力的代理指标，并警示批归一化在损失归报中可能造成严重误导。论文本质上是一篇对既有报告结果的可复现性审查与揭示性复现。

Abstract: LeWorldModel trains a latent world model with a prediction loss and a single anti-collapse regulariser, and reports approximately 87% of goals reached on TwoRoom, its simplest diagnostic environment. We reproduce that result by independent reimplementation on roughly $25 of rented compute, with all evaluation on one laptop CPU.
  We reach 94.0% at the repository's evaluation goal offset, against 84.0% for the authors' own released checkpoint measured under our protocol on identical episodes, and we reproduce the reported representation result directly (position probe Pearson r = 0.9988 against a reported 0.996). Reaching that point required four conventions that determine the outcome and appear in no released configuration file: dense action gathering across a frameskip block, a programmatically-set action-encoder width, ImageNet pixel normalisation, and action z-scoring. A reproducer following the released configurations alone obtains a model whose predictor cannot converge.
  The evaluation protocol is itself contested by the released material. The paper's appendix and the repository's configuration specify different goal offsets and step budgets; on the authors' own weights these yield 14.0% and 84.0%, and only the configuration's values reproduce the reported figure. On fifty identical episodes, changing nothing but how the goal is constructed moves that checkpoint from 84.0% to 8.0%.
  Two findings generalise. One-step prediction accuracy does not predict long-horizon planning success: across three checkpoints spanning a sevenfold range in prediction error, including the authors' own, it orders short-horizon success monotonically and fails to order long-horizon success at all. And a batch normalisation layer inflated our reported validation loss by up to a factor of 300, concealing a training loss that was flat throughout.

</details>


### [139] [REATS: LLM Reasoning-based Ensemble Learning for Adaptive Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10149)
*Xu Zhang,Chang Xu,Hui Sun,Nan Ma,Zijian Zhang,Peng Wang,Wei Wang,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REATS框架，利用大语言模型的推理能力作为智能集成路由器，通过链式推理将文本时间序列模式描述与数值特征结合，生成可解释的样本自适应集成权重，在八个基准上优于现有集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实时间序列具有多样性，没有任何单一预测模型能在所有样本上持续占优。现有集成学习方法依赖固定规则或仅基于数值输入的黑盒模型，未能利用大语言模型的推理能力进行可解释的权重决策。

Method: REATS包含三个关键设计：(i) 结构化输入管道，将原始时间序列转换为混合文本-数值表示，固定token成本，支持无API依赖的规则链式推理构建，并引入相似样本先验检索；(ii) 多样多行权重监督方案配合token高效的百分比表格格式，降低数值复杂度并缓解LLM幻觉；(iii) 两阶段微调框架（SFT+GRPO），通过倒数奖励映射将连续无界MSE差距转化为有界信号，放大近最优敏感性，解决朴素奖励设计中均匀敏感性和离群值主导优势压缩问题。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的实验表明，REATS优于竞争性的集成基线方法，同时提供自然语言解释，展现出强的迁移学习能力和对未见候选模型的域外泛化能力。

Conclusion: REATS验证了LLM推理能力可用于可解释的样本自适应集成权重生成，通过精心设计输入表示、监督格式和微调策略，在性能、可解释性和泛化性上均取得优势。

Abstract: Due to the diversity of real-world time series, no single forecasting model consistently dominates across all samples. Ensemble learning addresses this by combining complementary model strengths, yet existing methods rely on fixed rules or black-box models based solely on numerical inputs, failing to leverage LLM reasoning for interpretable weighting decisions. We propose REATS, which leverages LLM reasoning capabilities as an intelligent ensemble router that jointly processes textual temporal pattern descriptions and numerical features to produce interpretable, sample-adaptive ensemble weights through chain-of-thought reasoning. To enable effective LLM-based ensembling, we study its key design choices and propose: (i) a structured input pipeline that transforms raw time series into hybrid textual--numerical representations with fixed token cost, enabling rule-based chain-of-thought construction without API dependency, augmented with retrieved similar-sample priors; (ii) a diverse multi-row weight supervision scheme coupled with a token-efficient percentage-table format that reduces numerical complexity and mitigates LLM hallucinations; and (iii) a two-stage fine-tuning framework combining SFT with GRPO, where a reciprocal reward mapping transforms the continuous unbounded MSE gap into bounded signals with amplified near-oracle sensitivity, addressing the uniform sensitivity and outlier-dominated advantage compression inherent in naive reward designs for regression-based GRPO. Experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate that REATS outperforms competitive ensemble baselines while providing natural language explanations and demonstrating strong transfer learning and out-of-domain generalization to unseen candidate models.

</details>


### [140] [Intrinsic Structure: Spectral Identifiability for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10172)
*Ashim Dhor,Pin-Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为机械可解释性中的字典学习（稀疏自编码器）提供了可辨识性（identifiability）理论基础。作者将前向传播视为以深度为时间的受控动力系统，并通过Koopman算子将其提升为有限线性实现，证明其谱（spectrum）是模型坐标无关的属性，可在大样本下以M^{-1/2}速率（至置换）被恢复。在GPT-2 small、Gemma-2-2B和Qwen3-8B-Base上验证了谱的收敛性与理论预测的指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性通过识别模型内部的电路来解释模型，但无法判断一个电路是模型本身的属性还是发现它的方法的人为产物。稀疏自编码器就体现了这一问题：不同的随机种子和宽度从相同激活中恢复出显著不同的特征，而现有理论无法说明这种变异性是偶然的还是结构性的。作者希望将字典学习置于可辨识性（identifiability）的基础上。

Method: 将前向传播视为以深度为时间的受控动力系统，利用Koopman算子将其提升为有限线性实现。该实现的谱是模型的坐标无关属性，证明其可从M个校准样本以M^{-1/2}速率（至置换）恢复，并给出匹配的极小极大下界、面向重尾激活的中位数（median-of-means）变体，以及一个分离定理：当实现非正规（non-normal）时，承载激活方差的向量方向与承载跨深度信息的方向不能重合。

Result: 在GPT-2 small、Gemma-2-2B和Qwen3-8B-Base上，谱处处收敛，并在Qwen3-8B-Base上达到理论预测的指数（0.506±0.031）；不足部分坍缩为关于各单元样本阈值的一条曲线。Koopman模态优于随机方向，但在间接宾语识别任务上不如主成分，差距随深度距离以4.1倍衰减，与定理预测一致。

Conclusion: Koopman谱是模型内在的、可辨识的且带有明确误差界（error bar）的"指纹"，但它不是一种可读（legible）的分解。可辨识的对象与可读的对象不是同一个对象——这一核心发现强调了可解释性中"可辨识性"与"可理解性"之间的根本张力。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability explains models by identifying circuits inside them, but has no way to tell whether a circuit is a property of the model or an artifact of the method that found it. Sparse autoencoders illustrate the problem: different seeds and widths recover materially different features from the same activations, and no theory says whether that variability is incidental or structural. We put dictionary learning for interpretability on an identifiability footing. Treating the forward pass as a controlled dynamical system with depth as time and lifting it with the Koopman operator yields a finite linear realisation whose \emph{spectrum} is a coordinate-free property of the model. We prove the spectrum is recoverable from $M$ calibration samples at rate $M^{-1/2}$ up to permutation - to our knowledge the first identifiability theorem for a mechanistic-interpretability primitive, with a matching minimax lower bound, a median-of-means variant for heavy-tailed activations, and a dissociation theorem: whenever the realisation is non-normal, the directions carrying activation variance and the directions carrying information across depth cannot coincide. The identifiable object and the legible object are not the same object. On GPT-2 small, Gemma-2-2B and Qwen3-8B-Base the spectrum converges everywhere and attains the predicted exponent on Qwen3-8B-Base ($0.506 \pm 0.031$); shortfalls collapse onto one curve against each cell's sample threshold. Koopman modes beat random directions but lose to principal components on indirect-object identification, with the gap decaying $4.1\times$ in depth-distance, as the theorem predicts. The Koopman spectrum is an identifiable, model-intrinsic fingerprint with a stated error bar, not a legible decomposition.

</details>


### [141] [From Prediction to Incrementality: Causal Optimization for Large-Scale Targeting and Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10182)
*Changshuai Wei,John Bencina,Phuc Nguyen,Andre Assuncao Silva T Ribeiro,Benjamin Zelditch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以决策为中心的因果优化框架，将因果神经网络（Transformer骨干）、贝叶斯神经bandit层和基于对偶的大规模线性规划层统一在同一目标下，用于在大规模定向和推荐系统中优化增量影响（而非预测分数）。在LinkedIn Feed营销流量的在线A/B测试中，该端到端处理策略实现了统计显著的主长期价值指标+7.20%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的大规模定向和推荐系统基于预测分数配合启发式或局部分配策略，在业务目标是增量影响（如营销活动、激励、通知）时，会系统性地将资源错误分配给那些无论如何都会行动的用户，造成资源浪费。

Method: 提出一个决策为中心框架，在全局约束下优化因果效应，统一三个组件：1）带Transformer骨干的因果神经网络用于个体治疗效果估计；2）贝叶斯神经bandit层用于不确定性感知的探索；3）基于对偶的大规模线性规划层用于约束分配。框架还通过Transformer编码器和结果嵌入支持序列上下文及多结果、属性条件评分。

Result: 通过公开bandit数据集的离线模拟、针对性架构消融实验以及LinkedIn Feed营销流量的在线A/B测试进行评估。端到端处理策略实现了统计显著的+7.20%主长期价值指标提升。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在生产规模下实现受业务约束的因果优化的可行性，并提炼了关于因果训练数据构建、成本与交付控制的生产经验，这些对成功部署至关重要。

Abstract: Large-scale targeting and recommendation systems are typically built around predictive scores fed into heuristic or local allocation. When the business goal is incremental impact, as in marketing campaigns, incentives, and notifications, this paradigm systematically misallocates resources toward users who would have acted anyway. We present a decision-centric framework that instead optimizes causal effects under global constraints, aligning three components under a single objective: a causal neural network with a Transformer backbone for individual treatment-effect estimation, a Bayesian neural-bandit layer for uncertainty-aware exploration, and a dual-based large-scale linear-programming layer for constrained allocation. The framework also supports sequential context and multi-outcome, attribute-conditioned scoring through a Transformer encoder and outcome embeddings. We evaluate it with offline simulations on a public bandit dataset, targeted architectural ablations, and an online A/B test on LinkedIn Feed marketing traffic. We also distill production lessons on causal training-data construction and cost and delivery control, which were critical to successful deployment. The end-to-end treatment policy delivered a statistically significant $+7.20\%$ lift in the primary long-term-value metric, demonstrating the feasibility of production-scale causal optimization under business constraints.

</details>


### [142] [ELMER: Evolutionary Language Model that Explores and Refines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10196)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种进化语言模型（Evolutionary Language Model），在自然语言策略描述空间中进行可操控的变异搜索，通过编译为可执行程序来度量行为变化。该模型利用oDPO微调的Qwen3-8B实现条件语义变异、NL到DSL（GPTL）的编译及反向翻译，显著提升了行为校准和有限预算搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序进化的核心挑战在于：传统方法只能事后测量变异是否有效，却无法在变异前控制行为空间中的移动距离。句法编辑大小是行为变化的不可靠代理指标——小改动可能造成巨大行为偏移，大重写却可能保持执行轨迹不变。因此需要一个可引导（steerable）的搜索表示，能够在行为空间中精确控制变异步长。

Method: 提出一种进化语言模型，使用完全微调的Qwen3-8B模型学习三个任务条件化操作：(1) 条件语义变异（conditional semantic mutation），以变异强度（低/中/高）为条件输入，通过oDPO（Direct Preference Optimization）进行微调；(2) 自然语言到GPTL（领域特定语言）的编译，使策略可执行；(3) GPTL到自然语言的翻译，将执行结果反馈回语义表示。搜索在自然语言策略描述空间中进行，并编译为类型化程序执行以度量行为。

Result: 在252次固定预算进化搜索中，oDPO同时改善了行为校准（behavioral calibration）和有限预算搜索效率。自然语言表示达到了最高的观测保持适应度（held-out fitness）。分析表明，条件输入（变异强度）系统性地改变了语义编辑组成，且在匹配的小到中等行为位移下，语言变异比直接程序变异更好地保留了父代适应度。

Conclusion: 自然语言可以作为可操控的、基于执行反馈的搜索表示，覆盖可执行程序空间。该方法弥合了语义变异意图与实际行为变化之间的差距，为程序进化提供了更精细的行为级控制能力。

Abstract: Program evolution can measure whether a mutation helped, but it rarely controls how far the mutation moves in behavior space. Syntactic edit size is an unreliable proxy: a small code change can alter nearly every action, while a larger rewrite can preserve the same execution trace. We introduce an Evolutionary Language Model that searches over natural-language policy descriptions and compiles typed programs for execution. A fully fine-tuned Qwen3-8B model learns three task-conditioned operations: conditional semantic mutation, natural language to domain-specific language (GPTL) compilation, and GPTL to natural language translation. The model is fine-tuned with conditional input on the mutation strength (low, medium, high) using Direct Preference Optimization (oDPO). Across 252 fixed-budget evolutionary searches, oDPO improves both behavioral calibration and finite-budget search efficiency. Natural-language attains the highest observed held-out fitness. Our analysis shows that the condition input (mutation strength) systematically changes semantic edit composition and that language mutations preserve more parent fitness at matched small-to-moderate behavioral displacement. These results show that language can serve as a steerable, execution-grounded search representation over executable program space.

</details>


### [143] [Boundary-Seeking Policy Gradient for Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10204)
*Chenhua Fan,Jiahui Zhu,Yuhang Zhang,Honghao Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出边界搜索策略梯度（BSPG），一种利用约束最优策略位于可行域边界这一结构的一阶方法。其更新方向包含切向（提升奖励且保持成本不变）和法向（残差驱动的有符号分量调整策略朝向活跃边界）两个分量，且具有代数Lagrangian形式，无需学习对偶变量。理论证明约束残差以O(1/√T)收敛，收敛序列满足KKT条件；在Safety-Gymnasium导航任务中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带约束的马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）中，当约束在最优处活跃时，最优策略恰好位于约束边界上。然而标准的基于梯度的强化学习方法并未利用这一结构特征，往往收敛到可行域内部，导致次优的奖励表现。现有分析的局限在于只能保证可行性（约束满足），不能刻画约束值在收敛时的具体行为。

Method: BSPG将更新方向分解为两个分量：1）切向分量：在保持成本一阶不变的前提下提升奖励；2）法向分量：由约束残差驱动的有符号分量，将策略从可行域两侧调整至活跃边界。两个分量合成为代数Lagrangian形式，诱导出一个系数而无需学习对偶变量。在精确梯度和正则条件下，该方法具有有限地平线O(1/√T)的约束残差收敛界。

Result: 理论上：约束残差从两侧收敛到零，收敛速度O(1/√T)；切向分量是边界上的奖励上升方向；任何收敛的参数序列在活跃约束集上达到驻点，若极限也是可行集上的局部极大值则满足KKT条件。实验上：在Safety-Gymnasium导航任务中，BSPG相比基线获得更高奖励且更紧密地追踪约束边界。

Conclusion: BSPG通过显式利用最优策略位于约束边界这一特性，弥补了现有方法在收敛后约束值行为刻画方面的空白。它不仅保持可行性，还保证约束残差从两侧收敛至零，并满足KKT最优性条件，在实验中展现出优于基线的奖励和约束追踪表现。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning maximizes reward subject to safety constraints. For Constrained Markov Decision Processes, the linear-programming view over occupancy measures implies that whenever the constraint is active at optimality, the optimal policy lies exactly on the constraint boundary, yet standard gradient-based methods do not exploit this structure and often settle in the feasible interior. We introduce Boundary-Seeking Policy Gradient (BSPG), a first-order method whose update combines a tangential component that improves reward while preserving cost to first order with a signed, residual-driven normal component that regulates the policy toward the active boundary from either side; the combined direction admits an algebraic Lagrangian form with an induced coefficient and no learned dual variable. Under exact gradients and stated regularity conditions, the constraint residual converges to zero from either side with a finite-horizon $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ bound, the tangential component is a reward-ascent direction on the boundary, and any convergent parameter sequence is stationary on the active constraint set, satisfying the KKT conditions when the limit is also a local maximizer over the feasible set. This complements existing analyses, which certify feasibility but do not characterize the constraint value at convergence. On a standard Safety-Gymnasium navigation task, BSPG attains higher reward while tracking the boundary more tightly than the compared baselines.

</details>


### [144] [A matched-integrator evaluation of Hamiltonian neural networks on pendulum and Kepler dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10235)
*Lenick Kemunto Nyabuto,Yae Ulrich Gaba,Birahim Tewe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过严格受控的实验协议，系统评估了Hamiltonian Neural Networks（HNN）相比参数匹配的普通前馈神经网络在长时间预测和物理一致性上的优势，在非线性摆和三维Kepler二体问题上均验证了HNN显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HNN通过学习标量Hamiltonian来参数化保守动力学，提供了通用向量场神经网络所不具备的架构先验。然而，现有研究缺乏在严格受控条件下对这一先验价值的定量评估，因此需要设计一个公平的对比协议来衡量HNN的真实优势。

Method: 采用受控对比协议：HNN与参数匹配的前馈基线模型在同一RK4生成轨迹上训练，使用相同的中心差分导数目标与优化设置，推理时使用相同的RK4积分器，并在5个独立随机种子下重复实验。此外还进行了能量分层分析和Störmer-Verlet风格rollout诊断。

Result: 在非线性摆上，HNN将平均能量漂移降低42倍、轨迹MSE降低15.8倍（T=100，约16个摆周期），能量漂移有界且跨种子的变异性显著更低。差异在非线性区域更为明显。在三维Kepler二体问题上，HNN同样在轨迹、能量和角动量漂移方面优于基线。

Conclusion: 受控实验证实Hamiltonian参数化在长时程预测和物理一致性方面显著优于通用神经网络基线，且优势随系统非线性增强而放大，为保守动力学的神经建模提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Hamiltonian Neural Networks (HNNs) parameterize conservative dynamics through a learned scalar Hamiltonian, providing an architectural prior that is absent from generic vector-field neural networks. We evaluate this prior under a controlled protocol in which an HNN and a parameter-matched feedforward baseline are trained on the same RK4-generated trajectories, use the same central-difference derivative targets and optimization settings, and are integrated at inference with the same RK4 scheme. Results are reported over five independent training seeds.
  On the nonlinear pendulum, the HNN reduces mean energy drift by 42-fold and mean trajectory MSE by 15.8-fold at T = 100, approximately 16 pendulum periods. Its energy drift also remains bounded and exhibits substantially lower seed-to-seed variability than the standard-network baseline. An energy-stratified analysis shows that the difference becomes more pronounced as trajectories explore more nonlinear regions of phase space.
  As an additional diagnostic, we examine an explicit Störmer--Verlet-style rollout of the learned HNN. Because the learned Hamiltonian is not constrained to the separable form H(q,p) = T(p) + V(q), the standard symplecticity guarantee of velocity Verlet does not directly apply.
  We further apply the same matched-integrator protocol to the three-dimensional Kepler two-body problem. The HNN again exhibits lower trajectory, energy, and angular-momentum drift than the parameter-matched baseline. These experiments provide a controlled study of how Hamiltonian parameterization affects long-horizon prediction and physical consistency across two conservative dynamical systems.

</details>


### [145] [STCAD: Scalable Trajectory Clustering and Anomaly Detection on Terabyte-Scale AIS Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10249)
*Bertram Hage,Alexander Schiøtz,Felix Thomsen,Christian Rand,Peder Heiselberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个可扩展的无监督海事轨迹聚类框架，使用基于BERT的模型编码可变长度AIS轨迹，结合CURE层次聚类生成物理可解释的轨迹组，并通过重建损失和聚类噪声分配实现异常检测，在国家级TB级AIS数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理TB级AIS（自动识别系统）海量海事轨迹数据时，现有方法难以应对可变长度轨迹的表示学习、无法预先指定聚类数量，且缺乏有效的无监督异常检测手段，需要一套可扩展且物理可解释的解决方案。

Method: 1) 使用定制BERT模型对可变长度轨迹进行编码，训练方式为掩码词元建模（masked token modeling）；2) 采用CURE层次聚类算法对编码后的轨迹进行聚类，无需预先设定聚类数目；3) 基于重建损失和聚类噪声分配设计内在的无监督异常检测方法，以识别不规则导航模式。

Result: 在国家尺度、包含数十亿条消息、覆盖一年的AIS数据集上进行验证，获得了稳定的轨迹聚类结果，并能清晰区分名义行为与非正常船舶行为。

Conclusion: 该框架在超大规模AIS数据上实现了高效、稳定且物理可解释的轨迹聚类与异常检测，为海事交通分析和船舶行为监控提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We present a scalable framework for unsupervised clustering of maritime trajectories derived from terabyte-scale Automatic Identification System (AIS) archives. Variable-length trajectories are encoded with a custom BERT-based model trained via masked token modeling and clustered using CURE hierarchical clustering, producing physically interpretable trajectory groups without requiring a predefined number of clusters. An intrinsic unsupervised anomaly detection method based on reconstruction loss and clustering noise assignment identifies irregular navigation patterns. The framework is demonstrated on a national-scale AIS dataset comprising billions of messages spanning one year, yielding stable trajectory clusters and a clear separation between nominal and anomalous vessel behavior.

</details>


### [146] [CRHT: A Continuous Regression Hybrid Transformer for Vessel Trajectory Prediction with Online Cluster Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10256)
*Alexander Schiøtz,Bertram Hage,Christian Rand,Felix Thomsen,Peder Heiselberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为连续回归混合Transformer（CRHT）的深度学习框架，用于基于AIS数据的船舶轨迹预测。通过在线K-means聚类采样策略缓解空间数据不平衡，集成1D卷积层和注意力机制，在短期预测（1小时）中取得最优精度，同时平衡了导航真实性与机动跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有船舶轨迹预测模型存在地理偏差（geographic bias）和导航真实性问题，难以应对AIS数据中空间分布不均导致的罕见机动场景学习不足，影响预测的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 提出CRHT框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 在线K-means聚类采样策略，在训练过程中确保对罕见机动样本的多样化暴露，缓解空间数据不平衡；(2) 1D卷积层，用于提取局部运动学特征；(3) 多头注意力机制，捕获全局时间上下文信息。整体采用回归方式直接预测连续轨迹。

Result: CRHT在短期预测中表现优异，在1小时预测范围内取得最低误差。实验表明，离散模型在长时间跨度上具有较高的导航稳定性，而CRHT在精度和机动跟踪方面达到最优平衡，更适合实时海上监视场景。

Conclusion: CRHT为实时海上监视提供了一个兼具预测精度和机动跟踪能力的最优方案。与离散模型相比，它在短期预测中优势明显，并在预测精度与导航真实性之间取得了有效平衡，证明了混合架构在船舶轨迹预测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate vessel trajectory prediction is critical for maritime safety and anomaly detection, yet existing models often struggle with geographic bias and navigational realism. We propose the Continuous Regression Hybrid Transformer (CRHT), a deep learning framework designed to forecast vessel motion using Automatic Identification System (AIS) data. To mitigate spatial data imbalance, we introduce an online K-means cluster sampling strategy that ensures diverse exposure to rare maneuvers during training. Our hybrid architecture integrates 1D convolutional layers for local kinematic feature extraction with a multi-head attention mechanism for global temporal context. CRHT demonstrates superior performance in short-term forecasting, achieving the lowest errors at the 1-hour horizon. The results demonstrate that while discrete models provide high navigational stability over long horizons, CRHT offers an optimal balance of precision and maneuver tracking for real-time maritime surveillance.

</details>


### [147] [Toward Human Rights Benchmarking for LLMs: A Pilot Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10268)
*Savannah Thais,Wm. Matthew Kennedy,Abhigyan Acherjee,Matilda Wysocki,Malcolm Langford,Caitlin Kraft Buchman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍HumRightsBench，这是首个经专家验证的、基于场景的国际人权法推理评估基准。该基准改编IRAC法律推理框架为IRAP（以"提出救济"替代"法律结论"），并构建了由全球人权律师标注的真实场景数据集。实验结果显示模型准确率在0.339至0.577之间，任务间差异显著（0.025至0.774），证明该基准能有效评估大语言模型在人权重法律推理方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地介入涉及人权重法律判断的决策过程，但目前缺乏一个评估基准来检验模型能否在人权法框架下进行正确推理。该研究的核心动机是填补这一空白，推动AI评估科学在人权法这一新兴子领域的发展。

Method: 研究设计了一个稳健且可扩展的方法论来构建HumRightsBench：(1) 改编IRAC法律推理框架为IRAP，用"提出救济"（Proposing remedies）替代"法律结论"（Conclusion），以适应人权工作的独特推理模式；(2) 制作了一系列真实场景的试点样本，覆盖现实人权问题的多维度； (3) 由全球范围内的人权律师和专业人士对这些场景进行标注验证。

Result: 模型整体准确率从0.339到0.577不等，各法律推理任务的最小区间为0.025、最大为0.774，显示出不同模型和任务间存在显著性能差异，表明HumRightsBench能够有效区分各模型在人权法推理上的能力。

Conclusion: HumRightsBench是一个有效的评估工具，能够促进大语言模型人权法律推理这一新兴AI评估科学子领域的发展，为未来该方向的进一步研究和基准扩展奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly mediate legal determinations over what human rights are realized, and how. Yet, no evaluation benchmark exists to assess whether they can reason correctly about human rights law. To this end, we report our efforts to develop a robust and scalable methodology for creating HumRightsBench: the first expert-validated, scenario-based benchmark for evaluating reasoning grounded in the obligation structure of international human rights law. We adapt the IRAC framework for legal reasoning to better suit the unique reasoning patterns of human rights work (substituting P, "proposing remedies," for C, "legal conclusion," yielding IRAP) to structure our evaluation heuristics. We also produce a pilot series of authentic scenarios designed to implicate the many dimensions of real-world human rights issues and annotated by human rights lawyers and professionals across the world. Ultimately, we find that model accuracy scores range considerably across legal reasoning tasks (overall model performance ranges from 0.339 to 0.577, task min-max ranges from 0.025 to 0.774), which strongly implies that HumRightsBench is a capable instrument for advancing this emerging subfield of AI evaluations science at a critical moment in its evolution.

</details>


### [148] [Power law graph attention: exact generalization of scaled dot-product attention, empirical collapse at inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10288)
*Burc Gokden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PLDR-LLM提出了一种新的注意力机制PLGA，用学习到的、由输入生成的双线性算子（基于正张量的逐元素幂律）替代固定双线性形式的缩放点积注意力。论文提供了完整的架构规范、理论证明（包括包含SDPA、Perron-Frobenius结构、DAG正则化器的NOTEARS形式、非共振条件下的交换子判据等）、推理坍缩定理、度量不变性测量、条件性的三阶段机制，以及Lean 4机器验证的证明核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的缩放点积注意力（SDPA）使用固定的双线性形式（查询与键的点积），限制了模型表达输入间交互的灵活性。作者希望用输入生成的学习型双线性算子替代这一固定形式，从而增强注意力机制的适应性和表达能力，同时保持严格的理论可验证性和工程可复现性。

Method: （1）用正张量A_LM的逐元素幂律构造输入生成的双线性算子G_LM替换SDPA的固定双线性形式；（2）给出完整架构规范并对照固定版本的发布版本验证；（3）提出学习过程相关的理论声明（定理/条件定理/测量/猜想）的标签体系；（4）利用Perron-Frobenius理论分析正张量结构；（5）采用NOTEARS形式的DAG正则化器；（6）基于非共振条件和交换子判据分析相对位置依赖性；（7）在发布的检查点上实测条件性三阶段机制（旋转twirl、浓度、行映射收缩）；（8）使用Lean 4对选定的证明核心进行机器验证。

Result: 无条件成立的结果包括：PLGA在G_LM=I时精确包含SDPA；A_LM和A_P严格逐项为正且具备Perron-Frobenius结构；正性阻碍精确无环性；非共振条件下的交换子判据可判定哪些算子保持相对位置依赖。推理坍缩定理指出：对演绎输出的精确输入不变性会使推理坍缩为带常量算子的广义SDPA。测量方面：相对波动低至1e-6；扰动界对缓存推理仅量化不认证；组装的代理丢失了解码余量。在测试样本上，分块评分与顺序评分选择相同答案，且在TruthfulQA概率质量指标上逐项一致度在5e-5以内。

Conclusion: PLDR-LLM通过幂律图注意力提供了一种理论上严谨、工程上可复现的SDPA替代方案。该工作融合了严格证明（含Lean 4机器验证）、实验测量和三阶段机制假设，并在自组织临界性框架下将开放主张转化为可证伪的猜想。分块与顺序评分在测试集上高度一致，验证了方法的可操作性和可靠性。

Abstract: The Large Language Model from Power Law Decoder Representations (PLDR-LLM) and its attention, Power Law Graph Attention (PLGA), replace the fixed bilinear form of scaled dot-product attention (SDPA) with a learned, input-generated bilinear operator $G_{LM}$, built from a positive tensor $A_{LM}$ by elementwise power laws. The architecture is fully specified, verified against pinned reference releases; claims are labeled theorem, conditional theorem, measurement, or conjecture. Unconditionally: PLGA contains SDPA exactly at $G_{LM}=I$; $A_{LM}$ and $A_P$ are strictly entrywise positive, with Perron-Frobenius structure on $A_{LM}$; the DAG regularizer has the NOTEARS walk-counting form and positivity obstructs exact acyclicity; and, under nonresonance (satisfied by standard rotary frequencies), a commutant criterion identifies which operators preserve relative-position dependence. An inference-collapse theorem: exact input invariance of deductive outputs collapses inference to generalized SDPA with a constant operator. Measured invariance: relative fluctuations of $10^{-6}$ and below; perturbation bounds quantify but do not certify cached inference; the assembled proxy misses the decoding margin. A conditional three-stage mechanism (rotary twirl, concentration, row-map contraction) is measured on a released checkpoint. Blockwise training and scoring under the global Gram are stated with explicit target exposure; on tested samples, block and sequential scoring select identical answers and agree on the published TruthfulQA probability-mass metric within $5\times 10^{-5}$ per item. Self-organized criticality enters as a phenomenological framework with an intrinsic order parameter; open claims become falsifiable conjectures. Selected proof cores are machine-checked in Lean 4.

</details>


### [149] [MERA: Model Evolution and Routing with Skill Adaptation for Agentic Systems at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10333)
*Yuhang Yao,Zeyu Wang,Wanyi Chen,Tongyun Yang,Yuhang Han,Jie Xiao,Chengke Bao,Tianyi Zhao,Lynn Ai,Eric Yang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MERA提出了一种以单次模型调用为基本单位的自适应框架，通过多轮循环（重放失败调用获取教师演示、蒸馏重复流程到SkillBook、用监督学习和GRPO微调学生LoRA适配器），在推理时结合成本校准路由器和验证器回退机制，显著提升小模型能力而非仅做路由分配。四轮适配后Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B在HumanEval+MBPP上从28.7%提升至49.7%，部署时在60.8%的成本下保留88.3%的通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由方法利用任务内异构调用序列的不对称性，通过将简单调用分配给小模型、困难调用分配给大模型来降低推理成本，但这类方法不会改变小模型本身的能力，因此可节省的成本受限于学生模型当前已能解决的问题范围。核心动机是超越"绕过固定学生模型"的范式，直接提升小模型能力以实现更大的成本节省。

Method: MERA以单个模型调用（invocation）作为适配单元，每个循环包含三个关键步骤：(1) 重放失败的学生调用，获得执行验证（execution-verified）的教师演示；(2) 将重复出现的程序性流程蒸馏为迭代更新的SkillBook；(3) 通过监督学习和可选的GRPO微调学生LoRA适配器。部署时，改进后的学生模型由成本校准的路由器服务，并带有验证器回退（verifier-backed fallback）；候选SkillBook、适配器或路由器仅在联合重放保持任务质量时才被接受。

Result: 四轮适配使Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B在held-out HumanEval+MBPP上的通过率从28.7%提升至49.7%。在验证器回退机制下，部署策略以always-Luna成本的60.8%保留了88.3%的通过率。在TAU-2基准上，微调后的Qwen3.5-2B从14/35提升至18/35，达到了未适配4B模型的水平。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，验证器支持的多循环适配能够提升小模型的实际能力，而不仅仅是围绕固定学生模型进行路由优化。MERA证明将适配与路由结合、以调用级别为粒度的循环蒸馏-微调范式可以持续扩展学生模型的能力边界，从而在保持任务质量的同时显著降低推理成本。

Abstract: LLM agents execute heterogeneous sequences of model calls within a single task: some invocations require careful reasoning, while others are structured steps such as formatting or tool-argument construction. Prior routing methods exploit this asymmetry by assigning easy invocations to a cheaper small model and difficult ones to a large model. Such policies reduce inference cost, but they leave the small model's capability unchanged, so attainable savings remain bounded by the work the student can already solve. MERA instead improves the small model itself, using a single model invocation as the unit of adaptation. In each cycle, MERA replays failed student invocations to obtain execution-verified teacher demonstrations, distills recurring procedures into an iteratively updated SkillBook, and fine-tunes a student LoRA adapter via supervised learning and optional GRPO. Routing serves as supporting machinery for deployment: the improved student is served behind a cost-calibrated router with verifier-backed fallback, and a candidate SkillBook, adapter, or router is admitted only when joint replay preserves task quality. Empirically, four-cycle adaptation raises Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B from 28.7% to 49.7% pass on held-out HumanEval+MBPP. Under verifier-backed fallback, the deployed policy retains 88.3% pass at 60.8% of always-Luna cost. On TAU-2, a fine-tuned Qwen3.5-2B improves from 14/35 to 18/35 and matches an unadapted 4B model. These results indicate that verifier-backed multi-cycle adaptation can increase small-model capability, rather than only routing around a fixed student.

</details>


### [150] [Accelerated Learning of High Dimensional Functions with a Tensor-Featured Training Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10351)
*Karl Pierce,Yuehaw Khoo,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种加速高维函数学习优化的方法，通过将上下文特征引入DNN第一层，结合张量网络（TN）分解策略，使模型能够在5到40维空间中高效训练，并将存储成本降低至少8个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维函数的深度神经网络优化在计算上极具挑战性，需要寻找更高效的方法来加速优化过程并处理高维数据的存储和计算开销。

Method: 该方法采用两步交替优化策略：首先固定输入特征基，通过标准梯度下降优化DNN参数；随后特征层获得更新机会后再继续DNN优化。特征层包含两类函数：可在域上以矩阵无关方式快速求值的rank-1特征，以及需通过张量网络分解的复杂特征（tensor features）。后者利用离散化-分解策略将预训练DNN蒸馏为TN，并引入随机化张量分解策略来高效分解高维函数。

Result: 利用随机化张量分解策略，高维函数分解的存储成本降低了至少8个数量级；该方法能够高效训练5到40维的模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的结合张量网络分解与随机化策略的方法能够显著加速高维函数的学习优化，在更高维度上保持可行性和效率。

Abstract: In this work we present a method to accelerate the optimization of learning high dimensional functions using deep neural network (DNN). This optimization procedure introduces contextual features into the first layer of a DNN. The parameters of DNN are optimized via standard gradient descent while keeping the input-feature basis fixed. After optimization of the DNN parameters, the feature layer is provided a chance to update and change before DNN optimization resumes. The feature layer has two types of functions: those that can be evaluated quickly in a matrix-free way on the domain (i.e. rank-1 features) and more complex features that must first be decomposed using tensor network (TN) decomposition strategies (tensor features). In particular, we study the effect of adding features which distill pretrained DNN into TNs using a discretize and decompose strategy. To efficiently decompose high-dimensional functions constructed from discretized DNN, we leverage a randomized tensor decomposition strategy. Using randomization, we are able to reduce the storage cost of decomposing high dimensional functions by at least 8 orders of magnitude. Using this approach, we are able to efficiently train models between 5 and 40 dimensions.

</details>


### [151] [Efficient Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10357)
*Zelei Cheng,Amritansh Mishra,Sambit Sahu,William Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SINKFLEX-RL，一个面向双控制工具使用环境的模块化RL训练系统，整合Gymnasium兼容环境包装、VERL风格回放数据流、无需独立价值模型的组相对策略优化，以及保持模型特定sink缩放的FlexAttention路径。在Tau2Bench零售任务中验证奖励从0.25提升至0.44，且在内存优化上峰值VRAM降低19.7%（28.06GB→22.52GB），使8192-token配置在eager基线OOM的情况下以25.53GB成功运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野工具使用智能体需要在用户目标、领域策略、工具调用、模拟器状态和延迟可验证奖励之间进行复杂推理。虽然RL天然适合该场景，但多轮on-policy rollout产生长上下文，且模型特定注意力层可能需要自定义mask和学习的sink归一化，这给训练带来了内存和计算可行性挑战。

Method: SINKFLEX-RL采用模块化设计，包含四个关键组件：1）Gymnasium兼容的环境包装器；2）VERL风格的rollout数据流；3）无需独立价值模型的组相对策略优化（GRPO）；4）sink-aware的FlexAttention路径，设计用于在因果mask和滑动窗口mask下保持模型特定的sink缩放。通过整合环境接口、RL数据流和注意力内核设计，实现内存可行的长视野智能体训练。

Result: 在Tau2Bench零售基准中，验证奖励（mean@1）从训练早期的0.25提升至观察窗口后期的0.44；训练分数和轨迹奖励代理指标也呈上升趋势。在固定配置内存基准中，优化后的注意力路径在4096 token时将峰值VRAM从28.06GB降至22.52GB（降低19.7%），且成功以25.53GB运行8192-token配置，而eager基线在该配置下内存溢出。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，将环境接口、RL数据流和注意力内核设计进行系统级整合，对于实现内存可行的长视野agent训练具有重要价值。SINKFLEX-RL展示了通过注意力内核优化在保持训练有效性的同时显著降低内存需求的可能性。

Abstract: Long-horizon tool-using agents must reason over user goals, domain policies, tool calls, simulator state, and delayed verifiable rewards. Reinforcement learning (RL) is a natural fit for this setting, but multi-turn on-policy rollouts create long contexts, while model-specific attention layers may require custom masks and learned sink normalization. We present SINKFLEX-RL, a modular training system for RL in dual-control tool-use environments.
